    94. 

  94.           .superRefine((v, ctx) => {
    95. 

  95.             if (!v.valueAliases) return;
    96. 

  96.             const values = new Set(v.values);
    97. 

  97.             if (
    98. 

  98.               v.valueAliases.length !== values.size ||
    99.

    100. 

  100.                 values.size ||
    101. 

  101.               v.valueAliases.some((a) => !values.has(a.value))
    102. 

  102.             )
    103.

    104.

    105. 

  105.                 path: ['valueAliases'],
    106.

  107.                   'Value aliases must cover each declared domain value exactly once',
    108.

    111. 

  111.               v.valueAliases.length

  193. export type Assignment = Record<string, number>;

  194. export interface FactorObservation {
    195. 

  195.   id: string;
    196. 

  196.   scenario: string;

  197.   value: number;
    198. 

  198.   residual: number;
    199. 

  199.   energy: number;
    200. 

  200.   mode: 'hard' | 'preference';
    201. 

  201.   category: string;
    202. 

  202.   provenance: EnergyProgram['factors'][number]['provenance'];

  204. export interface EnergyEvaluation {
    205. 

  205.   id: string;
    206. 

  206.   assignment: Assignment;
    207. 

  207.   hardFailures: number;
    208. 

  208.   hardResidual: number;
    209. 

  209.   energy: number;
    210. 

  210.   factors: FactorObservation[];
    211. 

  211.   derived: Record<string, Assignment>;
    212. 

  212.   status: 'mathematically-admissible' | 'mathematically-rejected';
    213. 

  213. }

  214. 

  215. function assertTree(input: unknown, depth = 0, count = { n: 0 }): void {
    216. 

  216.   if (depth > 40 || ++count.n > 50000)
    217. 

  217.     throw new Error('Energy program exceeds expression depth/size limit');
    218. 

  218.   if (input && typeof input === 'object')
    219. 

  219.     for (const v of Object.values(input)) assertTree(v, depth + 1, count);
    220. 

  220. }
    221. 

  221. 

  222. export function validateExpression(e: Expression): void {
    223. 

  223.   if (typeof e === 'number' || 'ref' in e) return;
    224. 

  224.   const arity = ['log2', 'sqrt', 'ceil', 'abs'].includes(e.op)
    225. 

  225.     ? 1
    226. 

  226.     : ['sub', 'div', 'pow', 'lt', 'lte'].includes(e.op)
    227. 

  227.       ? 2
    228. 

  228.       : e.op === 'if'
    229. 

  229.         ? 3
    230. 

  230.         : null;
    231. 

  231.   if (arity !== null && e.args.length !== arity)
    232. 

  232.     throw new Error(`${e.op} requires ${arity} operands`);
    233. 

  233.   e.args.forEach(validateExpression);
    234. 

  234. }
    235. 

  235. export function compileEnergy(input: unknown) {
    236. 

  236.   assertTree(input);
    237. 

  237.   const p = EnergyProgramSchema.parse(input);
    238. 

  238.   // Identifier order is not a preference and never affects tie breaking.
    239. 

  239.   p.variables.sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
    240. 

  240.   p.derived.sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
    241. 

  241.   p.factors.sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
    242. 

  242.   p.scenarios.sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
    243. 

  243.   p.factors = p.factors.filter((f) => f.mode === 'hard' || f.weight !== 0);
    244. 

  244.   const ids = new Set<string>();
    245. 

  245.   for (const v of p.variables) {
    246. 

  246.     if (ids.has(v.id)) throw new Error(`Duplicate variable ${v.id}`);
    247. 

  247.     ids.add(v.id);
    248. 

  248.     v.values = [...new Set(v.values)].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    249. 

  249.     v.valueAliases?.sort((a, b) => a.value - b.value);
    250. 

  250.     if (v.locked !== undefined && !v.values.includes(v.locked))
    251. 

  251.       throw new Error(`Locked ${v.id} outside domain`);
    252. 

  252.   }
    253. 

  253.   const scenarioNames = Object.keys(p.scenarios[0]!.values).sort();
    254. 

  254.   for (const s of p.scenarios) {
    255. 

  255.     if (canonical(Object.keys(s.values).sort()) !== canonical(scenarioNames))
    256. 

  256.       throw new Error('Every scenario must declare the same input keys');
    257. 

  257.   }
    258. 

  258.   if (new Set(p.scenarios.map((s) => s.id)).size !== p.scenarios.length)
    259. 

  259.     throw new Error('Duplicate scenario id');
    260. 

  260.   for (const name of scenarioNames) {
    261. 

  261.     if (ids.has(name)) throw new Error(`Scenario shadows variable ${name}`);
    262. 

  262.     ids.add(name);
    263. 

  263.   }
    264. 

  264.   const pending = [...p.derived],
    265. 

  265.     derived: EnergyProgram['derived'] = [];
    266. 

  266.   while (pending.length) {
    267. 

  267.     const i = pending.findIndex((d) =>
    268. 

  268.       expressionRefs(d.expression).every((r) => ids.has(r)),
    269. 

  269.     );
    270. 

  270.     if (i < 0) throw new Error('Unknown or cyclic derived dependency');
    271. 

  271.     const d = pending.splice(i, 1)[0]!;
    272. 

  272.     if (ids.has(d.id)) throw new Error(`Duplicate derived ${d.id}`);
    273. 

  273.     validateExpression(d.expression);
    274. 

  274.     ids.add(d.id);
    275. 

  275.     derived.push(d);
    276. 

  276.   }
    277. 

  277.   const factorIds = new Set<string>();
    278. 

  278.   for (const f of p.factors) {
    279. 

  279.     if (factorIds.has(f.id)) throw new Error(`Duplicate factor ${f.id}`);
    280. 

  280.     factorIds.add(f.id);
    281. 

  281.     validateExpression(f.expression);
    282. 

  282.     for (const scenario of Object.keys(f.scenarioBands ?? {}))
    283. 

  283.       if (!p.scenarios.some((s) => s.id === scenario))
    284. 

  284.         throw new Error(`Unknown factor scenario ${f.id}.${scenario}`);
    285. 

  285.     for (const r of expressionRefs(f.expression))
    286. 

  286.       if (!ids.has(r)) throw new Error(`Unknown factor input ${r}`);
    287. 

  287.   }
    288. 

  288.   p.derived = derived;
    289. 

  289.   function freeze<T>(x: T): T {
    290. 

  290.     if (x && typeof x === 'object') {
    291. 

  291.       Object.values(x).forEach(freeze);
    292. 

  292.       Object.freeze(x);
    293. 

  293.     }
    294. 

  294.     return x;
    295. 

  295.   }
    296. 

  296.   freeze(p);
    297. 

  297.   const identity = hash({ engine: 'energy-v1', program: p });
    298. 

  298.   let factorEvaluations = 0;
    299. 

  299.   function evaluate(assignment: Assignment): EnergyEvaluation {
    300. 

  300.     if (Object.keys(assignment).length !== p.variables.length)
    301. 

  301.       throw new Error('Assignment must specify exactly the declared variables');
    302. 

  302.     for (const v of p.variables) {
    303. 

  303.       const x = assignment[v.id];
    304. 

  304.       if (
    305. 

  305.         x === undefined ||
    306. 

  306.         !v.values.includes(x) ||
    307. 

  307.         (v.locked !== undefined && x !== v.locked)
    308. 

  308.       )
    309. 

  309.         throw new Error(`Invalid assignment ${v.id}`);
    310. 

  310.     }
    311. 

  311.     const observations: FactorObservation[] = [],
    312. 

  312.       outputs: Record<string, Assignment> = {};
    313. 

  313.     let energy = 0,
    314. 

  314.       hardFailures = 0,
    315. 

  315.       hardResidual = 0;
    316. 

  316.     for (const s of p.scenarios) {
    317. 

  317.       const values = { ...assignment, ...s.values },
    318. 

  318.         out: Assignment = {};
    319. 

  319.       for (const d of derived) {
    320. 

  320.         out[d.id] = evaluateExpression(d.expression, values);
    321. 

  321.         values[d.id] = out[d.id]!;
    322. 

  322.       }
    323. 

  323.       outputs[s.id] = out;
    324. 

  324.       for (const f of p.factors) {
    325. 

  325.         factorEvaluations++;
    326. 

  326.         const value = evaluateExpression(f.expression, values);
    327. 

  327.         const distance = Math.min(
    328. 

  328.           ...(f.scenarioBands?.[s.id] ?? f.bands).map((b) =>
    329. 

  329.             Math.max(b.min - value, 0, value - b.max),
    330. 

  330.           ),
    331. 

  331.         );
    332. 

  332.         const residual = distance / f.scale;
    333. 

  333.         const contribution =
    334. 

  334.           f.mode === 'preference' && f.weight !== 0
    335. 

  335.             ? f.weight * residual * residual
    336. 

  336.             : 0;
    337. 

  337.         if (!Number.isFinite(residual) || !Number.isFinite(contribution))
    338. 

  338.           throw new Error(`Nonfinite factor ${f.id}`);
    339. 

  339.         if (f.mode === 'hard' && distance > 0) {
    340. 

  340.           hardFailures++;
    341. 

  341.           hardResidual += residual;
    342. 

  342.         }
    343. 

  343.         energy += contribution;
    344. 

  344.         if (!Number.isFinite(energy) || !Number.isFinite(hardResidual))
    345. 

  345.           throw new Error('Nonfinite aggregate energy');
    346. 

  346.         observations.push({
    347. 

  347.           id: f.id,
    348. 

  348.           scenario: s.id,
    349. 

  349.           value,
    350. 

  350.           residual,
    351. 

  351.           energy: contribution,
    352. 

  352.           mode: f.mode,
    353. 

  353.           category: f.category,
    354. 

  354.           provenance: { ...f.provenance },
    355. 

  355.         });
    356. 

  356.       }
    357. 

  357.     }
    358. 

  358.     return {
    359. 

  359.       id: hash({ identity, assignment }),
    360. 

  360.       assignment: { ...assignment },
    361. 

  361.       hardFailures,
    362. 

  362.       hardResidual,
    363. 

  363.       energy,
    364. 

  364.       factors: observations,
    365. 

  365.       derived: outputs,
    366. 

  366.       status:
    367. 

  367.         hardFailures === 0
    368. 

  368.           ? 'mathematically-admissible'
    369. 

  369.           : 'mathematically-rejected',
    370. 

  370.     };
    371. 

  371.   }
    372. 

  372.   return {
    373. 

  373.     program: p,
    374. 

  374.     identity,
    375. 

  375.     evaluate,
    376. 

  376.     get factorEvaluations() {
    377. 

  377.       return factorEvaluations;
    378. 

  378.     },
    379. 

  379.   };
    380. 

  380. }
    381. 

  381. function compare(a: EnergyEvaluation, b: EnergyEvaluation) {
    382. 

  382.   return (
    383. 

  383.     a.hardFailures - b.hardFailures ||
    384. 

  384.     a.hardResidual - b.hardResidual ||
    385. 

  385.     a.energy - b.energy ||
    386. 

  386.     canonical(a.assignment).localeCompare(canonical(b.assignment))
    387. 

  387.   );
    388. 

  388. }
    389. 

  389. 

  390. /** A cost profile is the per-scenario preference vector, not its sum. Equal
    391. 

  391.  * vectors can still have different geometry or unmodelled visual consequences.
    392. 

  392.  */
    393. 

  393. function preferenceProfile(candidate: EnergyEvaluation) {
    394. 

  394.   return candidate.factors
    395. 

  395.     .filter((factor) => factor.mode === 'preference')
    396. 

  396.     .map((factor) => factor.energy);
    397. 

  397. }
    398. 

  398. 

  399. function retainDiverse(
    400. 

  400.   admissible: EnergyEvaluation[],
    401. 

  401.   program: EnergyProgram,
    402. 

  402.   limit: number,
    403. 

  403.   maxComparisons: number,
    404. 

  404. ) {
    405. 

  405.   const groups = new Map<
    406. 

  406.     string,
    407. 

  407.     { costs: number[]; members: EnergyEvaluation[] }
    408. 

  408.   >();
    409. 

  409.   for (const candidate of admissible) {
    410. 

  410.     const costs = preferenceProfile(candidate),
    411. 

  411.       key = canonical(costs);
    412. 

  412.     const group = groups.get(key);
    413. 

  413.     if (group) group.members.push(candidate);
    414. 

  414.     else groups.set(key, { costs, members: [candidate] });
    415. 

  415.   }
    416. 

  416.   const profiles = [...groups.values()];
    417. 

  417.   let comparisons = 0,
    418. 

  418.     exhausted = false;
    419. 

  419.   const consume = () => {
    420. 

  420.     if (comparisons === maxComparisons) {
    421. 

  421.       exhausted = true;
    422. 

  422.       return false;
    423. 

  423.     }
    424. 

  424.     comparisons++;
    425. 

  425.     return true;
    426. 

  426.   };
    427. 

  427.   const dominates = (a: number[], b: number[]) => {
    428. 

  428.     let strict = false;
    429. 

  429.     for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
    430. 

  430.       if (!consume()) return false;
    431. 

  431.       if (a[i]! > b[i]!) return false;
    432. 

  432.       strict ||= a[i]! < b[i]!;
    433. 

  433.     }
    434. 

  434.     return strict;
    435. 

  435.   };
    436. 

  436.   // Compress plateaus before skyline comparisons. Each scalar comparison is
    437. 

  437.   // budgeted: 100k evaluated assignments must not imply unbounded all-pairs work.
    438. 

  438.   let frontier: typeof profiles = [];
    439. 

  439.   for (const profile of profiles) {
    440. 

  440.     if (frontier.some((other) => dominates(other.costs, profile.costs))) {
    441. 

  441.       if (exhausted) break;
    442. 

  442.       continue;
    443. 

  443.     }
    444. 

  444.     if (exhausted) break;
    445. 

  445.     frontier = frontier.filter(
    446. 

  446.       (other) => !dominates(profile.costs, other.costs),
    447. 

  447.     );
    448. 

  448.     if (exhausted) break;
    449. 

  449.     frontier.push(profile);
    450. 

  450.   }
    451. 

  451.   const paretoComplete = !exhausted;
    452. 

  452.   // A partial skyline is not a Pareto proof. On exhaustion use the familiar
    453. 

  453.   // ranked prefix, and explicitly report that no frontier was established.
    454. 

  454.   let candidates = paretoComplete
    455. 

  455.     ? frontier.slice(0, limit).map((profile) => profile.members[0]!)
    456. 

  456.     : admissible.slice(0, limit);
    457. 

  457.   const paretoIds = paretoComplete
    458. 

  458.     ? new Set(frontier.flatMap((profile) => profile.members.map((a) => a.id)))
    459. 

  459.     : new Set<string>();
    460. 

  460.   // First represent different factor-cost vectors. Only spare slots represent
    461. 

  461.   // equally ranked geometry, by farthest-first normalized numeric domain distance.
    462. 

  462.   // This is coverage of authored values; it is not perceptual distance or merit.
    463. 

  463.   if (paretoComplete && candidates.length < limit) {
    464. 

  464.     const pool = frontier.flatMap((profile) => profile.members).sort(compare);
    465. 

  465.     const selected = new Set(candidates.map((a) => a.id));
    466. 

  466.     while (candidates.length < limit && candidates.length < pool.length) {
    467. 

  467.       let best: EnergyEvaluation | undefined,
    468. 

  468.         bestDistance = -1;
    469. 

  469.       for (const candidate of pool) {
    470. 

  470.         if (selected.has(candidate.id)) continue;
    471. 

  471.         let nearest = Infinity;
    472. 

  472.         for (const other of candidates) {
    473. 

  473.           let distance = 0;
    474. 

  474.           for (const variable of program.variables) {
    475. 

  475.             if (!consume()) break;
    476. 

  476.             const span = variable.values.at(-1)! - variable.values[0]!;
    477. 

  477.             if (span > 0)
    478. 

  478.               distance +=
    479. 

  479.                 ((candidate.assignment[variable.id]! -
    480. 

  480.                   other.assignment[variable.id]!) /
    481. 

  481.                   span) **
    482. 

  482.                 2;
    483. 

  483.           }
    484. 

  484.           if (exhausted) break;
    485. 

  485.           nearest = Math.min(nearest, distance);
    486. 

  486.         }
    487. 

  487.         if (exhausted) break;
    488. 

  488.         if (nearest > bestDistance) {
    489. 

  489.           best = candidate;
    490. 

  490.           bestDistance = nearest;
    491. 

  491.         }
    492. 

  492.       }
    493. 

  493.       if (exhausted || !best) {
    494. 

  494.         candidates.push(
    495. 

  495.           ...pool
    496. 

  496.             .filter((a) => !selected.has(a.id))
    497. 

  497.             .slice(0, limit - candidates.length),
    498. 

  498.         );
    499. 

  499.         break;
    500. 

  500.       }
    501. 

  501.       candidates.push(best);
    502. 

  502.       selected.add(best.id);
    503. 

  503.     }
    504. 

  504.   }
    505. 

  505.   const first = admissible[0];
    506. 

  506.   const preferences =
    507. 

  507.     first?.factors.flatMap((factor, index) => {
    508. 

  508.       if (factor.mode !== 'preference') return [];
    509. 

  509.       const observations = admissible.map((a) => a.factors[index]!);
    510. 

  510.       const definition = program.factors.find((f) => f.id === factor.id)!;
    511. 

  511.       let minimum = Infinity,
    512. 

  512.         maximum = -Infinity,
    513. 

  513.         minimumResidual = Infinity,
    514. 

  514.         satisfiedAssignments = 0;
    515. 

  515.       for (const observation of observations) {
    516. 

  516.         minimum = Math.min(minimum, observation.value);
    517. 

  517.         maximum = Math.max(maximum, observation.value);
    518. 

  518.         minimumResidual = Math.min(minimumResidual, observation.residual);
    519. 

  519.         if (observation.residual === 0) satisfiedAssignments++;
    520. 

  520.       }
    521. 

  521.       return [
    522. 

  522.         {
    523. 

  523.           id: factor.id,
    524. 

  524.           scenario: factor.scenario,
    525. 

  525.           targetBands:
    526. 

  526.             definition.scenarioBands?.[factor.scenario] ?? definition.bands,
    527. 

  527.           observedRange: [minimum, maximum],
    528. 

  528.           minimumResidual,
    529. 

  529.           satisfiedAssignments,
    530. 

  530.         },
    531. 

  531.       ];
    532. 

  532.     }) ?? [];
    533. 

  533.   return {
    534. 

  534.     candidates,
    535. 

  535.     pareto: candidates.filter((a) => paretoIds.has(a.id)).map((a) => a.id),
    536. 

  536.     diagnostics: {
    537. 

  537.       policy: 'pareto-diverse' as const,
    538. 

  538.       scope:
    539. 

  539.         'Evaluated mathematically admissible assignments only; numeric profiles do not establish visual equivalence or quality.',
    540. 

  540.       admissibleAssignments: admissible.length,
    541. 

  541.       preferenceProfiles: profiles.length,
    542. 

  542.       paretoProfiles: paretoComplete ? frontier.length : null,
    543. 

  543.       paretoComplete,
    544. 

  544.       diversityComplete: !exhausted,
    545. 

  545.       comparisons,
    546. 

  546.       maxComparisons,
    547. 

  547.       retainedPreferenceProfiles: new Set(
    548. 

  548.         candidates.map((a) => canonical(preferenceProfile(a))),
    549. 

  549.       ).size,
    550. 

  550.       minimumEnergyAssignments: first
    551. 

  551.         ? admissible.filter((a) => a.energy === first.energy).length
    552. 

  552.         : 0,
    553. 

  553.       jointlySatisfiedAssignments: admissible.filter((a) =>
    554. 

  554.         a.factors.every((f) => f.mode === 'hard' || f.residual === 0),
    555. 

  555.       ).length,
    556. 

  556.       preferences,
    557. 

  557.       distance:
    558. 

  558.         'Squared numeric differences divided by each authored domain span; categorical indices have no perceptual interpretation.',
    559. 

  559.       fallback: exhausted
    560. 

  560.         ? paretoComplete
    561. 

  561.           ? 'ranked remaining Pareto assignments'
    562. 

  562.           : 'ranked admissible prefix; Pareto membership unknown'
    563. 

  563.         : null,
    564. 

  564.     },
    565. 

  565.   };
    566. 

  566. }
    567. 

  567. export function searchEnergy(
    568. 

  568.   input: z.input<typeof EnergySearchSchema>,
    569. 

  569.   validation?: { validateAssignment: (assignment: Assignment) => void },
    570. 

  570. ) {
    571. 

  571.   assertTree(input);
    572. 

  572.   const request = EnergySearchSchema.parse(input),
    573. 

  573.     options = EnergySearchOptionsSchema.parse(request.options ?? {}),
    574. 

  574.     compiled = compileEnergy(request.program),
    575. 

  575.     p = compiled.program;
    576. 

  576.   const start = performance.now(),
    577. 

  577.     domains = p.variables.map((v) =>
    578. 

  578.       v.locked === undefined ? v.values : [v.locked],
    579. 

  579.     );
    580. 

  580.   const cardinality = domains.reduce(
    581. 

  581.     (n, d) => Math.min(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, n * d.length),
    582. 

  582.     1,
    583. 

  583.   );
    584. 

  584.   const nodes = (e: Expression): number =>
    585. 

  585.     typeof e === 'number' || 'ref' in e
    586. 

  586.       ? 1
    587. 

  587.       : 1 + e.args.reduce<number>((n, a) => n + nodes(a), 0);
    588. 

  588.   const operationsPerState = Math.max(
    589. 

  589.     1,
    590. 

  590.     p.scenarios.length *
    591. 

  591.       (p.derived.reduce((n, d) => n + nodes(d.expression), 0) +
    592. 

  592.         p.factors.reduce((n, f) => n + nodes(f.expression), 0)),
    593. 

  593.   );
    594. 

  594.   const observationsPerState = Math.max(
    595. 

  595.     1,
    596. 

  596.     p.scenarios.length * p.factors.length,
    597. 

  597.   );
    598. 

  598.   const stateBudget = Math.min(
    599. 

  599.     options.maxEvaluations,
    600. 

  600.     Math.floor(options.maxFactorObservations / observationsPerState),
    601. 

  601.     Math.floor(options.maxExpressionOperations / operationsPerState),
    602. 

  602.   );
    603. 

  603.   const exhaustive =
    604. 

  604.     options.method === 'exhaustive' ||
    605. 

  605.     (options.method === 'auto' && cardinality <= stateBudget);
    606. 

  606.   const choiceNodes: {
    607. 

  607.     id: string;
    608. 

  608.     parent: string | null;
    609. 

  609.     choice: { variable: string; from: number; to: number } | null;
    610. 

  610.     seed: Assignment | null;
    611. 

  611.     energy: number | null;
    612. 

  612.     status: string;
    613. 

  613.   }[] = [];
    614. 

  614.   const seen = new Map<string, EnergyEvaluation>(),
    615. 

  615.     visited = new Set<string>(),
    616. 

  616.     invalid: { assignment: Assignment; reason: string }[] = [];
    617. 

  617.   let attempts = 0;
    618. 

  618.   const rounds: {
    619. 

  619.     round: number;
    620. 

  620.     evaluated: number;
    621. 

  621.     bestEnergy: number | null;
    622. 

  622.     admissible: number;
    623. 

  623.   }[] = [];
    624. 

  624.   function visit(
    625. 

  625.     a: Assignment,
    626. 

  626.     parent: EnergyEvaluation | null = null,
    627. 

  627.     choice: { variable: string; from: number; to: number } | null = null,
    628. 

  628.   ) {
    629. 

  629.     const key = canonical(a);
    630. 

  630.     if (visited.has(key)) return seen.get(key);
    631. 

  631.     if (visited.size >= stateBudget) return undefined;
    632. 

  632.     visited.add(key);
    633. 

  633.     attempts++;
    634. 

  634.     try {
    635. 

  635.       const value = compiled.evaluate(a);
    636. 

  636.       validation?.validateAssignment(a);
    637. 

  637.       seen.set(key, value);
    638. 

  638.       choiceNodes.push({
    639. 

  639.         id: value.id,
    640. 

  640.         parent: parent?.id ?? null,
    641. 

  641.         choice,
    642. 

  642.         seed: parent ? null : { ...a },
    643. 

  643.         energy: value.energy,
    644. 

  644.         status: value.status,
    645. 

  645.       });
    646. 

  646.       return value;
    647. 

  647.     } catch (error) {
    648. 

  648.       choiceNodes.push({
    649. 

  649.         id: hash({ identity: compiled.identity, assignment: a }),
    650. 

  650.         parent: parent?.id ?? null,
    651. 

  651.         choice,
    652. 

  652.         seed: parent ? null : { ...a },
    653. 

  653.         energy: null,
    654. 

  654.         status: 'undefined-mathematics',
    655. 

  655.       });
    656. 

  656.       if (invalid.length < 8)
    657. 

  657.         invalid.push({ assignment: { ...a }, reason: String(error) });
    658. 

  658.       return undefined;
    659. 

  659.     }
    660. 

  660.   }
    661. 

  661.   if (exhaustive) {
    662. 

  662.     const a: Assignment = {};
    663. 

  663.     function walk(i: number): void {
    664. 

  664.       if (visited.size >= stateBudget) return;
    665. 

  665.       if (i === p.variables.length) {
    666. 

  666.         visit(a);
    667. 

  667.         return;
    668. 

  668.       }
    669. 

  669.       for (const x of domains[i]!) {
    670. 

  670.         a[p.variables[i]!.id] = x;
    671. 

  671.         walk(i + 1);
    672. 

  672.         if (visited.size >= stateBudget) break;
    673. 

  673.       }
    674. 

  674.     }
    675. 

  675.     walk(0);
    676. 

  676.   } else {
    677. 

  677.     // Deterministic seeds span domain endpoints and a centre. Full assignments are always evaluated.
    678. 

  678.     const seeds = [0, 1, 2].map((seed) =>
    679. 

  679.       Object.fromEntries(
    680. 

  680.         p.variables.map((v, i) => [
    681. 

  681.           v.id,
    682. 

  682.           domains[i]![
    683. 

  683.             seed === 0
    684. 

  684.               ? Math.floor((domains[i]!.length - 1) / 2)
    685. 

  685.               : seed === 1
    686. 

  686.                 ? 0
    687. 

  687.                 : domains[i]!.length - 1
    688. 

  688.           ]!,
    689. 

  689.         ]),
    690. 

  690.       ),
    691. 

  691.     );
    692. 

  692.     seeds.forEach((a) => visit(a));
    693. 

  693.     let beam = [...seen.values()].sort(compare).slice(0, options.beamWidth);
    694. 

  694.     for (
    695. 

  695.       let round = 0;
    696. 

  696.       round < options.rounds && visited.size < stateBudget;
    697. 

  697.       round++
    698. 

  698.     ) {
    699. 

  699.       const oldSize = visited.size;
    700. 

  700.       const parents = beam.length
    701. 

  701.         ? beam.map((p) => ({ assignment: p.assignment, evaluation: p }))
    702. 

  702.         : seeds.map((a) => ({ assignment: a, evaluation: null }));
    703. 

  703.       expand: for (const parent of parents)
    704. 

  704.         for (let i = 0; i < p.variables.length; i++)
    705. 

  705.           for (const value of domains[i]!) {
    706. 

  706.             const variable = p.variables[i]!.id;
    707. 

  707.             visit(
    708. 

  708.               { ...parent.assignment, [variable]: value },
    709. 

  709.               parent.evaluation,
    710. 

  710.               { variable, from: parent.assignment[variable]!, to: value },
    711. 

  711.             );
    712. 

  712.             if (visited.size >= stateBudget) break expand;
    713. 

  713.           }
    714. 

  714.       beam = [...seen.values()].sort(compare).slice(0, options.beamWidth);
    715. 

  715.       const best = beam.find((v) => v.hardFailures === 0);
    716. 

  716.       rounds.push({
    717. 

  717.         round,
    718. 

  718.         evaluated: seen.size,
    719. 

  719.         bestEnergy: best?.energy ?? null,
    720. 

  720.         admissible: [...seen.values()].filter((v) => v.hardFailures === 0)
    721. 

  721.           .length,
    722. 

  722.       });
    723. 

  723.       if (visited.size === oldSize) break;
    724. 

  724.     }
    725. 

  725.   }
    726. 

  726.   const ranked = [...seen.values()].sort(compare),
    727. 

  727.     admissible = ranked.filter((x) => x.hardFailures === 0),
    728. 

  728.     diversity =
    729. 

  729.       options.retention === 'pareto-diverse'
    730. 

  730.         ? retainDiverse(
    731. 

  731.             admissible,
    732. 

  732.             p,
    733. 

  733.             options.limit,
    734. 

  734.             options.maxRetentionComparisons ?? 1000000,
    735. 

  735.           )
    736. 

  736.         : null,
    737. 

  737.     candidates = diversity?.candidates ?? admissible.slice(0, options.limit);
    738. 

  738.   // Strict dominance is evaluated on individual preference factors, not the weighted aggregate.
    739. 

  739.   const pareto =
    740. 

  740.     diversity?.pareto ??
    741. 

  741.     candidates
    742. 

  742.       .filter(
    743. 

  743.         (a) =>
    744. 

  744.           !admissible.some(
    745. 

  745.             (b) =>
    746. 

  746.               b !== a &&
    747. 

  747.               b.factors.every(
    748. 

  748.                 (f, i) => f.mode === 'hard' || f.energy <= a.factors[i]!.energy,
    749. 

  749.               ) &&
    750. 

  750.               b.factors.some(
    751. 

  751.                 (f, i) =>
    752. 

  752.                   f.mode === 'preference' && f.energy < a.factors[i]!.energy,
    753. 

  753.               ),
    754. 

  754.           ),
    755. 

  755.       )
    756. 

  756.       .map((c) => c.id);
    757. 

  757.   return {
    758. 

  758.     version: 1 as const,
    759. 

  759.     programIdentity: compiled.identity,
    760. 

  760.     program: p,
    761. 

  761.     status: candidates.length
    762. 

  762.       ? ('proposed' as const)
    763. 

  763.       : ('no-admissible-candidate' as const),
    764. 

  764.     candidates,
    765. 

  765.     pareto,
    766. 

  766.     ...(diversity ? { retention: diversity.diagnostics } : {}),
    767. 

  767.     rejected: ranked
    768. 

  768.       .filter((x) => x.hardFailures > 0)
    769. 

  769.       .slice(0, 3)
    770. 

  770.       .map((x) => ({
    771. 

  771.         id: x.id,
    772. 

  772.         assignment: x.assignment,
    773. 

  773.         failures: x.factors.filter((f) => f.mode === 'hard' && f.residual > 0),
    774. 

  774.       })),
    775. 

  775.     choiceGraph: {
    776. 

  776.       nodes: choiceNodes,
    777. 

  777.       roots: choiceNodes.filter((n) => n.parent === null).map((n) => n.id),
    778. 

  778.       semantics:
    779. 

  779.         'Every edge is an evaluated local numeric mutation; nodes are predictions/calculations, not renderer receipts.',
    780. 

  780.     },
    781. 

  781.     trace: rounds,
    782. 

  782.     invalid,
    783. 

  783.     statistics: {
    784. 

  784.       evaluated: attempts,
    785. 

  785.       factorEvaluations: compiled.factorEvaluations,
    786. 

  786.       uniqueStateBudget: options.maxEvaluations,
    787. 

  787.       effectiveStateBudget: stateBudget,
    788. 

  788.       expressionOperationUpperBound: attempts * operationsPerState,
    789. 

  789.       observationBudget: options.maxFactorObservations,
    790. 

  790.       cardinality,
    791. 

  791.       method: exhaustive ? 'exhaustive' : 'beam',
    792. 

  792.       complete: exhaustive && attempts === cardinality,
    793. 

  793.       latencyMs: performance.now() - start,
    794. 

  794.       selectorCalls: 0,
    795. 

  795.       mainAgentCalls: 0,
    796. 

  796.     },
    797. 

  797.     scope:
    798. 

  798.       'Brief-conditioned dimensionless preference energy, never an aesthetic or physical-unit score. Mathematical admissibility is not renderer verification.',
    799. 

  799.   };
    800. 

  800. }

  57.         dependsOn: [`layout:${b.id}`],
    58. 

  58.       },
    59. 

  59.       {

  68.         ],
    69. 

  69.         dependsOn: [],
    70. 

  70.       },
    71.

    72.

    99. 

  99. export function overlaps(a: string, b: string): boolean {
    100. 

  100.   return a === b || a.startsWith(`${b}/`) || b.startsWith(`${a}/`);
    101. 

  101. }
    102. 

  102. export function invalidated(state: DesignState, paths: string[]): string[] {
    103. 

  103.   const nodes = graph(state);
    104. 

  104.   const affected = new Set<string>();
    105. 

  105.   for (const node of nodes)
    106. 

  106.     if (node.reads.some((r) => paths.some((p) => overlaps(r, p))))
    107. 

  107.       affected.add(node.id);
    108. 

  108.   let changed = true;
    109. 

  109.   while (changed) {
    110. 

  110.     changed = false;
    111. 

  111.     for (const node of nodes)
    112. 

  112.       if (
    113. 

  113.         !affected.has(node.id) &&
    114. 

  114.         node.dependsOn.some((d) => affected.has(d))
    115. 

  115.       ) {
    116. 

  116.         affected.add(node.id);
    117. 

  117.         changed = true;
    118. 

  118.       }
    119. 

  119.   }
    120. 

  120.   return nodes.filter((n) => affected.has(n.id)).map((n) => n.id);
    121. 

  121. }
    122. 

  122. export function perturb(state: DesignState, path: string, value: unknown) {
    123. 

  123.   if (
    124. 

  124.     !path.startsWith('/') ||
    125. 

  125.     path
    126. 

  126.       .split('/')
    127. 

  127.       .some((k) => ['__proto__', 'constructor', 'prototype'].includes(k))
    128. 

  128.   )
    129. 

  129.     throw new Error('path must be a safe JSON pointer');
    130. 

  130.   if (state.locks.some((lock) => overlaps(lock, path)))
    131. 

  131.     throw new Error(
    132. 

  132.       `path ${path} is locked; remove the lock explicitly before editing`,
    133. 

  133.     );
    134. 

  134.   const next = structuredClone(state);
    135. 

  135.   const keys = path
    136. 

  136.     .slice(1)
    137. 

  137.     .split('/')
    138. 

  138.     .map((s) => s.replace(/~1/g, '/').replace(/~0/g, '~'));
    139. 

  139.   let parent: unknown = next;
    140. 

  140.   for (const key of keys.slice(0, -1)) {
    141. 

  141.     if (!parent || typeof parent !== 'object' || !Object.hasOwn(parent, key))
    142. 

  142.       throw new Error(`unknown state path ${path}`);
    143. 

  143.     parent = (parent as Record<string, unknown>)[key];
    144. 

  144.   }
    145. 

  145.   const last = keys.at(-1)!;
    146. 

  146.   if (!parent || typeof parent !== 'object' || !Object.hasOwn(parent, last))
    147. 

  147.     throw new Error(`unknown state path ${path}`);
    148. 

  148.   (parent as Record<string, unknown>)[last] = value;
    149. 

  149.   return {
    150. 

  150.     state: parseState(next),
    151. 

  151.     changed: [path],
    152. 

  152.     invalidated: invalidated(state, [path]),
    153. 

  153.     measurementStatus: 'invalidated; remeasure before selection',
    154. 

  154.   };
    155. 

  155. }

  217. function selectorVariables(program: EnergyProgram) {
    218. 

  218.   return program.variables.map(({ id, unit, label, valueAliases }) => ({
    219. 

  219.     id,
    220. 

  220.     unit,

  225. function preferenceSummary(
    226. 

  226.   program: EnergyProgram,
    227. 

  227.   factors: Pick<
    228. 

  228.     EnergyEvaluation['factors'][number],
    229. 

  229.     'id' | 'scenario' | 'value' | 'residual' | 'mode'
    230. 

  230.   >[],
    231. 

  231.   violationsOnly = false,
    232. 

  232. ) {
    233. 

  233.   const summaries = program.factors
    234. 

  234.     .filter((f) => f.mode === 'preference')
    235. 

  235.     .flatMap((definition) => {
    236. 

  236.       const rows = factors.filter((f) => f.id === definition.id);
    237. 

  237.       if (!rows.length) return [];
    238. 

  238.       const worst = rows.reduce((a, b) => (b.residual > a.residual ? b : a));
    239. 

  239.       if (violationsOnly && worst.residual === 0) return [];
    240. 

  240.       const expression = definition.expression;
    241. 

  241.       const unit =
    242. 

  242.         typeof expression !== 'number' && 'ref' in expression
    243. 

  243.           ? [...program.variables, ...program.derived].find(
    244. 

  244.               (v) => v.id === expression.ref,
    245. 

  245.             )?.unit
    246. 

  246.           : undefined;
    247. 

  247.       return [
    248. 

  248.         {
    249. 

  249.           id: definition.id,
    250. 

  250.           ...(unit ? { unit } : {}),
    251. 

  251.           values: [
    252. 

  252.             Math.min(...rows.map((f) => f.value)),
    253. 

  253.             Math.max(...rows.map((f) => f.value)),
    254. 

  254.           ],
    255. 

  255.           maxResidual: worst.residual,
    256. 

  256.           worstScenario: {
    257. 

  257.             id: worst.scenario,
    258. 

  258.             value: worst.value,
    259. 

  259.             residual: worst.residual,
    260. 

  260.             targetBands:
    261. 

  261.               definition.scenarioBands?.[worst.scenario] ?? definition.bands,
    262. 

  262.           },
    263. 

  263.           provenanceKind: definition.provenance.kind,
    264. 

  264.         },
    265. 

  265.       ];
    266. 

  266.     })
    267. 

  267.     .sort((a, b) => b.maxResidual - a.maxResidual || a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
    268. 

  268.   return {
    269. 

  269.     rows: summaries.slice(0, preferenceSummaryLimit),
    270. 

  270.     omitted: Math.max(0, summaries.length - preferenceSummaryLimit),
    271. 

  271.   };
    272. 

  272. }
