  315. const pendingScope =
    316. 

  316.   'Stage choices express separate, brief-conditioned preferences. Mathematical admissibility is not renderer verification or a universal beauty score.';
    317. 

  317. 

  318. function normalize(input: DecisionWorkflowInput) {
    319. 

  319.   // Bound hostile/deep input before recursive Zod expression parsing.
    320. 

  320.   let nodes = 0;
    321. 

  321.   const inspect = (value: unknown, depth = 0): void => {
    322. 

  322.     if (++nodes > 2000000 || depth > 48)
    323. 

  323.       throw new Error('Workflow exceeds input size/depth budget');
    324. 

  324.     if (value && typeof value === 'object')
    325. 

  325.       for (const child of Object.values(value)) inspect(child, depth + 1);
    326. 

  326.   };
    327. 

  327.   inspect(input);
    328. 

  328.   const request = RunDecisionWorkflowSchema.parse(input);
    329. 

  329.   const stages = new Map<string, Stage>();
    330. 

  330.   for (const stage of request.workflow.stages) {
    331. 

  331.     if (stages.has(stage.id)) throw new Error(`Duplicate stage ${stage.id}`);
    332. 

  332.     stages.set(stage.id, stage);
    333. 

  333.     if (new Set(stage.dependsOn).size !== stage.dependsOn.length)
    334. 

  334.       throw new Error(`Duplicate dependency in ${stage.id}`);
    335. 

  335.     if (new Set(stage.contextKeys).size !== stage.contextKeys.length)
    336. 

  336.       throw new Error(`Duplicate context key in ${stage.id}`);
    337. 

  337.     for (const key of stage.contextKeys)
    338. 

  338.       if (!Object.hasOwn(request.workflow.context, key))
    339. 

  339.         throw new Error(`Unknown context input ${key}`);
    340. 

  340.     compileEnergy(stage.program);
    341. 

  341.   }
    342. 

  342.   let edges = 0;
    343. 

  343.   for (const stage of stages.values()) {
    344. 

  344.     edges += stage.dependsOn.length;
    345. 

  345.     for (const dependency of stage.dependsOn)
    346. 

  346.       if (!stages.has(dependency) || dependency === stage.id)
    347. 

  347.         throw new Error(`Unknown/self dependency ${dependency}`);
    348. 

  348.     const bound = new Set<string>();
    349. 

  349.     for (const binding of stage.bindings) {
    350. 

  350.       if (!stage.dependsOn.includes(binding.stage))
    351. 

  351.         throw new Error(
    352. 

  352.           `Binding ${binding.input} must declare dependency ${binding.stage}`,
    353. 

  353.         );
    354. 

  354.       if (
    355. 

  355.         !stages
    356. 

  356.           .get(binding.stage)!
    357. 

  357.           .program.variables.some((v) => v.id === binding.variable)
    358. 

  358.       )
    359. 

  359.         throw new Error(
    360. 

  360.           `Unknown predecessor variable ${binding.stage}.${binding.variable}`,
    361. 

  361.         );
    362. 

  362.       if (bound.has(binding.input))
    363. 

  363.         throw new Error(`Duplicate bound input ${binding.input}`);
    364. 

  364.       bound.add(binding.input);
    365. 

  365.       if (
    366. 

  366.         stage.program.scenarios.some(
    367. 

  367.           (s) => !Object.hasOwn(s.values, binding.input),
    368. 

  368.         )
    369. 

  369.       )
    370. 

  370.         throw new Error(
    371. 

  371.           `Binding target ${binding.input} must be a declared scenario input`,
    372. 

  372.         );
    373. 

  373.     }
    374. 

  374.     if (stage.when !== undefined) {
    375. 

  375.       validateExpression(stage.when);
    376. 

  376.       for (const ref of expressionRefs(stage.when))
    377. 

  377.         if (!bound.has(ref))
    378. 

  378.           throw new Error(
    379. 

  379.             `Unknown guard input ${ref}; guards use predecessor bindings`,
    380. 

  380.           );
    381. 

  381.     }
    382. 

  382.     if (stage.selection === 'local-selector' && !request.selector)
    383. 

  383.       throw new Error(`Stage ${stage.id} requires selector configuration`);
    384. 

  384.     if (stage.lookahead) {
    385. 

  385.       if (
    386. 

  386.         new Set(stage.lookahead.successors).size !==
    387. 

  387.         stage.lookahead.successors.length
    388. 

  388.       )
    389. 

  389.         throw new Error(`Duplicate lookahead successor in ${stage.id}`);
    390. 

  390.       for (const id of stage.lookahead.successors)
    391. 

  391.         if (!stages.get(id)?.dependsOn.includes(stage.id))
    392. 

  392.           throw new Error(
    393. 

  393.             `Lookahead ${stage.id}.${id} must follow a declared direct dependency`,
    394. 

  394.           );
    395. 

  395.     }
    396. 

  396.   }
    397. 

  397.   if (edges > 128) throw new Error('Workflow exceeds dependency edge budget');
    398. 

  398.   const order: string[] = [];
    399. 

  399.   while (order.length < stages.size) {
    400. 

  400.     const ready = [...stages.values()]
    401. 

  401.       .filter(
    402. 

  402.         (s) =>
    403. 

  403.           !order.includes(s.id) && s.dependsOn.every((d) => order.includes(d)),
    404. 

  404.       )
    405. 

  405.       .sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
    406. 

  406.     if (!ready.length) throw new Error('Cyclic workflow dependencies');
    407. 

  407.     order.push(...ready.map((s) => s.id));
    408. 

  408.   }
    409. 

  409.   const reserved = [...stages.values()].reduce(
    410. 

  410.     (sum, s) =>
    411. 

  411.       sum +
    412. 

  412.       EnergySearchOptionsSchema.parse(s.options ?? {}).maxEvaluations +
    413. 

  413.       (s.lookahead?.maxEvaluations ?? 0),
    414. 

  414.     0,
    415. 

  415.   );
    416. 

  416.   if (
    417. 

  417.     reserved > request.workflow.budgets.maxEvaluations ||
    418. 

  418.     reserved > request.workflow.budgets.maxGraphNodes
    419. 

  419.   )
    420. 

  420.     throw new Error(
    421. 

  421.       'Declared stage search budgets exceed workflow evaluation/graph budget',
    422. 

  422.     );
    423. 

  423.   for (const [name, fallback] of [
    424. 

  424.     ['maxFactorObservations', 32000000],
    425. 

  425.     ['maxExpressionOperations', 800000000],
    426. 

  426.   ] as const) {
    427. 

  427.     const amount = [...stages.values()].reduce(
    428. 

  428.       (sum, s) =>
    429. 

  429.         sum +
    430. 

  430.         EnergySearchOptionsSchema.parse(s.options ?? {})[name] +
    431. 

  431.         (s.lookahead?.[name] ?? 0),
    432. 

  432.       0,
    433. 

  433.     );
    434. 

  434.     if (amount > (request.workflow.budgets[name] ?? fallback))
    435. 

  435.       throw new Error(
    436. 

  436.         `Declared stage and lookahead ${name} exceed workflow budget`,
    437. 

  437.       );
    438. 

  438.   }
    439. 

  439.   if (request.recipe && Object.keys(request.recipe.entries).length > 16)
    440. 

  440.     throw new Error('Recipe exceeds stage budget');
    441. 

  441.   return { request, stages, order };
    442. 

  442. }
    443. 

  443. 

  444. /** Explicit executable machine: step advances one dependency-ordered design stage; run drives it to completion. */
    445. 

  445. export class DecisionWorkflowMachine {
    446. 

  446.   readonly order: readonly string[];
    447. 

  447.   private request: Request;
    448. 

  448.   private stages: Map<string, Stage>;
    449. 

  449.   private hooks: DecisionWorkflowTestHooks | undefined;
    450. 

  450.   private results: Record<string, DecisionStageResult> = {};
    451. 

  451.   private entries: Record<string, CacheEntry> = {};
    452. 

  452.   private cursor = 0;
    453. 

  453.   private running = false;
    454. 

  454.   private checkpoint: string | null = null;
    455. 

  455.   private reusableSearches = new Map<string, EnergySearch>();
    456. 

  456.   private session:
    457. 

  457.     Awaited<ReturnType<typeof prepareLocalOptionsSession>> | undefined;
    458. 

  458.   private statistics: Statistics = {
    459. 

  459.     searches: 0,
    460. 

  460.     evaluated: 0,
    461. 

  461.     revalidated: 0,
    462. 

  462.     factorEvaluations: 0,
    463. 

  463.     graphNodes: 0,
    464. 

  464.     selectorCalls: 0,
    465. 

  465.     requestedReadoutPasses: 0,
    466. 

  466.     completedReadoutPasses: 0,
    467. 

  467.     neuralCalls: 0,
    468. 

  468.     testSelectorCalls: 0,
    469. 

  469.     cacheHits: 0,
    470. 

  470.     checkpointHashes: 0,
    471. 

  471.     expressionOperationUpperBound: 0,
    472. 

  472.     lookahead: {
    473. 

  473.       searches: 0,
    474. 

  474.       evaluated: 0,
    475. 

  475.       factorEvaluations: 0,
    476. 

  476.       expressionOperationUpperBound: 0,
    477. 

  477.       graphNodes: 0,
    478. 

  478.       branches: 0,
    479. 

  479.       revalidated: 0,
    480. 

  480.       reusedSearches: 0,
    481. 

  481.     },
    482. 

  482.   };
    483. 

  483.   constructor(
    484. 

  484.     input: DecisionWorkflowInput,
    485. 

  485.     hooks?: DecisionWorkflowTestHooks,
    486. 

  486.     private execution: SelectorExecutionOptions = {},
    487. 

  487.   ) {
    488. 

  488.     const normalized = normalize(input);
    489. 

  489.     this.request = normalized.request;
    490. 

  490.     this.stages = normalized.stages;
    491. 

  491.     this.order = Object.freeze(normalized.order);
    492. 

  492.     this.hooks = hooks;
    493. 

  493.     if (hooks && !hooks.testSelectorIdentity.trim())
    494. 

  494.       throw new Error('Test selector needs an explicit identity');
    495. 

  495.   }
    496. 

  496.   getSnapshot() {
    497. 

  497.     const complete = this.cursor === this.order.length;
    498. 

  498.     const successful = Object.values(this.results).every((r) =>
    499. 

  499.       ['chosen', 'replayed', 'skipped'].includes(r.status),
    500. 

  500.     );
    501. 

  501.     return structuredClone({
    502. 

  502.       state: complete
    503. 

  503.         ? ('complete' as const)
    504. 

  504.         : this.running
    505. 

  505.           ? ('running' as const)
    506. 

  506.           : ('ready' as const),
    507. 

  507.       status: !complete
    508. 

  508.         ? ('in-progress' as const)
    509. 

  509.         : !successful
    510. 

  510.           ? ('incomplete' as const)
    511. 

  511.           : this.statistics.searches
    512. 

  512.             ? ('proposed' as const)
    513. 

  513.             : ('replayed' as const),
    514. 

  514.       nextStage: this.order[this.cursor] ?? null,
    515. 

  515.       order: this.order,
    516. 

  516.       stages: this.results,
    517. 

  517.       recipe: {
    518. 

  518.         version: 1 as const,
    519. 

  519.         engine: 'design-decision-workflow-v1' as const,
    520. 

  520.         workflowId: this.request.workflow.id,
    521. 

  521.         entries: this.entries,
    522. 

  522.       },
    523. 

  523.       statistics: this.statistics,
    524. 

  524.       rendererVerification: 'pending' as const,
    525. 

  525.       scope: pendingScope,
    526. 

  526.     });
    527. 

  527.   }
    528. 

  528.   async step() {
    529. 

  529.     this.execution.signal?.throwIfAborted();
    530. 

  530.     if (this.running)
    531. 

  531.       throw new Error('Concurrent workflow step is not allowed');
    532. 

  532.     if (this.cursor === this.order.length) return this.getSnapshot();
    533. 

  533.     this.running = true;
    534. 

  534.     const id = this.order[this.cursor]!;
    535. 

  535.     try {
    536. 

  536.       this.results[id] = await this.execute(this.stages.get(id)!);
    537. 

  537.     } catch (error) {
    538. 

  538.       this.results[id] = {
    539. 

  539.         id,
    540. 

  540.         status: 'failed',
    541. 

  541.         cacheKey: null,
    542. 

  542.         assignment: null,
    543. 

  543.         alternatives: [],
    544. 

  544.         selection: null,
    545. 

  545.         evidence: null,
    546. 

  546.         reason: String(error),
    547. 

  547.         rendererVerification: 'pending',
    548. 

  548.       };
    549. 

  549.       if (this.execution.signal?.aborted) throw error;
    550. 

  550.     } finally {
    551. 

  551.       this.cursor++;
    552. 

  552.       this.running = false;
    553. 

  553.     }
    554. 

  554.     return this.getSnapshot();
    555. 

  555.   }
    556. 

  556.   async run() {
    557. 

  557.     while (this.cursor < this.order.length) await this.step();
    558. 

  558.     return this.getSnapshot();
    559. 

  559.   }
    560. 

  560.   private bindingsFor(
    561. 

  561.     stage: Stage,
    562. 

  562.     parent?: { id: string; assignment: Assignment },
    563. 

  563.   ): Assignment {
    564. 

  564.     return Object.fromEntries(
    565. 

  565.       stage.bindings.map((binding) => [
    566. 

  566.         binding.input,
    567. 

  567.         (binding.stage === parent?.id
    568. 

  568.           ? parent.assignment
    569. 

  569.           : this.results[binding.stage]?.assignment)?.[binding.variable],
    570. 

  570.       ]),
    571. 

  571.     ) as Assignment;
    572. 

  572.   }
    573. 

  573.   private boundProgram(stage: Stage, bindings: Assignment) {
    574. 

  574.     const program = structuredClone(stage.program);
    575. 

  575.     for (const scenario of program.scenarios)
    576. 

  576.       Object.assign(scenario.values, bindings);
    577. 

  577.     return program;
    578. 

  578.   }
    579. 

  579.   private lookaheadIdentity(stage: Stage) {
    580. 

  580.     if (!stage.lookahead) return undefined;
    581. 

  581.     return {
    582. 

  582.       engine: 'direct-successor-lookahead-v1',
    583. 

  583.       policy: stage.lookahead,
    584. 

  584.       successors: [...stage.lookahead.successors].sort().map((id) => {
    585. 

  585.         const successor = this.stages.get(id)!;
    586. 

  586.         return {
    587. 

  587.           stage: successor,
    588. 

  588.           context: Object.fromEntries(
    589. 

  589.             successor.contextKeys.map((key) => [
    590. 

  590.               key,
    591. 

  591.               this.request.workflow.context[key],
    592. 

  592.             ]),
    593. 

  593.           ),
    594. 

  594.           otherPredecessors: successor.dependsOn
    595. 

  595.             .filter((id) => id !== stage.id)
    596. 

  596.             .sort()
    597. 

  597.             .map((id) => ({
    598. 

  598.               id,
    599. 

  599.               key: this.results[id]?.cacheKey ?? null,
    600. 

  600.               assignment: this.results[id]?.assignment ?? null,
    601. 

  601.             })),
    602. 

  602.         };
    603. 

  603.       }),
    604. 

  604.     };
    605. 

  605.   }
    606. 

  606.   private noteSearch(search: EnergySearch, lookahead: boolean) {
    607. 

  607.     this.statistics.searches++;
    608. 

  608.     this.statistics.evaluated += search.statistics.evaluated;
    609. 

  609.     this.statistics.factorEvaluations += search.statistics.factorEvaluations;
    610. 

  610.     this.statistics.expressionOperationUpperBound +=
    611. 

  611.       search.statistics.expressionOperationUpperBound;
    612. 

  612.     this.statistics.graphNodes += search.choiceGraph.nodes.length;
    613. 

  613.     if (lookahead) {
    614. 

  614.       const count = this.statistics.lookahead;
    615. 

  615.       count.searches++;
    616. 

  616.       count.evaluated += search.statistics.evaluated;
    617. 

  617.       count.factorEvaluations += search.statistics.factorEvaluations;
    618. 

  618.       count.expressionOperationUpperBound +=
    619. 

  619.         search.statistics.expressionOperationUpperBound;
    620. 

  620.       count.graphNodes += search.choiceGraph.nodes.length;
    621. 

  621.     }
    622. 

  622.   }
    623. 

  623.   private inspectSuccessors(
    624. 

  624.     stage: Stage,
    625. 

  625.     candidates: EnergyEvaluation[],
    626. 

  626.   ): LookaheadEvidence {
    627. 

  627.     const policy = stage.lookahead!;
    628. 

  628.     const branches: LookaheadBranch[] = [];
    629. 

  629.     let remainingEvaluations = policy.maxEvaluations,
    630. 

  630.       remainingFactors = policy.maxFactorObservations,
    631. 

  631.       remainingOperations = policy.maxExpressionOperations;
    632. 

  632.     for (const parent of candidates.slice(0, policy.maxBranches)) {
    633. 

  633.       this.statistics.lookahead.branches++;
    634. 

  634.       const successors: SuccessorOutcome[] = [];
    635. 

  635.       for (const id of policy.successors) {
    636. 

  636.         const child = this.stages.get(id)!;
    637. 

  637.         const unresolved = child.dependsOn.filter(
    638. 

  638.           (id) => id !== stage.id && !this.results[id]?.assignment,
    639. 

  639.         );
    640. 

  640.         if (unresolved.length) {
    641. 

  641.           successors.push({
    642. 

  642.             stage: id,
    643. 

  643.             outcome: 'unknown',
    644. 

  644.             bindings: {},
    645. 

  645.             search: null,
    646. 

  646.             reason: `Other predecessors unresolved: ${unresolved.join(', ')}`,
    647. 

  647.           });
    648. 

  648.           continue;
    649. 

  649.         }
    650. 

  650.         const bindings = this.bindingsFor(child, {
    651. 

  651.           id: stage.id,
    652. 

  652.           assignment: parent.assignment,
    653. 

  653.         });
    654. 

  654.         if (
    655. 

  655.           child.when !== undefined &&
    656. 

  656.           evaluateExpression(child.when, bindings) === 0
    657. 

  657.         ) {
    658. 

  658.           successors.push({
    659. 

  659.             stage: id,
    660. 

  660.             outcome: 'skipped',
    661. 

  661.             bindings,
    662. 

  662.             search: null,
    663. 

  663.           });
    664. 

  664.           continue;
    665. 

  665.         }
    666. 

  666.         if (
    667. 

  667.           remainingEvaluations < 1 ||
    668. 

  668.           remainingFactors < 1 ||
    669. 

  669.           remainingOperations < 1
    670. 

  670.         ) {
    671. 

  671.           successors.push({
    672. 

  672.             stage: id,
    673. 

  673.             outcome: 'unknown',
    674. 

  674.             bindings,
    675. 

  675.             search: null,
    676. 

  676.             reason: 'Lookahead numeric budget exhausted',
    677. 

  677.           });
    678. 

  678.           continue;
    679. 

  679.         }
    680. 

  680.         const program = this.boundProgram(child, bindings);
    681. 

  681.         const declared = EnergySearchOptionsSchema.parse(child.options ?? {});
    682. 

  682.         const search = searchEnergy({
    683. 

  683.           program,
    684. 

  684.           options: {
    685. 

  685.             ...declared,
    686. 

  686.             maxEvaluations: Math.min(
    687. 

  687.               declared.maxEvaluations,
    688. 

  688.               remainingEvaluations,
    689. 

  689.             ),
    690. 

  690.             maxFactorObservations: Math.min(
    691. 

  691.               declared.maxFactorObservations,
    692. 

  692.               remainingFactors,
    693. 

  693.             ),
    694. 

  694.             maxExpressionOperations: Math.min(
    695. 

  695.               declared.maxExpressionOperations,
    696. 

  696.               remainingOperations,
    697. 

  697.             ),
    698. 

  698.           },
    699. 

  699.         });
    700. 

  700.         this.noteSearch(search, true);
    701. 

  701.         remainingEvaluations -= search.statistics.evaluated;
    702. 

  702.         remainingFactors -= search.statistics.factorEvaluations;
    703. 

  703.         remainingOperations -= search.statistics.expressionOperationUpperBound;
    704. 

  704.         if (search.statistics.complete)
    705. 

  705.           this.reusableSearches.set(
    706. 

  706.             hash({
    707. 

  707.               programIdentity: search.programIdentity,
    708. 

  708.               options: declared,
    709. 

  709.             }),
    710. 

  710.             search,
    711. 

  711.           );
    712. 

  712.         const satisfied = (a: EnergyEvaluation) =>
    713. 

  713.           a.factors.every((f) => f.mode === 'hard' || f.residual === 0);
    714. 

  714.         const witness =
    715. 

  715.           search.candidates.find(satisfied) ?? search.candidates[0];
    716. 

  716.         const receipt: SuccessorSearchEvidence = {
    717. 

  717.           programIdentity: search.programIdentity,
    718. 

  718.           searchReceiptHash: hash(search),
    719. 

  719.           statistics: search.statistics,
    720. 

  720.           candidates: search.candidates.map((a) => ({
    721. 

  721.             id: a.id,
    722. 

  722.             assignment: a.assignment,
    723. 

  723.             energy: a.energy,
    724. 

  724.             hardFailures: a.hardFailures,
    725. 

  725.             preferenceTargetsSatisfied: satisfied(a),
    726. 

  726.           })),
    727. 

  727.           witness: witness
    728. 

  728.             ? {
    729. 

  729.                 id: witness.id,
    730. 

  730.                 assignment: witness.assignment,
    731. 

  731.                 preferenceTargetsSatisfied: satisfied(witness),
    732. 

  732.                 factors: witness.factors.map(
    733. 

  733.                   ({ id, scenario, value, residual, energy, mode }) => ({
    734. 

  734.                     id,
    735. 

  735.                     scenario,
    736. 

  736.                     value,
    737. 

  737.                     residual,
    738. 

  738.                     energy,
    739. 

  739.                     mode,
    740. 

  740.                   }),
    741. 

  741.                 ),
    742. 

  742.               }
    743. 

  743.             : null,
    744. 

  744.           scope:
    745. 

  745.             'Retained assignments and one source-bound witness from this bounded search. Full program, graph and derived tables remain private for this run. A saved best candidate is not a global optimum.',
    746. 

  746.         };
    747. 

  747.         successors.push({
    748. 

  748.           stage: id,
    749. 

  749.           bindings,
    750. 

  750.           search: receipt,
    751. 

  751.           outcome: search.candidates.length
    752. 

  752.             ? 'admissible'
    753. 

  753.             : search.statistics.complete
    754. 

  754.               ? 'infeasible'
    755. 

  755.               : 'unknown',
    756. 

  756.           ...(!search.candidates.length && !search.statistics.complete
    757. 

  757.             ? { reason: 'Incomplete search has no continuation witness' }
    758. 

  758.             : {}),
    759. 

  759.         });
    760. 

  760.       }
    761. 

  761.       branches.push({
    762. 

  762.         parent,
    763. 

  763.         successors,
    764. 

  764.         completion: successors.every((s) =>
    765. 

  765.           ['admissible', 'skipped'].includes(s.outcome),
    766. 

  766.         )
    767. 

  767.           ? 'witnessed'
    768. 

  768.           : successors.some((s) => s.outcome === 'infeasible')
    769. 

  769.             ? 'infeasible'
    770. 

  770.             : 'unknown',
    771. 

  771.         jointlySatisfied: successors.every(
    772. 

  772.           (s) =>
    773. 

  773.             s.outcome === 'skipped' ||
    774. 

  774.             s.search?.candidates.some((a) => a.preferenceTargetsSatisfied),
    775. 

  775.         ),
    776. 

  776.       });
    777. 

  777.     }
    778. 

  778.     return {
    779. 

  779.       engine: 'direct-successor-lookahead-v1',
    780. 

  780.       policy,
    781. 

  781.       branches,
    782. 

  782.       uncoveredParents: candidates.slice(policy.maxBranches).map((a) => a.id),
    783. 

  783.       scope:
    784. 

  784.         'Witnesses cover declared direct successors under retained parent assignments. No recursive DAG optimum, summed cross-stage objective, renderer or preference verification. Unknown branches are not proven impossible.',
    785. 

  785.     };
    786. 

  786.   }
    787. 

  787.   private continuation(
    788. 

  788.     stage: Stage,
    789. 

  789.     selected: EnergyEvaluation,
    790. 

  790.     lookahead: LookaheadEvidence,
    791. 

  791.   ): z.infer<typeof ContinuationSchema> {
    792. 

  792.     const branch = lookahead.branches.find((b) => b.parent.id === selected.id);
    793. 

  793.     return {
    794. 

  794.       engine: 'direct-successor-lookahead-v1',
    795. 

  795.       parent: selected.assignment,
    796. 

  796.       successors: stage.lookahead!.successors.map((id) => {
    797. 

  797.         const outcome = branch?.successors.find((s) => s.stage === id);
    798. 

  798.         return {
    799. 

  799.           stage: id,
    800. 

  800.           outcome: outcome?.outcome ?? 'unknown',
    801. 

  801.           alternatives:
    802. 

  802.             outcome?.search?.candidates.map((a) => a.assignment) ?? [],
    803. 

  803.         };
    804. 

  804.       }),
    805. 

  805.     };
    806. 

  806.   }
    807. 

  807.   private revalidateContinuation(
    808. 

  808.     stage: Stage,
    809. 

  809.     payload: z.infer<typeof CachePayloadSchema>,
    810. 

  810.   ) {
    811. 

  811.     if (!stage.lookahead || payload.kind !== 'chosen') return;
    812. 

  812.     const receipt = payload.continuation;
    813. 

  813.     if (!receipt || hash(receipt.parent) !== hash(payload.assignment))
    814. 

  814.       throw new Error(
    815. 

  815.         'Cached lookahead continuation does not bind the chosen parent',
    816. 

  816.       );
    817. 

  817.     if (
    818. 

  818.       hash(receipt.successors.map((s) => s.stage).sort()) !==
    819. 

  819.       hash([...stage.lookahead.successors].sort())
    820. 

  820.     )
    821. 

  821.       throw new Error('Cached lookahead successors do not match the policy');
    822. 

  822.     for (const saved of receipt.successors) {
    823. 

  823.       const child = this.stages.get(saved.stage)!;
    824. 

  824.       if (
    825. 

  825.         stage.lookahead.completion === 'require' &&
    826. 

  826.         !['admissible', 'skipped'].includes(saved.outcome)
    827. 

  827.       )
    828. 

  828.         throw new Error('Cached parent has no required continuation witness');
    829. 

  829.       if (saved.outcome === 'unknown' || saved.outcome === 'infeasible') {
    830. 

  830.         if (saved.alternatives.length)
    831. 

  831.           throw new Error('Unwitnessed continuation contains assignments');
    832. 

  832.         continue; // Historical absence is not re-certified by replay.
    833. 

  833.       }
    834. 

  834.       if (
    835. 

  835.         child.dependsOn.some(
    836. 

  836.           (id) => id !== stage.id && !this.results[id]?.assignment,
    837. 

  837.         )
    838. 

  838.       )
    839. 

  839.         throw new Error('Cached continuation has an unresolved predecessor');
    840. 

  840.       const bindings = this.bindingsFor(child, {
    841. 

  841.         id: stage.id,
    842. 

  842.         assignment: receipt.parent,
    843. 

  843.       });
    844. 

  844.       const active =
    845. 

  845.         child.when === undefined ||
    846. 

  846.         evaluateExpression(child.when, bindings) !== 0;
    847. 

  847.       if (active !== (saved.outcome === 'admissible'))
    848. 

  848.         throw new Error('Cached successor guard changed');
    849. 

  849.       if (!active) {
    850. 

  850.         if (saved.alternatives.length)
    851. 

  851.           throw new Error('Skipped continuation has assignments');
    852. 

  852.         continue;
    853. 

  853.       }
    854. 

  854.       if (!saved.alternatives.length)
    855. 

  855.         throw new Error('Cached continuation lacks an admissible witness');
    856. 

  856.       const compiled = compileEnergy(this.boundProgram(child, bindings));
    857. 

  857.       for (const assignment of saved.alternatives) {
    858. 

  858.         this.statistics.revalidated++;
    859. 

  859.         this.statistics.lookahead.revalidated++;
    860. 

  860.         const before = compiled.factorEvaluations;
    861. 

  861.         try {
    862. 

  862.           if (compiled.evaluate(assignment).hardFailures)
    863. 

  863.             throw new Error(
    864. 

  864.               'Cached continuation violates successor hard guards',
    865. 

  865.             );
    866. 

  866.         } finally {
    867. 

  867.           this.statistics.factorEvaluations +=
    868. 

  868.             compiled.factorEvaluations - before;
    869. 

  869.           this.statistics.lookahead.factorEvaluations +=
    870. 

  870.             compiled.factorEvaluations - before;
    871. 

  871.         }
    872. 

  872.       }
    873. 

  873.     }
    874. 

  874.   }
    875. 

  875.   private async execute(stage: Stage): Promise<DecisionStageResult> {
    876. 

  876.     const base = {
    877. 

  877.       id: stage.id,
    878. 

  878.       assignment: null,
    879. 

  879.       alternatives: [],
    880. 

  880.       selection: null,
    881. 

  881.       evidence: null,
    882. 

  882.       rendererVerification: 'pending' as const,
    883. 

  883.     };
    884. 

  884.     const blocked = stage.dependsOn.filter(
    885. 

  885.       (id) => !['chosen', 'replayed'].includes(this.results[id]!.status),
    886. 

  886.     );
    887. 

  887.     if (blocked.length)
    888. 

  888.       return {
    889. 

  889.         ...base,
    890. 

  890.         status: 'blocked',
    891. 

  891.         cacheKey: null,
    892. 

  892.         reason: `Predecessor has no selected assignment: ${blocked.join(', ')}`,
    893. 

  893.       };
    894. 

  894.     if (stage.selection === 'local-selector' && !this.session) {
    895. 

  895.       this.session = await prepareLocalOptionsSession(
    896. 

  896.         this.request.selector!.gguf,
    897. 

  897.         this.execution,
    898. 

  898.       );
    899. 

  899.       this.checkpoint = this.session.checkpointHash;
    900. 

  900.       this.statistics.checkpointHashes++;
    901. 

  901.     }
    902. 

  902.     const bindings = this.bindingsFor(stage);
    903. 

  903.     const program = this.boundProgram(stage, bindings);
    904. 

  904.     const compiled = compileEnergy(program);
    905. 

  905.     const options = EnergySearchOptionsSchema.parse(stage.options ?? {});
    906. 

  906.     const context = Object.fromEntries(
    907. 

  907.       [...stage.contextKeys]
    908. 

  908.         .sort()
    909. 

  909.         .map((key) => [key, this.request.workflow.context[key]]),
    910. 

  910.     );
    911. 

  911.     const guard =
    912. 

  912.       stage.when === undefined ||
    913. 

  913.       evaluateExpression(stage.when, bindings) !== 0;
    914. 

  914.     const policy =
    915. 

  915.       stage.selection === 'local-selector'
    916. 

  916.         ? {
    917. 

  917.             profile: this.request.selector!.profile,
    918. 

  918.             readout: this.request.selector!.readout,
    919. 

  919.             maxPromptTokens: this.request.selector!.maxPromptTokens,
    920. 

  920.             checkpoint: this.checkpoint,
    921. 

  921.             payloadSemantics: selectorPayloadSemantics,
    922. 

  922.             implementation: this.hooks
    923. 

  923.               ? `test-injected:${this.hooks.testSelectorIdentity}`
    924. 

  924.               : 'semif-direct-options-v1',
    925. 

  925.           }
    926. 

  926.         : 'mathematical';
    927. 

  927.     const key = hash({
    928. 

  928.       engine: 'design-decision-workflow-v1',
    929. 

  929.       workflowId: this.request.workflow.id,
    930. 

  930.       stage: {
    931. 

  931.         id: stage.id,
    932. 

  932.         purpose: stage.purpose,
    933. 

  933.         provenance: stage.provenance,
    934. 

  934.         question: stage.question,
    935. 

  935.         dependencies: [...stage.dependsOn].sort().map((id) => ({
    936. 

  936.           id,
    937. 

  937.           key: this.results[id]!.cacheKey,
    938. 

  938.           assignment: this.results[id]!.assignment,
    939. 

  939.         })),
    940. 

  940.         bindings: [...stage.bindings].sort((a, b) =>
    941. 

  941.           a.input.localeCompare(b.input),
    942. 

  942.         ),
    943. 

  943.         when: stage.when ?? null,
    944. 

  944.       },
    945. 

  945.       programIdentity: compiled.identity,
    946. 

  946.       options,
    947. 

  947.       context,
    948. 

  948.       policy,
    949. 

  949.       ...(stage.lookahead ? { lookahead: this.lookaheadIdentity(stage) } : {}),
    950. 

  950.     });
    951. 

  951.     const previous =
    952. 

  952.       this.request.recipe?.workflowId === this.request.workflow.id
    953. 

  953.         ? this.request.recipe.entries[stage.id]
    954. 

  954.         : undefined;
    955. 

  955.     let invalidation: string | undefined;
    956. 

  956.     if (previous) {
    957. 

  957.       if (previous.key !== key)
    958. 

  958.         invalidation =
    959. 

  959.           'Stage program, dependencies, context, guard, policy or checkpoint changed';
    960. 

  960.       else if (hash(previous.payload) !== previous.digest)
    961. 

  961.         invalidation = 'Cached payload integrity mismatch';
    962. 

  962.       else {
    963. 

  963.         try {
    964. 

  964.           const payload = previous.payload;
    965. 

  965.           if (guard !== (payload.kind === 'chosen'))
    966. 

  966.             throw new Error('Cached guard outcome changed');
    967. 

  967.           const alternatives = payload.alternatives.map((a) => {
    968. 

  968.             this.statistics.revalidated++;
    969. 

  969.             const before = compiled.factorEvaluations;
    970. 

  970.             try {
    971. 

  971.               return compiled.evaluate(a);
    972. 

  972.             } finally {
    973. 

  973.               this.statistics.factorEvaluations +=
    974. 

  974.                 compiled.factorEvaluations - before;
    975. 

  975.             }
    976. 

  976.           });
    977. 

  977.           if (alternatives.some((a) => a.hardFailures))
    978. 

  978.             throw new Error('Cached alternative violates mathematical guards');
    979. 

  979.           if (
    980. 

  980.             payload.kind === 'chosen' &&
    981. 

  981.             (!payload.assignment ||
    982. 

  982.               !alternatives.some(
    983. 

  983.                 (a) => hash(a.assignment) === hash(payload.assignment),
    984. 

  984.               ))
    985. 

  985.           )
    986. 

  986.             throw new Error('Cached choice is absent from alternatives');
    987. 

  987.           if (
    988. 

  988.             payload.kind === 'skipped' &&
    989. 

  989.             (payload.assignment !== null || alternatives.length)
    990. 

  990.           )
    991. 

  991.             throw new Error('Skipped stage must not have an assignment');
    992. 

  992.           this.revalidateContinuation(stage, payload);
    993. 

  993.           this.entries[stage.id] = structuredClone(previous);
    994. 

  994.           this.statistics.cacheHits++;
    995. 

  995.           return {
    996. 

  996.             ...base,
    997. 

  997.             status: guard ? 'replayed' : 'skipped',
    998. 

  998.             cacheKey: key,
    999. 

  999.             assignment: payload.assignment,
    1000. 

  1000.             alternatives,
    1001. 

  1001.             selection: payload.selection,
    1002. 

  1002.             evidence: payload.evidence,
    1003. 

  1003.           };
    1004. 

  1004.         } catch (error) {
    1005. 

  1005.           invalidation = `Replay validation failed: ${String(error)}`;
    1006. 

  1006.         }
    1007. 

  1007.       }
    1008. 

  1008.     }
    1009. 

  1009.     const history = previous ? { previous: structuredClone(previous) } : {};
    1010. 

  1010.     if (this.request.mode === 'replay')
    1011. 

  1011.       return {
    1012. 

  1012.         ...base,
    1013. 

  1013.         ...history,
    1014. 

  1014.         status: 'stale',
    1015. 

  1015.         cacheKey: key,
    1016. 

  1016.         reason: invalidation ?? 'No matching cached stage',
    1017. 

  1017.       };
    1018. 

  1018.     if (!guard) {
    1019. 

  1019.       this.save(stage.id, key, {
    1020. 

  1020.         kind: 'skipped',
    1021. 

  1021.         assignment: null,
    1022. 

  1022.         alternatives: [],
    1023. 

  1023.         selection: null,
    1024. 

  1024.         evidence: { guard: false, bindings },
    1025. 

  1025.       });
    1026. 

  1026.       return {
    1027. 

  1027.         ...base,
    1028. 

  1028.         ...history,
    1029. 

  1029.         status: 'skipped',
    1030. 

  1030.         cacheKey: key,
    1031. 

  1031.         evidence: { guard: false, bindings },
    1032. 

  1032.         ...(invalidation ? { reason: invalidation } : {}),
    1033. 

  1033.       };
    1034. 

  1034.     }
    1035. 

  1035.     const reuseKey = hash({ programIdentity: compiled.identity, options });
    1036. 

  1036.     const reused = this.reusableSearches.get(reuseKey);
    1037. 

  1037.     const search = reused ?? searchEnergy({ program, options });
    1038. 

  1038.     if (reused) this.statistics.lookahead.reusedSearches++;
    1039. 

  1039.     else this.noteSearch(search, false);
    1040. 

  1040.     const lookahead =
    1041. 

  1041.       stage.lookahead && search.candidates.length
    1042. 

  1042.         ? this.inspectSuccessors(stage, search.candidates)
    1043. 

  1043.         : null;
    1044. 

  1044.     const evidence = {
    1045. 

  1045.       programIdentity: search.programIdentity,
    1046. 

  1046.       bindings,
    1047. 

  1047.       context,
    1048. 

  1048.       search,
    1049. 

  1049.       ...(reused ? { reusedLookaheadSearch: true } : {}),
    1050. 

  1050.       ...(lookahead ? { lookahead } : {}),
    1051. 

  1051.     };
    1052. 

  1052.     if (!search.candidates.length)
    1053. 

  1053.       return {
    1054. 

  1054.         ...base,
    1055. 

  1055.         ...history,
    1056. 

  1056.         status: 'failed',
    1057. 

  1057.         cacheKey: key,
    1058. 

  1058.         evidence,
    1059. 

  1059.         reason: 'No mathematically admissible candidate in bounded search',
    1060. 

  1060.       };
    1061. 

  1061.     const eligible =
    1062. 

  1062.       lookahead?.policy.completion === 'require'
    1063. 

  1063.         ? search.candidates.filter((a) =>
    1064. 

  1064.             lookahead.branches.some(
    1065. 

  1065.               (b) => b.parent.id === a.id && b.completion === 'witnessed',
    1066. 

  1066.             ),
    1067. 

  1067.           )
    1068. 

  1068.         : search.candidates;
    1069. 

  1069.     if (!eligible.length)
    1070. 

  1070.       return {
    1071. 

  1071.         ...base,
    1072. 

  1072.         ...history,
    1073. 

  1073.         status: 'failed',
    1074. 

  1074.         cacheKey: key,
    1075. 

  1075.         evidence,
    1076. 

  1076.         alternatives: search.candidates,
    1077. 

  1077.         reason:
    1078. 

  1078.           'No retained parent has a witnessed required successor completion within the declared lookahead bounds',
    1079. 

  1079.       };
    1080. 

  1080.     let selected = eligible.reduce(
    1081. 

  1081.       (best, a) => (a.energy < best.energy ? a : best),
    1082. 

  1082.       eligible[0]!,
    1083. 

  1083.     );
    1084. 

  1084.     if (lookahead?.policy.parentTies === 'prefer-successor-targets') {
    1085. 

  1085.       const costs = (a: EnergyEvaluation) =>
    1086. 

  1086.         hash(
    1087. 

  1087.           a.factors.filter((f) => f.mode === 'preference').map((f) => f.energy),
    1088. 

  1088.         );
    1089. 

  1089.       const tied = costs(selected);
    1090. 

  1090.       selected =
    1091. 

  1091.         eligible.find(
    1092. 

  1092.           (a) =>
    1093. 

  1093.             costs(a) === tied &&
    1094. 

  1094.             lookahead.branches.some(
    1095. 

  1095.               (b) => b.parent.id === a.id && b.jointlySatisfied,
    1096. 

  1096.             ),
    1097. 

  1097.         ) ?? selected;
    1098. 

  1098.     }
    1099. 

  1099.     let selection: unknown = {
    1100. 

  1100.       policy: lookahead
    1101. 

  1101.         ? 'minimum parent preference energy among eligible paths; optional successor target guidance only on identical parent cost vectors'
    1102. 

  1102.         : 'minimum declared preference energy; deterministic ties',
    1103. 

  1103.       modelCalled: false,
    1104. 

  1104.     };
    1105. 

  1105.     if (stage.selection === 'local-selector' && eligible.length > 1) {
    1106. 

  1106.       const selector = this.request.selector!;
    1107. 

  1107.       const alternatives = eligible.slice(
    1108. 

  1108.         0,
    1109. 

  1109.         selector.readout === 'balanced' ? 6 : 16,
    1110. 

  1110.       );
    1111. 

  1111.       const passes = selector.readout === 'balanced' ? alternatives.length : 1;
    1112. 

  1112.       if (
    1113. 

  1113.         this.statistics.selectorCalls + 1 >
    1114. 

  1114.           this.request.workflow.budgets.maxSelectorCalls ||
    1115. 

  1115.         this.statistics.requestedReadoutPasses + passes >
    1116. 

  1116.           this.request.workflow.budgets.maxReadoutPasses
    1117. 

  1117.       )
    1118. 

  1118.         return {
    1119. 

  1119.           ...base,
    1120. 

  1120.           ...history,
    1121. 

  1121.           status: 'failed',
    1122. 

  1122.           cacheKey: key,
    1123. 

  1123.           alternatives: search.candidates,
    1124. 

  1124.           evidence,
    1125. 

  1125.           reason: 'Selector call/readout budget exhausted',
    1126. 

  1126.         };
    1127. 

  1127.       this.statistics.selectorCalls++;
    1128. 

  1128.       this.statistics.requestedReadoutPasses += passes;
    1129. 

  1129.       if (this.hooks) this.statistics.testSelectorCalls++;
    1130. 

  1130.       else this.statistics.neuralCalls++;
    1131. 

  1131.       try {
    1132. 

  1132.         const request: SelectionRequest = {
    1133. 

  1133.           ...selector,
    1134. 

  1134.           payload: {
    1135. 

  1135.             state: {
    1136. 

  1136.               stage: stage.id,
    1137. 

  1137.               purpose: stage.purpose,
    1138. 

  1138.               brief: program.brief,
    1139. 

  1139.               context,
    1140. 

  1140.               bindings,
    1141. 

  1141.               scope: pendingScope,
    1142. 

  1142.               variables: selectorVariables(compiled.program),
    1143. 

  1143.               consequenceSummary: {
    1144. 

  1144.                 limit: preferenceSummaryLimit,
    1145. 

  1145.                 order:
    1146. 

  1146.                   'largest worst-scenario normalized residual first; source-bound full factors remain in the receipt',
    1147. 

  1147.               },
    1148. 

  1148.               ...(lookahead
    1149. 

  1149.                 ? {
    1150. 

  1150.                     lookahead: {
    1151. 

  1151.                       completion: lookahead.policy.completion,
    1152. 

  1152.                       parentTies: lookahead.policy.parentTies,
    1153. 

  1153.                       scope: lookahead.scope,
    1154. 

  1154.                       successorVariables: Object.fromEntries(
    1155. 

  1155.                         stage.lookahead!.successors.map((id) => [
    1156. 

  1156.                           id,
    1157. 

  1157.                           selectorVariables(this.stages.get(id)!.program),
    1158. 

  1158.                         ]),
    1159. 

  1159.                       ),
    1160. 

  1160.                     },
    1161. 

  1161.                   }
    1162. 

  1162.                 : {}),
    1163. 

  1163.             },
    1164. 

  1164.             question: stage.question,
    1165. 

  1165.             options: alternatives.map((candidate, i) => {
    1166. 

  1166.               const summary = preferenceSummary(
    1167. 

  1167.                 compiled.program,
    1168. 

  1168.                 candidate.factors,
    1169. 

  1169.               );
    1170. 

  1170.               return {
    1171. 

  1171.                 id: `alternative-${i}`,
    1172. 

  1172.                 description: JSON.stringify({
    1173. 

  1173.                   assignment: candidate.assignment,
    1174. 

  1174.                   preferences: summary.rows,
    1175. 

  1175.                   omittedPreferenceFactors: summary.omitted,
    1176. 

  1176.                   hardGuards: 'passed in every declared scenario',
    1177. 

  1177.                   ...(lookahead
    1178. 

  1178.                     ? {
    1179. 

  1179.                         successors:
    1180. 

  1180.                           lookahead.branches
    1181. 

  1181.                             .find((b) => b.parent.id === candidate.id)
    1182. 

  1182.                             ?.successors.map((s) => {
    1183. 

  1183.                               const witness = s.search?.witness;
    1184. 

  1184.                               const childSummary = preferenceSummary(
    1185. 

  1185.                                 this.stages.get(s.stage)!.program,
    1186. 

  1186.                                 witness?.factors ?? [],
    1187. 

  1187.                                 true,
    1188. 

  1188.                               );
    1189. 

  1189.                               return {
    1190. 

  1190.                                 stage: s.stage,
    1191. 

  1191.                                 outcome: s.outcome,
    1192. 

  1192.                                 completeSearch:
    1193. 

  1193.                                   s.search?.statistics.complete ?? null,
    1194. 

  1194.                                 witness: witness?.assignment ?? null,
    1195.

    1196. 

  1196.                                   witness?.preferenceTargetsSatisfied ?? null,
    1197.

    1198.

    1199. 

  1199.                                 reason: s.reason ?? null,

  1200.                               };
    1201. 

  1201.                             }) ?? 'not inspected within branch budget',
    1202. 

  1202.                       }
    1203. 

  1203.                     : {}),
    1204. 

  1204.                 }),
    1205. 

  1205.               };
    1206. 

  1206.             }),
    1207. 

  1207.           },
    1208. 

  1208.         };
    1209.

  1210.           ? this.hooks.testSelector(request)
    1211. 

  1211.           : this.session!.select(request));
    1212. 

  1212.         this.statistics.completedReadoutPasses += passes;
    1213. 

  1213.         const index = request.payload.options.findIndex(
    1214. 

  1214.           (o) => o.id === readout.selected,
    1215. 

  1215.         );
    1216. 

  1216.         if (index < 0)
    1217. 

  1217.           throw new Error('Selector returned unknown mathematical alternative');
    1218. 

  1218.         selected = alternatives[index]!;
    1219. 

  1219.         selection = {
    1220. 

  1220.           ...readout,
    1221. 

  1221.           modelCalled: !this.hooks,
    1222. 

  1222.           testInjected: Boolean(this.hooks),
    1223. 

  1223.           requestedReadoutPasses: passes,
    1224. 

  1224.         };
    1225. 

  1225.       } catch (error) {
    1226. 

  1226.         if (this.execution.signal?.aborted) throw error;
    1227. 

  1227.         return {
    1228. 

  1228.           ...base,
    1229. 

  1229.           ...history,
    1230. 

  1230.           status: 'failed',
    1231. 

  1231.           cacheKey: key,
    1232. 

  1232.           alternatives: search.candidates,
    1233. 

  1233.           evidence,
    1234. 

  1234.           reason: `Selector failed: ${String(error)}`,
    1235. 

  1235.         };
    1236. 

  1236.       }
    1237. 

  1237.     }
    1238. 

  1238.     this.save(stage.id, key, {
    1239. 

  1239.       kind: 'chosen',
    1240. 

  1240.       assignment: selected.assignment,
    1241. 

  1241.       alternatives: search.candidates.map((c) => c.assignment),
    1242. 

  1242.       selection,
    1243. 

  1243.       evidence,
    1244. 

  1244.       ...(lookahead
    1245. 

  1245.         ? { continuation: this.continuation(stage, selected, lookahead) }
    1246. 

  1246.         : {}),
    1247. 

  1247.     });
    1248. 

  1248.     return {
    1249. 

  1249.       ...base,
    1250. 

  1250.       ...history,
    1251. 

  1251.       status: 'chosen',
    1252. 

  1252.       cacheKey: key,
    1253. 

  1253.       assignment: selected.assignment,
    1254. 

  1254.       alternatives: search.candidates,
    1255. 

  1255.       selection,
    1256. 

  1256.       evidence,
    1257. 

  1257.       ...(invalidation ? { reason: invalidation } : {}),
    1258. 

  1258.     };
    1259. 

  1259.   }

  1. /** Portable, no Node APIs: consume a build-time validated handoff in React Native or web. */

  9. export interface DesignRuntimePolicy {
    10.

    18. 

  18.   assignment: Values;

  19.   runtimePolicy?: DesignRuntimePolicy;

  54. export function resolveDesignRuntime(
    55. 

  55.   handoff: PortableDesign,
    56. 

  56.   scenario: Values,
    57. 

  57.   options: DesignRuntimeOptions = {},
    58. 

  58. ) {
    59. 

  59.   const keys = Object.keys(handoff.program.scenarios[0]!.values);
    60. 

  60.   if (
    61. 

  61.     keys.length !== Object.keys(scenario).length ||
    62. 

  62.     keys.some((k) => !Object.hasOwn(scenario, k))
    63. 

  63.   )
    64. 

  64.     throw new Error('Runtime scenario keys differ from design program');
    65. 

  65.   if (Object.values(scenario).some((v) => !Number.isFinite(v)))
    66. 

  66.     throw new Error('Runtime scenario inputs must be finite');
    67. 

  67.   const matches = handoff.program.scenarios.filter((s) =>
    68. 

  68.     keys.every((key) => s.values[key] === scenario[key]),
    69. 

  69.   );
    70. 

  70.   const explicit = options.scenarioId;
    71. 

  71.   if (explicit !== undefined && !matches.some((s) => s.id === explicit))
    72. 

  72.     throw new Error(
    73. 

  73.       'Runtime scenarioId must name a scenario with exactly these inputs',
    74. 

  74.     );
    75. 

  75.   const selected = explicit === undefined ? matches[0]?.id : explicit;
    76. 

  76.   const effectiveBands = (
    77. 

  77.     f: PortableDesign['program']['factors'][number],
    78. 

  78.     id?: string,
    79. 

  79.   ) => (id === undefined ? undefined : f.scenarioBands?.[id]) ?? f.bands;
    80. 

  80.   const bandKey = (bands: { min: number; max: number }[]) =>
    81. 

  81.     JSON.stringify([...bands].sort((a, b) => a.min - b.min || a.max - b.max));
    82. 

  82.   const useBase =
    83. 

  83.     !matches.length && handoff.runtimePolicy?.unsampledScenarioBands === 'base';
    84. 

  84.   const unresolvedFactors = handoff.program.factors.filter((f) =>
    85. 

  85.     explicit !== undefined || useBase
    86. 

  86.       ? false
    87. 

  87.       : matches.length
    88. 

  88.         ? matches.some(
    89. 

  89.             (s) =>
    90. 

  90.               bandKey(effectiveBands(f, s.id)) !==
    91. 

  91.               bandKey(effectiveBands(f, selected)),
    92. 

  92.           )
    93. 

  93.         : Object.keys(f.scenarioBands ?? {}).length > 0,
    94. 

  94.   );
    95. 

  95.   const unresolved = new Set(unresolvedFactors.map((f) => f.id));
    96. 

  96.   // Ambiguous named scenarios fail closed, including divergent preference meaning.
    97. 

  97.   // At unsampled inputs only unresolved hard guards prevent geometry admission.
    98. 

  98.   const unresolvedApplicability =
    99. 

  99.     unresolved.size > 0 &&
    100. 

  100.     (matches.length > 1 || unresolvedFactors.some((f) => f.mode === 'hard'));
    101. 

  101.   const values = { ...handoff.assignment, ...scenario };
    102. 

  102.   for (const d of handoff.program.derived)
    103. 

  103.     values[d.id] = evaluateExpression(d.expression, values);
    104. 

  104.   const resolve = (b: Record<string, Expression>) =>
    105. 

  105.     Object.fromEntries(
    106. 

  106.       Object.entries(b).map(([key, e]) => [key, evaluateExpression(e, values)]),
    107. 

  107.     );
    108. 

  108.   const failures = handoff.program.factors
    109. 

  109.     .filter(
    110. 

  110.       (f) =>
    111. 

  111.         f.mode === 'hard' &&
    112. 

  112.         !unresolved.has(f.id) &&
    113. 

  113.         !effectiveBands(f, selected).some((b) => {
    114. 

  114.           const v = evaluateExpression(f.expression, values);
    115. 

  115.           return v >= b.min && v <= b.max;
    116. 

  116.         }),
    117. 

  117.     )
    118. 

  118.     .map((f) => f.id);
    119. 

  119.   const result = {
    120. 

  120.     values,
    121. 

  121.     admissible: failures.length === 0,
    122. 

  122.     failures,
    123. 

  123.     regions: Object.fromEntries(
    124. 

  124.       handoff.machine.states.map((s) => [
    125. 

  125.         s.id,
    126. 

  126.         Object.fromEntries(
    127. 

  127.           s.regions.map((r) => [
    128. 

  128.             r.id,
    129. 

  129.             { role: r.role, ...resolve(r.bindings) },
    130. 

  130.           ]),
    131. 

  131.         ),
    132. 

  132.       ]),
    133. 

  133.     ),
    134. 

  134.     motions: Object.fromEntries(
    135. 

  135.       Object.entries(handoff.machine.motions).map(([id, m]) => [
    136. 

  136.         id,
    137. 

  137.         {
    138. 

  138.           mass: evaluateExpression(m.mass, values),
    139. 

  139.           stiffness: evaluateExpression(m.stiffness, values),
    140. 

  140.           damping: evaluateExpression(m.damping, values),
    141. 

  141.           channel: m.channel,
    142. 

  142.           reducedMotion: m.reducedMotion,
    143. 

  143.         },
    144. 

  144.       ]),
    145. 

  145.     ),
    146. 

  146.   };
    147. 

  147.   for (const [id, m] of Object.entries(result.motions))
    148. 

  148.     if (!(m.mass > 0) || !(m.stiffness > 0) || !(m.damping >= 0))
    149. 

  149.       throw new Error(`Invalid spring parameters for ${id}`);
    150. 

  150.   for (const state of handoff.machine.states)
    151. 

  151.     for (const t of state.on)
    152. 

  152.       if (t.when !== undefined) evaluateExpression(t.when, values);
    153. 

  153.   const expressions = [
    154. 

  154.     ...handoff.program.derived.map((d) => d.expression),
    155. 

  155.     ...handoff.program.factors.map((f) => f.expression),
    156. 

  156.     ...handoff.machine.states.flatMap((s) => [

  157.       ...s.regions.flatMap((r) => Object.values(r.bindings)),
    158. 

  158.       ...s.on.flatMap((t) => (t.when !== undefined ? [t.when] : [])),
    159. 

  159.     ]),
    160. 

  160.     ...Object.values(handoff.machine.motions).flatMap((m) => [
    161. 

  161.       m.mass,
    162. 

  162.       m.stiffness,
    163. 

  163.       m.damping,
    164. 

  164.     ]),
    165. 

  165.   ];
    166. 

  166.   const relevant = new Set(expressions.flatMap(expressionRefs));
    167. 

  167.   const outside = keys.filter(
    168. 

  168.     (k) =>
    169. 

  169.       relevant.has(k) &&
    170. 

  170.       (scenario[k]! <
    171. 

  171.         Math.min(...handoff.program.scenarios.map((s) => s.values[k]!)) ||
    172. 

  172.         scenario[k]! >
    173. 

  173.           Math.max(...handoff.program.scenarios.map((s) => s.values[k]!))),
    174. 

  174.   );
    175. 

  175.   return {
    176. 

  176.     ...result,
    177. 

  177.     runtimeSemantics: DESIGN_RUNTIME_SEMANTICS,
    178. 

  178.     scenarioResolution: {
    179. 

  179.       status: unresolved.size
    180. 

  180.         ? 'unresolved'
    181. 

  181.         : explicit !== undefined
    182. 

  182.           ? 'explicit'
    183. 

  183.           : matches.length
    184. 

  184.             ? 'exact'
    185. 

  185.             : useBase
    186. 

  186.               ? 'explicit-base-policy'
    187. 

  187.               : 'continuous-base',
    188. 

  188.       scenarioId: explicit ?? (matches.length === 1 ? selected! : null),
    189. 

  189.       matchingScenarioIds: matches.map((s) => s.id),
    190. 

  190.       unresolvedFactors: [...unresolved],
    191. 

  191.     },
    192. 

  192.     admissible:
    193. 

  193.       result.admissible && outside.length === 0 && !unresolvedApplicability,
    194. 

  194.     applicability: unresolvedApplicability
    195. 

  195.       ? 'scenario-bands-unresolved'
    196. 

  196.       : outside.length
    197. 

  197.         ? 'outside-declared-envelope'
    198. 

  198.         : 'within-declared-envelope',
    199. 

  199.     outsideEnvelope: outside,
    200. 

  200.     verification: 'pending-renderer-verification' as const,
    201. 

  201.   };
    202.

  129.     if (new Set(state.on.map((x) => x.event)).size !== state.on.length)
    130. 

  130.       throw new Error('Ambiguous event: use one transition per state/event');
    131. 

  131.     for (const region of state.regions)
    132. 

  132.       Object.values(region.bindings).forEach(check);
    133. 

  133.     for (const t of state.on) {
    134. 

  134.       if (!ids.has(t.target) || t.event === 'REFLOW')
    135. 

  135.         throw new Error('Unknown target or reserved REFLOW event');
    136. 

  136.       if (t.motion && !Object.hasOwn(spec.motions, t.motion))
    137. 

  137.         throw new Error(`Unknown motion ${t.motion}`);
    138. 

  138.       if (t.when !== undefined) check(t.when);
    139. 

  139.     }
    140. 

  140.   }
    141. 

  141.   for (const m of Object.values(spec.motions)) {
    142. 

  142.     check(m.mass);
    143. 

  143.     check(m.stiffness);
    144. 

  144.     check(m.damping);
    145. 

  145.   }
    146. 

  146. }
    147. 

  147. function resolveValidated(
    148. 

  148.   program: EnergyProgram,
    149. 

  149.   assignment: Assignment,
    150. 

  150.   machine: Machine,
    151. 

  151.   scenario: Assignment,
    152. 

  152.   options?: DesignRuntimeOptions,
    153. 

  153.   runtimePolicy?: DesignRuntimePolicy,
    154. 

  154. ) {
    155. 

  155.   return resolveDesignRuntime(
    156. 

  156.     {
    157. 

  157.       program,
    158. 

  158.       assignment,
    159. 

  159.       machine,
    160. 

  160.       ...(runtimePolicy ? { runtimePolicy } : {}),
    161. 

  161.     },
    162. 

  162.     scenario,
    163. 

  163.     options,
    164. 

  164.   );
    165. 

  165. }
    166. 

  166. export function compileDesignBindings(
    167. 

  167.   program: EnergyProgram,
    168. 

  168.   machine: z.input<typeof DesignMachineSpecSchema>,
    169. 

  169. ) {
    170. 

  170.   const p = compileEnergy(program).program,
    171. 

  171.     spec = DesignMachineSpecSchema.parse(machine);
    172. 

  172.   validateMachine(spec, p);
    173. 

  173.   return (assignment: Assignment) => {
    174. 

  174.     for (const s of p.scenarios)
    175. 

  175.       resolveValidated(p, assignment, spec, s.values, { scenarioId: s.id });
    176. 

  176.   };
    177. 

  177. }
    178. 

  178. export function createDesignHandoff(input: DesignHandoffInput) {
    179. 

  179.   const r = DesignHandoffSchema.parse(input),
    180. 

  180.     compiled = compileEnergy(r.program),
    181. 

  181.     evaluation = compiled.evaluate(r.assignment),
    182. 

  182.     spec = r.machine;
    183. 

  183.   if (evaluation.hardFailures)
    184. 

  184.     throw new Error('Cannot hand off a mathematically rejected assignment');
    185. 

  185.   validateMachine(spec, compiled.program);
    186. 

  186.   for (const s of compiled.program.scenarios) {
    187. 

  187.     const facts = resolveValidated(
    188. 

  188.       compiled.program,
    189. 

  189.       r.assignment,
    190. 

  190.       spec,
    191. 

  191.       s.values,
    192. 

  192.       { scenarioId: s.id },
    193. 

  193.       r.runtimePolicy,
    194. 

  194.     );
    195. 

  195.     for (const state of spec.states)
    196. 

  196.       for (const t of state.on)
    197. 

  197.         if (t.when !== undefined) evaluateExpression(t.when, facts.values);
    198. 

  198.   }
    199. 

  199.   const body = {
    200. 

  200.     version: 1 as const,
    201. 

  201.     engine: 'design-handoff-v1' as const,
    202. 

  202.     runtimeSemantics: DESIGN_RUNTIME_SEMANTICS,
    203. 

  203.     program: compiled.program,
    204. 

  204.     programIdentity: compiled.identity,
    205. 

  205.     machine: spec,
    206. 

  206.     assignment: r.assignment,
    207. 

  207.     ...(r.runtimePolicy ? { runtimePolicy: r.runtimePolicy } : {}),
    208. 

  208.     decisions: compiled.program.variables.map((v) => ({
    209. 

  209.       variable: v.id,
    210. 

  210.       chosen: r.assignment[v.id]!,
    211. 

  211.       alternatives: v.values.filter((x) => x !== r.assignment[v.id]),
    212. 

  212.       locked: v.locked !== undefined,
    213. 

  213.     })),
    214. 

  214.     verification: {
    215. 

  215.       status: 'pending-renderer-verification' as const,
    216. 

  216.       required: [
    217. 

  217.         'Actual browser/native text and Yoga geometry at supported widths and text scales',
    218. 

  218.         'Semantic accessibility, contrast and reduced-motion behavior',
    219. 

  219.         'Visual/task critique against the product brief',
    220. 

  220.       ],
    221. 

  221.     },
    222. 

  222.     scope:
    223. 

  223.       'Resolved design choice machine. Energy expresses this brief; it does not certify quality, renderer feasibility or an optimum.',
    224. 

  224.   };
    225. 

  225.   return { ...body, identity: hash(body) };
    226. 

  226. }
    227. 

  227. export type DesignHandoff = ReturnType<typeof createDesignHandoff>;
    228. 

  228. export const ReplayHandoffSchema = z
    229. 

  229.   .object({
    230. 

  230.     program: EnergyProgramSchema,
    231. 

  231.     machine: DesignMachineSpecSchema,
    232. 

  232.     recipe: z.unknown(),
    233. 

  233.     scenario: z.record(Id, z.number().finite()),
    234. 

  234.     scenarioId: Id.optional(),
    235. 

  235.     runtimePolicy: RuntimePolicySchema.optional(),
    236. 

  236.   })
    237. 

  237.   .strict();
    238. 

  238. export function replayDesignHandoff(
    239. 

  239.   input: z.input<typeof ReplayHandoffSchema>,
    240. 

  240. ) {
    241. 

  241.   const r = ReplayHandoffSchema.parse(input),
    242. 

  242.     recipe = z
    243. 

  243.       .object({
    244. 

  244.         identity: z.string(),
    245. 

  245.         programIdentity: z.string(),
    246. 

  246.         assignment: z.record(Id, z.number().finite()),
    247. 

  247.       })
    248. 

  248.       .passthrough()
    249. 

  249.       .parse(r.recipe),
    250. 

  250.     compiled = compileEnergy(r.program);
    251. 

  251.   if (compiled.identity !== recipe.programIdentity)
    252. 

  252.     return {
    253. 

  253.       status: 'invalidated' as const,
    254. 

  254.       reason:
    255. 

  255.         'Program, brief, policy, runtime identity, domain or scenario changed',
    256. 

  256.       selectorCalls: 0,
    257. 

  257.     };
    258. 

  258.   const expected = createDesignHandoff({
    259. 

  259.     program: r.program,
    260. 

  260.     machine: r.machine,
    261. 

  261.     assignment: recipe.assignment,
    262. 

  262.     ...(r.runtimePolicy ? { runtimePolicy: r.runtimePolicy } : {}),
    263. 

  263.   });
    264. 

  264.   if (
    265. 

  265.     expected.identity !== recipe.identity ||
    266. 

  266.     hash(expected) !== hash(r.recipe)
    267. 

  267.   )
    268. 

  268.     return {
    269. 

  269.       status: 'invalidated' as const,
    270. 

  270.       reason: 'Machine or recipe changed',
    271. 

  271.       selectorCalls: 0,
    272. 

  272.     };
    273. 

  273.   const resolved = resolveValidated(
    274. 

  274.     expected.program,
    275. 

  275.     expected.assignment,
    276. 

  276.     expected.machine,
    277. 

  277.     r.scenario,
    278. 

  278.     r.scenarioId === undefined ? {} : { scenarioId: r.scenarioId },
    279. 

  279.     expected.runtimePolicy,
    280. 

  280.   );
    281. 

  281.   return {
    282. 

  282.     status: resolved.admissible
    283. 

  283.       ? ('replayed' as const)
    284. 

  284.       : ('requires-search' as const),
    285. 

  285.     handoffIdentity: expected.identity,
    286. 

  286.     resolved,
    287. 

  287.     selectorCalls: 0,
    288. 

  288.     energySearches: 0,
    289. 

  289.     rendererVerification: 'pending' as const,
    290. 

  290.   };
    291. 

  291. }
    292. 

  292. export function resolveHandoff(
    293. 

  293.   handoff: DesignHandoff,
    294. 

  294.   scenario: Assignment,
    295. 

  295.   options?: DesignRuntimeOptions,
    296. 

  296. ) {
    297. 

  297.   const verified = createDesignHandoff({
    298. 

  298.     program: handoff.program,
    299. 

  299.     assignment: handoff.assignment,
    300. 

  300.     machine: handoff.machine,
    301. 

  301.     ...(handoff.runtimePolicy ? { runtimePolicy: handoff.runtimePolicy } : {}),
    302. 

  302.   });
    303. 

  303.   if (verified.identity !== handoff.identity)
    304. 

  304.     throw new Error('Handoff identity mismatch');
    305. 

  305.   return resolveValidated(
    306. 

  306.     verified.program,
    307. 

  307.     verified.assignment,
    308. 

  308.     verified.machine,
    309. 

  309.     scenario,
    310. 

  310.     options,
    311. 

  311.     verified.runtimePolicy,
    312. 

  312.   );
    313. 

  313. }

  314. /** Runtime navigation uses actual XState v5, with branch guards on fresh facts. */

  315. export function createDesignActor(
    316. 

  316.   handoff: DesignHandoff,
    317. 

  317.   scenario: Assignment,
    318. 

  318.   options?: DesignRuntimeOptions,
    319. 

  319. ) {
    320. 

  320.   const verified = structuredClone(
    321. 

  321.     createDesignHandoff({
    322. 

  322.       program: handoff.program,
    323. 

  323.       assignment: handoff.assignment,
    324. 

  324.       machine: handoff.machine,
    325. 

  325.       ...(handoff.runtimePolicy
    326. 

  326.         ? { runtimePolicy: handoff.runtimePolicy }
    327. 

  327.         : {}),
    328. 

  328.     }),
    329. 

  329.   );
    330. 

  330.   if (verified.identity !== handoff.identity)
    331. 

  331.     throw new Error('Handoff identity mismatch');
    332. 

  332.   const initial = resolveValidated(
    333. 

  333.     verified.program,
    334. 

  334.     verified.assignment,
    335. 

  335.     verified.machine,
    336. 

  336.     scenario,
    337. 

  337.     options,
    338. 

  338.     verified.runtimePolicy,
    339. 

  339.   );
    340. 

  340.   if (!initial.admissible)
    341. 

  341.     throw new Error(
    342. 

  342.       'Runtime geometry violates handoff applicability; search/refine before building',
    343. 

  343.     );
    344. 

  344.   const nodeIds = new Map(
    345. 

  345.     verified.machine.states.map((s, i) => [s.id, `design_node_${i}`]),
    346. 

  346.   );
    347. 

  347.   const states = Object.fromEntries(
    348. 

  348.     verified.machine.states.map((s) => [
    349. 

  349.       s.id,
    350. 

  350.       {
    351. 

  351.         id: nodeIds.get(s.id)!,
    352. 

  352.         on: Object.fromEntries([
    353. 

  353.           [
    354. 

  354.             'REFLOW',
    355. 

  355.             {
    356. 

  356.               actions: assign(
    357. 

  357.                 ({
    358. 

  358.                   context,
    359. 

  359.                   event,
    360. 

  360.                 }: {
    361. 

  361.                   context: {
    362. 

  362.                     facts: typeof initial;
    363. 

  363.                     reflow: { status: string; reason: string | null };
    364. 

  364.                   };
    365. 

  365.                   event: {
    366. 

  366.                     type: string;
    367. 

  367.                     scenario?: Assignment;
    368. 

  368.                     scenarioId?: string;
    369. 

  369.                   };
    370. 

  370.                 }) => {
    371. 

  371.                   try {
    372. 

  372.                     if (!event.scenario)
    373. 

  373.                       throw new Error('REFLOW requires scenario');
    374. 

  374.                     const next = resolveValidated(
    375. 

  375.                       verified.program,
    376. 

  376.                       verified.assignment,
    377. 

  377.                       verified.machine,
    378. 

  378.                       event.scenario,
    379. 

  379.                       event.scenarioId === undefined
    380. 

  380.                         ? {}
    381. 

  381.                         : { scenarioId: event.scenarioId },
    382. 

  382.                       verified.runtimePolicy,
    383. 

  383.                     );
    384. 

  384.                     if (!next.admissible)
    385. 

  385.                       return {
    386. 

  386.                         reflow: {
    387. 

  387.                           status: 'requires-search',
    388. 

  388.                           reason:
    389. 

  389.                             next.failures.join(',') ||
    390. 

  390.                             next.scenarioResolution.unresolvedFactors.join(
    391. 

  391.                               ',',
    392. 

  392.                             ) ||
    393. 

  393.                             next.outsideEnvelope.join(','),
    394. 

  394.                         },
    395. 

  395.                       };
    396. 

  396.                     return {
    397. 

  397.                       facts: next,
    398. 

  398.                       reflow: { status: 'resolved', reason: null },
    399. 

  399.                     };
    400. 

  400.                   } catch (error) {
    401. 

  401.                     return {
    402. 

  402.                       reflow: { status: 'rejected', reason: String(error) },
    403. 

  403.                     };
    404. 

  404.                   }
    405. 

  405.                 },
    406. 

  406.               ),
    407. 

  407.             },
    408. 

  408.           ],
    409. 

  409.           ...s.on.map((t) => [
    410. 

  410.             t.event,
    411. 

  411.             {
    412. 

  412.               target: `#${nodeIds.get(t.target)!}`,
    413. 

  413.               ...(t.when !== undefined
    414. 

  414.                 ? {
    415. 

  415.                     guard: ({
    416. 

  416.                       context,
    417. 

  417.                     }: {
    418. 

  418.                       context: { facts: typeof initial };
    419. 

  419.                     }) =>
    420. 

  420.                       evaluateExpression(t.when!, context.facts.values) !== 0,
    421. 

  421.                   }
    422. 

  422.                 : {}),
    423. 

  423.               ...(t.motion ? { description: `motion:${t.motion}` } : {}),
    424. 

  424.             },
    425. 

  425.           ]),
    426. 

  426.         ]),
    427. 

  427.       },
    428. 

  428.     ]),
    429. 

  429.   );
    430. 

  430.   return createActor(
    431. 

  431.     createMachine({
    432. 

  432.       id: verified.machine.id,
    433. 

  433.       initial: verified.machine.initial,
    434. 

  434.       context: {
    435. 

  435.         facts: initial,
    436. 

  436.         reflow: { status: 'resolved', reason: null as string | null },
    437. 

  437.       },
    438. 

  438.       states,
    439. 

  439.     }),
    440. 

  440.   );
    441. 

  441. }
    442.

  119. export function describeValidationPlan(input: unknown) {
