  135. function inspectPhase(
    136. 

  136.   plan: ValidationPlan,
    137. 

  137.   identity: string,
    138. 

  138.   phase: ValidationPlan['phases'][number],
    139. 

  139.   receipts: Receipts,
    140. 

  140. ) {
    141. 

  141.   const requirements = phase.requirements.map((r) => {
    142. 

  142.     const receipt = receipts.find((x) => x.requirement === r.id);
    143. 

  143.     const status = !receipt
    144. 

  144.       ? 'missing'
    145. 

  145.       : receipt.planIdentity !== identity ||
    146. 

  146.           receipt.designIdentity !== plan.designIdentity ||
    147. 

  147.           receipt.inputIdentity !== r.inputIdentity
    148. 

  148.         ? 'stale'
    149. 

  149.         : receipt.tool !== r.tool || receipt.evidenceKind !== r.evidenceKind
    150. 

  150.           ? 'wrong-evidence'
    151. 

  151.           : receipt.outcome === 'unsupported' &&
    152. 

  152.               r.unsupportedPolicy === 'record'
    153. 

  153.             ? 'recorded-unsupported'
    154. 

  154.             : receipt.outcome;
    155. 

  155.     return {
    156. 

  156.       ...r,
    157. 

  157.       status,
    158. 

  158.       resultRef: receipt?.resultRef ?? null,
    159. 

  159.       detail: receipt?.detail ?? null,
    160. 

  160.     };
    161. 

  161.   });
    162. 

  162.   return {
    163. 

  163.     id: phase.id,
    164. 

  164.     purpose: phase.purpose,
    165. 

  165.     ready: requirements.every(
    166. 

  166.       (r) => r.status === 'pass' || r.status === 'recorded-unsupported',
    167. 

  167.     ),
    168. 

  168.     requirements,
    169. 

  169.   };
    170. 

  170. }
    171. 

  171. /** A planning gate around caller-supplied receipts, never a new factual verifier. */
    172. 

  172. export function createValidationActor(input: unknown) {
    173. 

  173.   const request = ValidationCheckSchema.parse(input),
    174. 

  174.     plan = request.plan,
    175. 

  175.     identity = hash(plan);
    176. 

  176.   const known = new Set(
    177. 

  177.     plan.phases.flatMap((p) => p.requirements.map((r) => r.id)),
    178. 

  178.   );
    179. 

  179.   function validateReceipts(receipts: Receipt[]) {
    180. 

  180.     const ids = new Set<string>();
    181. 

  181.     for (const r of receipts) {
    182. 

  182.       if (!known.has(r.requirement))
    183. 

  183.         throw new Error(`Unknown validation requirement ${r.requirement}`);
    184. 

  184.       if (ids.has(r.requirement))
    185. 

  185.         throw new Error(`Duplicate validation receipt ${r.requirement}`);
    186. 

  186.       ids.add(r.requirement);
    187. 

  187.     }
    188. 

  188.     return receipts;
    189. 

  189.   }
    190. 

  190.   validateReceipts(request.receipts);
    191. 

  191.   const immutableReceipts = (receipts: Receipt[]): Receipts =>
    192. 

  192.     Object.freeze(receipts.map((r) => Object.freeze({ ...r })));
    193. 

  193.   const freezeContext = ({ context }: { context: ValidationContext }) => {
    194. 

  194.     Object.freeze(context);
    195. 

  195.   };
    196. 

  196.   const states: Record<string, object> = { complete: { entry: freezeContext } };
    197. 

  197.   for (let i = 0; i < plan.phases.length; i++)
    198. 

  198.     states[`phase${i}`] = {
    199. 

  199.       entry: freezeContext,
    200. 

  200.       on: {
    201. 

  201.         ADVANCE: {
    202. 

  202.           target: i === plan.phases.length - 1 ? 'complete' : `phase${i + 1}`,
    203. 

  203.           guard: ({ context }: { context: ValidationContext }) =>
    204. 

  204.             plan.phases
    205. 

  205.               .slice(0, i + 1)
    206. 

  206.               .every(
    207. 

  207.                 (phase) =>
    208. 

  208.                   inspectPhase(plan, identity, phase, context.receipts).ready,
    209. 

  209.               ),
    210. 

  210.         },
    211. 

  211.       },
    212. 

  212.     };
    213. 

  213.   const machine = createMachine({
    214. 

  214.     types: {} as {
    215. 

  215.       context: ValidationContext;
    216. 

  216.       events: { type: 'ADVANCE' } | { type: 'RECEIPTS'; receipts: Receipt[] };
    217. 

  217.     },
    218. 

  218.     id: plan.id,
    219. 

  219.     initial: 'phase0',
    220. 

  220.     context: {
    221. 

  221.       receipts: immutableReceipts(request.receipts),
    222. 

  222.       receiptError: null,
    223. 

  223.     },
    224. 

  224.     states,
    225. 

  225.     // Receipt updates restart the gate: a later phase cannot keep an old pass
    226. 

  226.     // after an earlier receipt changes or fails.
    227. 

  227.     on: {
    228. 

  228.       RECEIPTS: {
    229. 

  229.         target: '.phase0',
    230. 

  230.         actions: assign(({ event }): ValidationContext => {
    231. 

  231.           try {
    232. 

  232.             return {
    233. 

  233.               receipts: immutableReceipts(
    234. 

  234.                 validateReceipts(
    235. 

  235.                   z
    236. 

  236.                     .array(ValidationReceiptSchema)
    237. 

  237.                     .max(512)
    238. 

  238.                     .parse(event.receipts),
    239. 

  239.                 ),
    240. 

  240.               ),
    241. 

  241.               receiptError: null,
    242. 

  242.             };
    243. 

  243.           } catch (error) {
    244. 

  244.             return {
    245. 

  245.               receipts: immutableReceipts([]),
    246. 

  246.               receiptError:
    247. 

  247.                 error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
    248. 

  248.             };
    249. 

  249.           }
    250. 

  250.         }),
    251. 

  251.       },
    252. 

  252.     },
    253. 

  253.   });
    254. 

  254.   return {
    255. 

  255.     actor: createActor(machine),
    256. 

  256.     plan: structuredClone(plan),
    257. 

  257.     identity,
    258. 

  258.     inspect: (receipts: Receipts) =>
    259. 

  259.       plan.phases.map((p) => inspectPhase(plan, identity, p, receipts)),
    260. 

  260.   };
    261. 

  261. }
    262. 

  262. export function checkValidation(input: unknown) {
    263. 

  263.   const request = ValidationCheckSchema.parse(input),
    264. 

  264.     { actor, plan, identity, inspect } = createValidationActor(request);
    265. 

  265.   actor.start();
    266. 

  266.   try {
    267. 

  267.     for (let i = 0; i < request.advance; i++) actor.send({ type: 'ADVANCE' });
    268. 

  268.     const snapshot = actor.getSnapshot(),
    269. 

  269.       phases = inspect(snapshot.context.receipts);
    270. 

  270.     const current =
    271. 

  271.       snapshot.value === 'complete'
    272. 

  272.         ? null
    273. 

  273.         : phases[Number(String(snapshot.value).replace('phase', ''))]!;
    274. 

  274.     return {
    275. 

  275.       planIdentity: identity,
    276. 

  276.       designIdentity: plan.designIdentity,
    277. 

  277.       state: current?.id ?? 'complete',
    278. 

  278.       complete: snapshot.value === 'complete',
    279. 

  279.       phases,
    280. 

  280.       next:
    281. 

  281.         current?.requirements.filter(
    282. 

  282.           (r) => r.status !== 'pass' && r.status !== 'recorded-unsupported',
    283. 

  283.         ) ?? [],
    284. 

  284.       recordedUnsupported: phases.flatMap((p) =>
    285. 

  285.         p.requirements.filter((r) => r.status === 'recorded-unsupported'),
    286. 

  286.       ),
    287. 

  287.       neuralCalls: 0,
    288. 

  288.       scope:
    289. 

  289.         'XState phase guards require current matching receipt declarations. They do not execute tools, inspect or authenticate receipt files, infer missing permutations, certify aesthetics or replace deterministic validators. Synthetic review cannot satisfy a human-review requirement.',
    290. 

  290.     };
    291. 

  291.   } finally {
    292. 

  292.     actor.stop();
    293. 

  293.   }
    294. 

  294. }

  119. export const OutputBindingsCheckSchema = z.discriminatedUnion('operation', [

  125. function pointer(value: unknown, path: string): unknown {
    126. 

  126.   if ((path !== '' && !path.startsWith('/')) || /~(?![01])/u.test(path))
    127. 

  127.     throw Error('Binding pointer must be an RFC 6901 JSON pointer');
    128. 

  128.   let selected = value;
    129. 

  129.   if (path !== '')
    130. 

  130.     for (const part of path.slice(1).split('/')) {
    131. 

  131.       const key = part.replaceAll('~1', '/').replaceAll('~0', '~');
    132. 

  132.       if (
    133. 

  133.         !selected ||
    134. 

  134.         typeof selected !== 'object' ||
    135. 

  135.         !Object.hasOwn(selected, key)
    136. 

  136.       )
    137. 

  137.         throw Error(`Binding pointer does not exist: ${path}`);
    138. 

  138.       selected = (selected as Record<string, unknown>)[key];
    139. 

  139.     }
    140. 

  140.   return selected;
    141. 

  141. }
    142. 

  142. function unique(values: string[], name: string) {
    143. 

  143.   if (new Set(values).size !== values.length) throw Error(`Duplicate ${name}`);
    144. 

  144. }
    145. 

  145. function validUnit(entry: z.infer<typeof Binding>) {
    146. 

  146.   const property = entry.target.property;
    147. 

  147.   const expected =
    148. 

  148.     property === 'columns'
    149. 

  149.       ? 'count'
    150. 

  150.       : ['color', 'backgroundColor', 'borderColor'].includes(property)
    151. 

  151.         ? 'color'
    152. 

  152.         : ['nativeFinish', 'flexDirection'].includes(property)
    153. 

  153.           ? 'token'
    154. 

  154.           : property.startsWith('spring.')
    155. 

  155.             ? 'spring-parameter'
    156. 

  156.             : property.startsWith('surface.') || property === 'opacity'
    157. 

  157.               ? 'ratio'
    158. 

  158.               : null;
    159. 

  159.   if (expected && entry.unit !== expected)
    160. 

  160.     throw Error(`Wrong unit for ${property}`);
    161. 

  161.   if (
    162. 

  162.     !expected &&
    163. 

  163.     entry.unit !==
    164. 

  164.       ({ web: 'css-px', 'native-ios': 'pt', 'native-android': 'dp' } as const)[
    165. 

  165.         entry.platform
    166. 

  166.       ]
    167. 

  167.   )
    168. 

  168.     throw Error(
    169. 

  169.       `Layout property ${property} requires its platform coordinate unit`,
    170. 

  170.     );
    171. 

  171. }
    172. 

  172. function validEntries(entries: z.infer<typeof ManifestEntry>[]) {
    173. 

  173.   unique(
    174. 

  174.     entries.map((b) => b.id),
    175. 

  175.     'binding IDs',
    176. 

  176.   );
    177. 

  177.   unique(
    178. 

  178.     entries.map(
    179. 

  179.       (b) =>
    180. 

  180.         `${b.scenario}/${b.platform}/${b.target.surface}/${b.target.property}`,
    181. 

  181.     ),
    182. 

  182.     'target properties',
    183. 

  183.   );
    184. 

  184.   for (const b of entries) {
    185. 

  185.     validUnit(b);
    186. 

  186.     if (
    187. 

  187.       (b.pointer !== '' && !b.pointer.startsWith('/')) ||
    188. 

  188.       /~(?![01])/u.test(b.pointer)
    189. 

  189.     )
    190. 

  190.       throw Error('Binding pointer must be an RFC 6901 JSON pointer');
    191. 

  191.     if (typeof b.expected !== 'number' && b.tolerance !== 0)
    192. 

  192.       throw Error(`Tolerance requires numeric value: ${b.id}`);
    193. 

  193.     if (
    194. 

  194.       ['css-px', 'pt', 'dp', 'ratio', 'count', 'spring-parameter'].includes(
    195. 

  195.         b.unit,
    196. 

  196.       ) &&
    197. 

  197.       typeof b.expected !== 'number'
    198. 

  198.     )
    199. 

  199.       throw Error(`Numeric unit requires numeric value: ${b.id}`);
    200. 

  200.     if (['color', 'token'].includes(b.unit) && typeof b.expected !== 'string')
    201. 

  201.       throw Error(`String unit requires string value: ${b.id}`);
    202. 

  202.   }
    203. 

  203. }

  204. export function resolveOutputBindings(
    205. 

  205.   input: z.input<typeof OutputBindingsResolveSchema>,
    206. 

  206. ) {
    207. 

  207.   const r = OutputBindingsResolveSchema.parse(input);
    208. 

  208.   if (
    209. 

  209.     hash(r.source.value) !== r.source.identity ||
    210. 

  210.     r.current.resultIdentity !== r.source.identity
    211. 

  211.   )
    212. 

  212.     throw Error('Binding source payload identity mismatch');
    213. 

  213.   const entries = r.bindings.map((b) => ({
    214. 

  214.     ...b,
    215. 

  215.     expected: Value.parse(pointer(r.source.value, b.pointer)),
    216. 

  216.   }));
    217. 

  217.   validEntries(entries);
    218. 

  218.   const body = {
    219. 

  219.     version: 1 as const,
    220. 

  220.     engine: 'output-bindings-v1' as const,
    221. 

  221.     current: r.current,
    222. 

  222.     sourceValueIdentity: r.source.identity,
    223. 

  223.     entries,
    224. 

  224.     scope:
    225. 

  225.       'Expected values projected from exact source pointers. No renderer ran; hashes detect payload changes, not authenticity or causal consumption.',
    226. 

  226.   };
    227. 

  227.   return { ...body, identity: hash(body) };
    228. 

  228. }
    229. 

  229. export function checkOutputBindings(
    230. 

  230.   input: z.input<typeof OutputBindingsCheckSchema>,
    231. 

  231. ) {
    232. 

  232.   const r = OutputBindingsCheckSchema.parse(input);
    233. 

  233.   if (r.operation === 'resolve') return resolveOutputBindings(r);
    234. 

  234.   const { identity, ...body } = r.manifest;
    235. 

  235.   if (hash(body) !== identity)
    236. 

  236.     throw Error('Binding manifest identity mismatch');
    237. 

  237.   if (r.manifest.sourceValueIdentity !== r.manifest.current.resultIdentity)
    238. 

  238.     throw Error('Binding manifest source payload identity mismatch');
    239. 

  239.   validEntries(r.manifest.entries);
    240. 

  240.   unique(
    241. 

  241.     r.observations.map((o) => o.binding),
    242. 

  242.     'observations',
    243. 

  243.   );
    244. 

  244.   const known = new Set(r.manifest.entries.map((e) => e.id));
    245. 

  245.   for (const o of r.observations)
    246. 

  246.     if (!known.has(o.binding))
    247. 

  247.       throw Error(`Observation has no binding: ${o.binding}`);
    248. 

  248.   const staleManifest = hash(r.current) !== hash(r.manifest.current);
    249. 

  249.   const entries = r.manifest.entries.map((b) => {
    250. 

  250.     const o = r.observations.find((x) => x.binding === b.id);
    251. 

  251.     let status:
    252. 

  252.       | 'pass'
    253. 

  253.       | 'stale'
    254. 

  254.       | 'unbound'
    255. 

  255.       | 'wrong-evidence'
    256. 

  256.       | 'override'
    257. 

  257.       | 'unknown'
    258. 

  258.       | 'unsupported'
    259. 

  259.       | 'recorded-unsupported';
    260. 

  260.     if (staleManifest) status = 'stale';
    261. 

  261.     else if (!o) status = 'unbound';
    262. 

  262.     else if (
    263. 

  263.       hash(o.current) !== hash(r.current) ||
    264. 

  264.       o.scenarioIdentity !== b.scenarioIdentity
    265. 

  265.     )
    266. 

  266.       status = 'stale';
    267. 

  267.     else if (
    268. 

  268.       o.scenario !== b.scenario ||
    269. 

  269.       o.platform !== b.platform ||
    270. 

  270.       o.unit !== b.unit ||
    271. 

  271.       o.surface !== b.target.surface ||
    272. 

  272.       o.property !== b.target.property ||
    273. 

  273.       o.sourceLocation !== b.target.sourceLocation ||
    274. 

  274.       o.provider !== (b.platform === 'web' ? 'browser' : b.platform)
    275. 

  275.     )
    276. 

  276.       status = 'wrong-evidence';
    277. 

  277.     else if (
    278. 

  278.       o.status === 'unknown' ||
    279. 

  279.       (o.status === 'observed' && o.value === undefined)
    280. 

  280.     )
    281. 

  281.       status = 'unknown';
    282. 

  282.     else if (o.status === 'unsupported')
    283. 

  283.       status =
    284. 

  284.         b.unsupportedPolicy === 'record'
    285. 

  285.           ? 'recorded-unsupported'
    286. 

  286.           : 'unsupported';
    287. 

  287.     else
    288. 

  288.       status = (
    289. 

  289.         typeof b.expected === 'number' && typeof o.value === 'number'
    290. 

  290.           ? Math.abs(o.value - b.expected) <= b.tolerance
    291. 

  291.           : o.value === b.expected
    292. 

  292.       )
    293. 

  293.         ? 'pass'
    294. 

  294.         : 'override';
    295. 

  295.     return {
    296. 

  296.       id: b.id,
    297. 

  297.       status,
    298. 

  298.       expected: b.expected,
    299. 

  299.       observed: o?.value ?? null,
    300. 

  300.       unit: b.unit,
    301. 

  301.       target: b.target,
    302. 

  302.       resultRef: o?.resultRef ?? null,
    303. 

  303.       detail: o?.detail ?? 'No current renderer observation',
    304. 

  304.     };
    305. 

  305.   });
    306. 

  306.   return {
    307. 

  307.     version: 1 as const,
    308. 

  308.     manifestIdentity: identity,
    309. 

  309.     current: r.current,
    310. 

  310.     complete: entries.every(
    311. 

  311.       (e) => e.status === 'pass' || e.status === 'recorded-unsupported',
    312. 

  312.     ),
    313. 

  313.     verified: entries.every((e) => e.status === 'pass'),
    314. 

  314.     entries,
    315. 

  315.     scope:
    316. 

  316.       'Compares caller-supplied current renderer observations with consumed-value expectations. Matching values support consistency, not authorship, causal influence, task success or whole-app accessibility.',
    317. 

  317.   };
    318. 

  318. }
    319.

  137. export const RelationalKernelCompileSchema = z
    138. 

  138.   .object({
    139. 

  139.     version: z.literal(1),
    140. 

  140.     id: Id,

  146.       .min(1)

  147.       .max(8),

  150. export type RelationalKernelInput = z.input<
    151. 

  151.   typeof RelationalKernelCompileSchema

  152. >;

  153. type Request = z.infer<typeof RelationalKernelCompileSchema>;
    154. 

  154. type Kernel = Request['kernels'][number];

  155. const ref = (name: string): Expression => ({ ref: name });
    156. 

  156. const op = (
    157. 

  157.   name: Exclude<Expression, number | { ref: string }>['op'],
    158. 

  158.   ...args: Expression[]
    159. 

  159. ): Expression => ({ op: name, args });
    160. 

  160. const sum = (xs: Expression[]): Expression =>
    161. 

  161.   xs.length === 1 ? xs[0]! : op('add', ...xs);
    162. 

  162. const lookup = (key: string, values: number[]): Expression =>
    163. 

  163.   values
    164. 

  164.     .slice(0, -1)
    165. 

  165.     .reduceRight<Expression>(
    166. 

  166.       (tail, value, i) =>
    167. 

  167.         op('if', op('lt', op('abs', op('sub', ref(key), i)), 0.5), value, tail),
    168. 

  168.       values.at(-1)!,
    169. 

  169.     );
    170. 

  170. const unique = (ids: string[], label: string) => {
    171. 

  171.   if (new Set(ids).size !== ids.length) throw Error(`Duplicate ${label}`);
    172. 

  172. };

  185. interface Output {

  192. export function compileRelationalKernels(input: RelationalKernelInput) {
    193. 

  193.   const r = RelationalKernelCompileSchema.parse(input);
    194. 

  194.   unique(
    195. 

  195.     r.kernels.map((k) => k.id),
    196. 

  196.     'kernel IDs',
    197. 

  197.   );
    198. 

  198.   unique(
    199. 

  199.     r.scenarios.map((s) => s.id),
    200. 

  200.     'scenario IDs',
    201. 

  201.   );
    202. 

  202.   for (const s of r.scenarios)
    203. 

  203.     if (
    204. 

  204.       (s.platform === 'web' && s.unit !== 'css-px') ||
    205. 

  205.       (s.platform === 'native-ios' && s.unit !== 'pt') ||
    206. 

  206.       (s.platform === 'native-android' && s.unit !== 'dp')
    207. 

  207.     )
    208. 

  208.       throw Error(`Platform/unit mismatch for ${s.id}`);
    209. 

  209.   const outputs: Output[] = [],
    210. 

  210.     evidence: Record<string, unknown> = {},
    211. 

  211.     obligations: Array<{
    212. 

  212.       kernel: string;
    213. 

  213.       scenario: string;
    214. 

  214.       platform: string;
    215. 

  215.       checks: string[];
    216. 

  216.     }> = [];
    217. 

  217.   const stages = r.kernels.map((k) => {
    218. 

  218.     unique(
    219. 

  219.       k.candidates.map((c) => c.id),
    220. 

  220.       `${k.id} candidate IDs`,
    221. 

  221.     );
    222. 

  222.     const provenance = { kind: 'brief' as const, source: k.source };
    223. 

  223.     const program: EnergyProgram = {
    224. 

  224.       version: 1,
    225. 

  225.       id: k.id,
    226. 

  226.       brief: { intent: k.purpose, source: k.source },
    227. 

  227.       identity: { ...r.identities, kernel: hash(k), brief: hash(r.brief) },
    228. 

  228.       variables: [
    229. 

  229.         {
    230. 

  230.           id: 'choice',
    231. 

  231.           unit: 'candidate-index',
    232. 

  232.           label: k.purpose.slice(0, 120),
    233. 

  233.           values: k.candidates.map((_, i) => i),
    234. 

  234.           valueAliases: k.candidates.map((c, i) => ({
    235. 

  235.             value: i,
    236. 

  236.             label: c.label,
    237. 

  237.           })),
    238. 

  238.         },
    239. 

  239.       ],
    240. 

  240.       scenarios: r.scenarios.map((s) => ({
    241. 

  241.         id: s.id,
    242. 

  242.         values: { width: s.width, height: s.height, textScale: s.textScale },
    243. 

  243.       })),
    244. 

  244.       derived: [],
    245. 

  245.       factors: [],
    246. 

  246.     };
    247. 

  247.     const add = (name: string, expression: Expression, unit: string) => {
    248. 

  248.       program.derived.push({
    249. 

  249.         id: name,
    250. 

  250.         expression,
    251. 

  251.         unit,
    252. 

  252.         provenance: {
    253. 

  253.           kind: 'calculated',
    254. 

  254.           source: `relational-kernels-v1:${k.kind}`,
    255. 

  255.         },
    256. 

  256.       });
    257. 

  257.       outputs.push({ stage: k.id, name, expression: ref(name), unit });
    258. 

  258.     };
    259. 

  259.     const factor = (
    260. 

  260.       id: string,
    261. 

  261.       expression: Expression,
    262. 

  262.       min: number,
    263. 

  263.       max: number,
    264. 

  264.       mode: 'hard' | 'preference',
    265. 

  265.       scale = 1,
    266. 

  266.     ) =>
    267. 

  267.       program.factors.push({
    268. 

  268.         id,
    269. 

  269.         expression,
    270. 

  270.         bands: [{ min, max }],
    271. 

  271.         mode,
    272. 

  272.         scale,
    273. 

  273.         weight: 1,
    274. 

  274.         category: k.kind,
    275. 

  275.         provenance,
    276. 

  276.       });
    277. 

  277.     const dependencies: string[] = [],
    278. 

  278.       bindings: Array<{ input: string; stage: string; variable: string }> = [];
    279. 

  279.     if (k.kind === 'type-row') {
    280. 

  280.       for (const c of k.candidates) {
    281. 

  281.         unique(
    282. 

  282.           c.cells.map((x) => x.scenario),
    283. 

  283.           `${k.id}/${c.id} cells`,
    284. 

  284.         );
    285. 

  285.         if (c.cells.length !== r.scenarios.length)
    286. 

  286.           throw Error(`Missing text scenario for ${k.id}/${c.id}`);
    287. 

  287.       }
    288. 

  288.       for (const s of r.scenarios) {
    289. 

  289.         const values = program.scenarios.find((x) => x.id === s.id)!.values;
    290. 

  290.         k.candidates.forEach((c, i) => {
    291. 

  291.           const cell = c.cells.find((x) => x.scenario === s.id);
    292. 

  292.           if (!cell) throw Error(`Missing text cell ${s.id}`);
    293. 

  293.           if (cell.sourceIdentity !== r.identities.source)
    294. 

  294.             throw Error(`Stale text source for ${k.id}/${c.id}`);
    295. 

  295.           if (cell.provider !== (s.platform === 'web' ? 'browser' : s.platform))
    296. 

  296.             throw Error(`Wrong renderer for ${s.id}`);
    297. 

  297.           if (
    298. 

  298.             cell.inputIdentity !==
    299. 

  299.             kernelTextMeasurementIdentity({
    300. 

  300.               text: k.text,
    301. 

  301.               fontIdentity: k.fontIdentity,
    302. 

  302.               fontSize: c.fontSize,
    303. 

  303.               lineHeight: c.lineHeight,
    304. 

  304.               trackingEm: c.trackingEm,
    305. 

  305.               width: cell.width,
    306. 

  306.               scenario: s,
    307. 

  307.             })
    308. 

  308.           )
    309. 

  309.             throw Error(`Stale text parameters for ${k.id}/${c.id}/${s.id}`);
    310. 

  310.           Object.assign(values, {
    311. 

  311.             [`cellHeight${i}`]: cell.height,
    312. 

  312.             [`cellWidth${i}`]: cell.width,
    313. 

  313.             [`cellOverflow${i}`]: Math.max(cell.overflowX, cell.overflowY),
    314. 

  314.           });
    315. 

  315.         });
    316. 

  316.       }
    317. 

  317.       program.variables.push({
    318. 

  318.         id: 'paddingBlock',
    319. 

  319.         unit: 'layout-unit',
    320. 

  320.         values: k.paddingBlock,
    321. 

  321.       });
    322. 

  322.       const chooseCell = (field: string) =>
    323. 

  323.         k.candidates
    324. 

  324.           .slice(0, -1)
    325. 

  325.           .reduceRight<Expression>(
    326. 

  326.             (tail, _, i) =>
    327. 

  327.               op(
    328. 

  328.                 'if',
    329. 

  329.                 op('lt', op('abs', op('sub', ref('choice'), i)), 0.5),
    330. 

  330.                 ref(`${field}${i}`),
    331. 

  331.                 tail,
    332. 

  332.               ),
    333. 

  333.             ref(`${field}${k.candidates.length - 1}`),
    334. 

  334.           );
    335. 

  335.       add(
    336. 

  336.         'fontSize',
    337. 

  337.         op(
    338. 

  338.           'mul',
    339. 

  339.           lookup(
    340. 

  340.             'choice',
    341. 

  341.             k.candidates.map((c) => c.fontSize),
    342. 

  342.           ),
    343. 

  343.           ref('textScale'),
    344. 

  344.         ),
    345. 

  345.         'layout-unit',
    346. 

  346.       );
    347. 

  347.       add(
    348. 

  348.         'lineHeightRatio',
    349. 

  349.         lookup(
    350. 

  350.           'choice',
    351. 

  351.           k.candidates.map((c) => c.lineHeight),
    352. 

  352.         ),
    353. 

  353.         'ratio',
    354. 

  354.       );
    355. 

  355.       add(
    356. 

  356.         'lineHeight',
    357. 

  357.         op('mul', ref('fontSize'), ref('lineHeightRatio')),
    358. 

  358.         'layout-unit',
    359. 

  359.       );
    360. 

  360.       add(
    361. 

  361.         'letterSpacing',
    362. 

  362.         op(
    363. 

  363.           'mul',
    364. 

  364.           ref('fontSize'),
    365. 

  365.           lookup(
    366. 

  366.             'choice',
    367. 

  367.             k.candidates.map((c) => c.trackingEm),
    368. 

  368.           ),
    369. 

  369.         ),
    370. 

  370.         'layout-unit',
    371. 

  371.       );
    372. 

  372.       add('textWidth', chooseCell('cellWidth'), 'layout-unit');
    373. 

  373.       add(
    374. 

  374.         'rowHeight',
    375. 

  375.         op(
    376. 

  376.           'max',
    377. 

  377.           k.minHitSize,
    378. 

  378.           op(
    379. 

  379.             'add',
    380. 

  380.             chooseCell('cellHeight'),
    381. 

  381.             op('mul', ref('paddingBlock'), 2),
    382. 

  382.           ),
    383. 

  383.         ),
    384. 

  384.         'layout-unit',
    385. 

  385.       );
    386. 

  386.       outputs.push({
    387. 

  387.         stage: k.id,
    388. 

  388.         name: 'paddingBlock',
    389. 

  389.         unit: 'layout-unit',
    390. 

  390.         expression: ref('paddingBlock'),
    391. 

  391.       });
    392. 

  392.       factor('text-overflow', chooseCell('cellOverflow'), 0, 0.75, 'hard');
    393. 

  393.       factor('row-maximum', ref('rowHeight'), 0, k.maxRowHeight, 'hard');
    394. 

  394.       factor(
    395. 

  395.         'row-density',
    396. 

  396.         ref('rowHeight'),
    397. 

  397.         k.minHitSize,
    398. 

  398.         k.preferredRowHeight,
    399. 

  399.         'preference',
    400. 

  400.         Math.max(1, k.preferredRowHeight - k.minHitSize),
    401. 

  401.       );
    402. 

  402.       evidence[k.id] = {
    403. 

  403.         kind: 'adapted-renderer-observations',
    404. 

  404.         cells: k.candidates.flatMap((c) => c.cells),
    405. 

  405.         fontIdentity: k.fontIdentity,
    406. 

  406.       };
    407. 

  407.     } else if (k.kind === 'regions') {
    408. 

  408.       unique(
    409. 

  409.         k.regions.map((x) => x.id),
    410. 

  410.         `${k.id} regions`,
    411. 

  411.       );
    412. 

  412.       let linked: Extract<Kernel, { kind: 'type-row' }> | undefined;
    413. 

  413.       if (k.roleLink) {
    414.

  729. export type CompiledRelationalKernels = ReturnType<

    732. 

  732. /** Convert a real workflow result into importable named token values; revalidate assignments, never search. */
    733. 

  733. export function resolveRelationalKernelOutputs(
    734. 

  734.   compiled: CompiledRelationalKernels,
    735. 

  735.   result: {
    736. 

  736.     stages: Record<
    737. 

  737.       string,
    738. 

  738.       {
    739. 

  739.         status: string;
    740. 

  740.         assignment: Record<string, number> | null;
    741. 

  741.         evidence: unknown;
    742. 

  742.       }
    743. 

  743.     >;
    744. 

  744.   },
    745. 

  745. ) {
    746. 

  746.   const { identity, ...body } = compiled;
    747. 

  747.   if (hash(body) !== identity)
    748. 

  748.     throw Error('Kernel compilation identity mismatch');
    749. 

  749.   const resultIdentity = hash(result),
    750. 

  750.     values: Record<string, Record<string, string | number>> = {};
    751. 

  751.   for (const stage of compiled.workflow.stages) {
    752. 

  752.     const selected = result.stages[stage.id];
    753. 

  753.     if (
    754. 

  754.       !selected?.assignment ||
    755. 

  755.       !['chosen', 'replayed'].includes(selected.status)
    756. 

  756.     )
    757. 

  757.       throw Error(`Unresolved kernel ${stage.id}`);
    758. 

  758.     const program = structuredClone(stage.program);
    759. 

  759.     for (const b of stage.bindings) {
    760. 

  760.       const parent = result.stages[b.stage]?.assignment;
    761. 

  761.       if (!parent || parent[b.variable] === undefined)
    762. 

  762.         throw Error(`Missing predecessor ${b.stage}`);
    763. 

  763.       for (const scenario of program.scenarios)
    764. 

  764.         scenario.values[b.input] = parent[b.variable]!;
    765. 

  765.     }
    766. 

  766.     const evaluator = compileEnergy(program),
    767. 

  767.       evaluation = evaluator.evaluate(selected.assignment);
    768. 

  768.     const evidence = z
    769. 

  769.       .object({ programIdentity: Digest })
    770. 

  770.       .passthrough()
    771. 

  771.       .parse(selected.evidence);
    772. 

  772.     if (evidence.programIdentity !== evaluator.identity)
    773. 

  773.       throw Error(`Stale workflow source for ${stage.id}`);
    774. 

  774.     if (evaluation.hardFailures)
    775. 

  775.       throw Error(`Selected kernel ${stage.id} violates hard constraints`);
    776. 

  776.     for (const scenario of evaluator.program.scenarios) {
    777. 

  777.       const context = {
    778. 

  778.           ...selected.assignment,
    779. 

  779.           ...scenario.values,
    780. 

  780.           ...evaluation.derived[scenario.id],
    781. 

  781.         },
    782. 

  782.         target = (values[scenario.id] ??= {});
    783. 

  783.       for (const output of compiled.outputs.filter((o) => o.stage === stage.id))
    784. 

  784.         target[`${stage.id}.${output.name}`] =
    785. 

  785.           output.expression === null
    786. 

  786.             ? output.candidateValues![selected.assignment.choice!]!
    787. 

  787.             : evaluateExpression(output.expression, context);
    788. 

  788.     }
    789. 

  789.   }
    790. 

  790.   return {
    791. 

  791.     version: 1 as const,
    792. 

  792.     compilationIdentity: identity,
    793. 

  793.     inputIdentity: compiled.inputIdentity,
    794. 

  794.     identities: compiled.input.identities,
    795. 

  795.     resultIdentity,
    796. 

  796.     scenarioIdentities: Object.fromEntries(
    797. 

  797.       compiled.input.scenarios.map((s) => [s.id, hash(s)]),
    798. 

  798.     ),
    799. 

  799.     values,
    800. 

  800.     obligations: compiled.obligations,
    801. 

  801.     verification: 'pending-current-renderer' as const,
    802. 

  802.   };
    803. 

  803. }

  57.     title: z.string().min(1).max(200),
    58. 

  58.     body: z.string().min(1).max(1000),
    59. 

  59.     action: z.string().min(1).max(80),
    60. 

  60.     titleFont: Font,
    61. 

  61.     bodyFont: Font,
    62. 

  62.     image: z
    63. 

  63.       .object({
    64. 

  64.         file: z.string().min(1).max(4096),
    65. 

  65.         source: z.string().min(1).max(2000),
    66. 

  66.         focalX: finite.min(0).max(1),
    67. 

  67.         focalY: finite.min(0).max(1),
    68. 

  68.       })
    69. 

  69.       .strict(),
    70. 

  70.     targets: z
    71. 

  71.       .object({
    72. 

  72.         hierarchy: band,
    73. 

  73.         gapRatio: band,
    74. 

  74.         tension: band,
    75. 

  75.         imageShare: band,
    76. 

  76.         roughnessContrast: band,
    77. 

  77.         motionRole: band,
    78. 

  78.         imageToTextHeight: band,
    79. 

  79.         imageToTextHeightByScenario: z
    80. 

  80.           .partialRecord(
    81. 

  81.             z.enum([
    82. 

  82.               'phone',
    83. 

  83.               'phone-large',
    84. 

  84.               'ipad',
    85. 

  85.               'ipad-large',
    86. 

  86.               'desktop',
    87. 

  87.               'desktop-large',
    88. 

  88.             ]),
    89. 

  89.             band,
    90. 

  90.           )
    91. 

  91.           .optional(),
    92. 

  92.       })
    93. 

  93.       .strict(),
    94. 

  94.     weights: z
    95. 

  95.       .object({
    96. 

  96.         hierarchy: finite.min(0).max(20),
    97. 

  97.         reading: finite.min(0).max(20),
    98. 

  98.         density: finite.min(0).max(20),
    99. 

  99.         tension: finite.min(0).max(20),
    100. 

  100.         image: finite.min(0).max(20),
    101. 

  101.         material: finite.min(0).max(20),
    102. 

  102.         motion: finite.min(0).max(20),
    103. 

  103.       })
    104. 

  104.       .strict(),
    105. 

  105.   })
    106. 

  106.   .strict();
    107. 

  107. export type DesignFieldBrief = z.infer<typeof DesignFieldBriefSchema>;
    108. 

  108. export const DESIGN_FIELD_DOMAINS = {
    109. 

  109.   titleSize: [52, 64, 76, 88, 100],
    110. 

  110.   bodySize: [16, 18, 20],
    111. 

  111.   contentMax: [900, 1080, 1260],
    112. 

  112.   gap: [20, 32, 44, 56],
    113. 

  113.   leading: [1.35, 1.5, 1.65],
    114. 

  114.   imageAspect: [1.1, 1.4, 1.7, 2],
    115. 

  115.   imageShare: [0.48, 0.64, 0.8],
    116. 

  116.   roughness: [0.18, 0.35, 0.55, 0.75],
    117. 

  117.   motionRole: [0, 1, 2],
    118. 

  118.   counterRoughness: [0.55, 0.7, 0.85],
    119. 

  119. } as const;
    120. 

  120. export const DESIGN_FIELD_SCENARIOS = [
    121. 

  121.   { id: 'phone', width: 390, height: 844, textScale: 1 },
    122. 

  122.   { id: 'phone-large', width: 390, height: 844, textScale: 2 },
    123. 

  123.   { id: 'ipad', width: 834, height: 1194, textScale: 1 },
    124. 

  124.   { id: 'ipad-large', width: 834, height: 1194, textScale: 2 },
    125. 

  125.   { id: 'desktop', width: 1440, height: 1000, textScale: 1 },
    126. 

  126.   { id: 'desktop-large', width: 1440, height: 1000, textScale: 2 },
    127. 

  127. ] as const;
    128. 

  128. const padding = (width: number) => (width < 600 ? 20 : 36);
    129. 

  129. const round = (n: number) => Math.round(n * 10000) / 10000;
    130. 

  130. const measurementPolicy =
    131. 

  131.   'design-field-text-v2: LTR plain text; declared leading/tracking/axes; title min(size,width*.135)*textScale; body min(contentMax*.55,available,680); natural height';
    132. 

  132. export function designFieldMeasurementRequests(
    133. 

  133.   brief: Pick<DesignFieldBrief, 'typography'>,
    134. 

  134. ) {
    135. 

  135.   return DESIGN_FIELD_SCENARIOS.flatMap((s) => {
    136. 

  136.     const base = {
    137. 

  137.       scenario: s.id,
    138. 

  138.       viewportWidth: s.width,
    139. 

  139.       viewportHeight: s.height,
    140. 

  140.       textScale: s.textScale,
    141. 

  141.     };
    142. 

  142.     return [
    143. 

  143.       ...DESIGN_FIELD_DOMAINS.titleSize.flatMap((size) =>
    144. 

  144.         DESIGN_FIELD_DOMAINS.contentMax.map((column) => ({
    145. 

  145.           ...base,
    146. 

  146.           key: `t${size}c${column}`,
    147. 

  147.           kind: 'title' as const,
    148. 

  148.           size,
    149. 

  149.           column,
    150. 

  150.           leading: brief.typography.titleLeading,
    151. 

  151.           effectiveSize: Math.min(size, s.width * 0.135) * s.textScale,
    152. 

  152.           measure: Math.min(column, s.width - 2 * padding(s.width)),
    153. 

  153.         })),
    154. 

  154.       ),
    155. 

  155.       ...DESIGN_FIELD_DOMAINS.bodySize.flatMap((size) =>
    156. 

  156.         DESIGN_FIELD_DOMAINS.contentMax.flatMap((column) =>
    157. 

  157.           DESIGN_FIELD_DOMAINS.leading.map((leading) => ({
    158. 

  158.             ...base,
    159. 

  159.             key: `b${size}c${column}l${String(leading).replace('.', '_')}`,
    160. 

  160.             kind: 'body' as const,
    161. 

  161.             size,
    162. 

  162.             column,
    163. 

  163.             leading,
    164. 

  164.             effectiveSize: size * s.textScale,
    165. 

  165.             measure: Math.min(
    166. 

  166.               column * 0.55,
    167. 

  167.               s.width - 2 * padding(s.width),
    168. 

  168.               680,
    169. 

  169.             ),
    170. 

  170.           })),
    171. 

  171.         ),
    172. 

  172.       ),
    173. 

  173.     ];
    174. 

  174.   });
    175. 

  175. }
    176. 

  176. const Row = z
    177. 

  177.   .object({
    178. 

  178.     scenario: z.string(),
    179. 

  179.     key: z.string(),
    180. 

  180.     height: finite.nonnegative(),
    181. 

  181.     width: finite.positive(),
    182. 

  182.     lines: z.number().int().min(1),
    183. 

  183.     overflowX: finite.nonnegative(),
    184. 

  184.     fontLoaded: z.boolean(),
    185. 

  185.   })
    186. 

  186.   .strict();
    187. 

  187. export const DesignFieldMeasurementsSchema = z
    188. 

  188.   .object({
    189. 

  189.     version: z.literal(1),
    190. 

  190.     sourceIdentity: Digest,
    191. 

  191.     identity: Digest,
    192. 

  192.     policy: z.literal(measurementPolicy),
    193. 

  193.     provider: z.enum(['chromium', 'test-fixture']),
    194. 

  194.     runtime: z.string().min(1),
    195. 

  195.     sources: z
    196. 

  196.       .object({
    197. 

  197.         titleFont: Digest,
    198. 

  198.         bodyFont: Digest,
    199. 

  199.         image: Digest,
    200. 

  200.         briefSource: Digest,
    201. 

  201.         content: Digest,
    202. 

  202.       })
    203. 

  203.       .strict(),
    204. 

  204.     rows: z.array(Row).min(1).max(1200),
    205. 

  205.     image: z
    206. 

  206.       .object({
    207. 

  207.         width: z.number().int().positive().max(8192),
    208. 

  208.         height: z.number().int().positive().max(8192),
    209. 

  209.         meanLuminance: finite.min(0).max(1),
    210. 

  210.         luminanceStdDev: finite.min(0).max(1),
    211. 

  211.         sampleCount: z.number().int().min(1).max(4096),
    212. 

  212.       })
    213. 

  213.       .strict(),
    214. 

  214.   })
    215. 

  215.   .strict();
    216. 

  216. export type DesignFieldMeasurements = z.infer<
    217. 

  217.   typeof DesignFieldMeasurementsSchema
    218. 

  218. >;
    219. 

  219. const digest = (bytes: Uint8Array) =>
    220. 

  220.   createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');
    221. 

  221. async function sourcesFor(brief: DesignFieldBrief) {
    222. 

  222.   const [titleFont, bodyFont, image, briefSource] = await Promise.all(
    223. 

  223.     [
    224. 

  224.       brief.titleFont.file,
    225. 

  225.       brief.bodyFont.file,
    226. 

  226.       brief.image.file,
    227. 

  227.       brief.source,
    228. 

  228.     ].map(async (file) => {
    229. 

  229.       const b = await readFile(file);
    230. 

  230.       if (b.length > 8 * 1024 * 1024)
    231. 

  231.         throw new Error('Design field source exceeds8MiB');
    232. 

  232.       return b;
    233. 

  233.     }),
    234. 

  234.   );
    235. 

  235.   return {
    236. 

  236.     bytes: { titleFont: titleFont!, bodyFont: bodyFont!, image: image! },
    237. 

  237.     sources: {
    238. 

  238.       titleFont: digest(titleFont!),
    239. 

  239.       bodyFont: digest(bodyFont!),
    240. 

  240.       image: digest(image!),
    241. 

  241.       briefSource: digest(briefSource!),
    242. 

  242.       content: hash({
    243. 

  243.         title: brief.title,
    244. 

  244.         body: brief.body,
    245. 

  245.         action: brief.action,
    246. 

  246.       }),
    247. 

  247.     },
    248. 

  248.   };
    249. 

  249. }
    250. 

  250. const sourceIdentity = (
    251. 

  251.   brief: DesignFieldBrief,
    252. 

  252.   sources: DesignFieldMeasurements['sources'],
    253. 

  253. ) =>
    254. 

  254.   hash({
    255. 

  255.     content: sources.content,
    256. 

  256.     titleFont: sources.titleFont,
    257. 

  257.     bodyFont: sources.bodyFont,
    258. 

  258.     image: sources.image,
    259. 

  259.     titleFace: brief.titleFont,
    260. 

  260.     bodyFace: brief.bodyFont,
    261. 

  261.     typography: brief.typography,
    262. 

  262.     policy: measurementPolicy,
    263. 

  263.     scenarios: DESIGN_FIELD_SCENARIOS,
    264. 

  264.     domains: DESIGN_FIELD_DOMAINS,
    265. 

  265.   });
    266. 

  266. /** Real DOM measurements for every independent typography combination; no model or guessed wrapping. */
    267. 

  267. export async function collectDesignFieldMeasurements(
    268. 

  268.   input: unknown,
    269. 

  269. ): Promise<DesignFieldMeasurements> {
    270. 

  270.   const brief = DesignFieldBriefSchema.parse(input),
    271. 

  271.     source = await sourcesFor(brief);
    272. 

  272.   for (const [font, text] of [
    273. 

  273.     [brief.titleFont, brief.title],
    274. 

  274.     [brief.bodyFont, brief.body],
    275. 

  275.   ] as const) {
    276. 

  276.     fontAt(
    277. 

  277.       BlockSchema.parse({
    278. 

  278.         id: 'measurement',
    279. 

  279.         role: 'title',
    280. 

  280.         text,
    281. 

  281.         font: {
    282. 

  282.           file: font.file,
    283. 

  283.           family: font.family,
    284. 

  284.           axes: font.axes,
    285. 

  285.           opticalSizing: font.opticalSizing,
    286. 

  286.         },
    287. 

  287.         size: 32,
    288. 

  288.         weight: font.weight,
    289. 

  289.         trackingEm: 0,
    290. 

  290.         leading: 1.5,
    291. 

  291.         measure: 400,
    292. 

  292.       }),
    293. 

  293.     );
    294. 

  294.     const facts = inspectFont(font.file, text);
    295. 

  295.     if (facts.glyphCoverage.missing.length)
    296. 

  296.       throw new Error(
    297. 

  297.         'Design field requires primary font coverage; fallback is not measured',
    298. 

  298.       );
    299. 

  299.   }
    300. 

  300.   const imageText = source.bytes.image.toString('utf8');
    301. 

  301.   if (
    302. 

  302.     !imageText.trimStart().startsWith('<svg') ||
    303. 

  303.     /\b(?:href|src)\s*=|<script|<foreignObject/i.test(imageText)
    304. 

  304.   )
    305. 

  305.     throw new Error(
    306. 

  306.       'Field fixture requires self-contained SVG without linked resources or scripts',
    307. 

  307.     );
    308. 

  308.   const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true });
    309. 

  309.   try {
    310. 

  310.     const page = await browser.newPage();
    311. 

  311.     await page.route('**/*', (r) => r.abort());
    312. 

  312.     await page.setContent(
    313. 

  313.       '<!doctype html><html><head></head><body></body></html>',
    314. 

  314.     );
    315. 

  315.     const payload = {
    316. 

  316.       brief,
    317. 

  317.       requests: designFieldMeasurementRequests(brief),
    318. 

  318.       titleFont: source.bytes.titleFont.toString('base64'),
    319. 

  319.       bodyFont: source.bytes.bodyFont.toString('base64'),
    320. 

  320.       image: source.bytes.image.toString('base64'),
    321. 

  321.     };
    322. 

  322.     const measured = await page.evaluate(async (p) => {
    323. 

  323.       const style = document.createElement('style');
    324. 

  324.       style.textContent = `@font-face{font-family:FieldTitle;src:url(data:font/ttf;base64,${p.titleFont});font-weight:${p.brief.titleFont.weight}}@font-face{font-family:FieldBody;src:url(data:font/ttf;base64,${p.bodyFont});font-weight:${p.brief.bodyFont.weight}}`;
    325. 

  325.       document.head.append(style);
    326. 

  326.       await Promise.all([
    327. 

  327.         document.fonts.load(`${p.brief.titleFont.weight} 32px FieldTitle`),
    328. 

  328.         document.fonts.load(`${p.brief.bodyFont.weight} 20px FieldBody`),
    329. 

  329.       ]);
    330. 

  330.       await document.fonts.ready;
    331. 

  331.       const rows = p.requests.map((r) => {
    332. 

  332.         const el = document.createElement('div'),
    333. 

  333.           title = r.kind === 'title',
    334. 

  334.           weight = title ? p.brief.titleFont.weight : p.brief.bodyFont.weight;
    335. 

  335.         el.textContent = title ? p.brief.title : p.brief.body;
    336. 

  336.         el.style.cssText = `font-family:${title ? 'FieldTitle' : 'FieldBody'};font-weight:${weight};font-size:${r.effectiveSize}px;line-height:${r.leading};letter-spacing:${title ? p.brief.typography.titleTrackingEm : p.brief.typography.bodyTrackingEm}em;font-optical-sizing:${title ? p.brief.titleFont.opticalSizing : p.brief.bodyFont.opticalSizing};font-variation-settings:${
    337. 

  337.           Object.entries(title ? p.brief.titleFont.axes : p.brief.bodyFont.axes)
    338. 

  338.             .map(([tag, value]) => `"${tag}" ${value}`)
    339. 

  339.             .join(',') || 'normal'
    340. 

  340.         };width:${r.measure}px;white-space:normal;overflow-wrap:normal;direction:ltr;box-sizing:content-box;margin:0;padding:0;border:0`;
    341. 

  341.         document.body.append(el);
    342. 

  342.         const box = el.getBoundingClientRect(),
    343. 

  343.           range = document.createRange();
    344. 

  344.         range.selectNodeContents(el);
    345. 

  345.         const rects = [...range.getClientRects()];
    346. 

  346.         const lines = new Set(rects.map((r) => Math.round(r.top * 64))).size;
    347. 

  347.         const overflowX = Math.max(
    348. 

  348.           0,
    349. 

  349.           ...rects.flatMap((r) => [box.left - r.left, r.right - box.right]),
    350. 

  350.         );
    351. 

  351.         const row = {
    352. 

  352.           scenario: r.scenario,
    353. 

  353.           key: r.key,
    354. 

  354.           height: box.height,
    355. 

  355.           width: box.width,
    356. 

  356.           lines,
    357. 

  357.           overflowX,
    358. 

  358.           fontLoaded: document.fonts.check(
    359. 

  359.             `${weight} ${r.effectiveSize}px ${title ? 'FieldTitle' : 'FieldBody'}`,
    360. 

  360.           ),
    361. 

  361.         };
    362. 

  362.         el.remove();
    363. 

  363.         return row;
    364. 

  364.       });
    365. 

  365.       const img = new Image();
    366. 

  366.       img.src = `data:image/svg+xml;base64,${p.image}`;
    367. 

  367.       await img.decode();
    368. 

  368.       const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    369. 

  369.       canvas.width = 32;
    370. 

  370.       canvas.height = 32;
    371. 

  371.       const context = canvas.getContext('2d')!;
    372. 

  372.       context.drawImage(img, 0, 0, 32, 32);
    373. 

  373.       const data = context.getImageData(0, 0, 32, 32).data;
    374. 

  374.       let sum = 0,
    375. 

  375.         sq = 0;
    376. 

  376.       for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i += 4) {
    377. 

  377.         const channels = [data[i]!, data[i + 1]!, data[i + 2]!].map((v) => {
    378. 

  378.           const s = v / 255;
    379. 

  379.           return s <= 0.04045 ? s / 12.92 : ((s + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4;
    380. 

  380.         });
    381. 

  381.         const l =
    382. 

  382.           0.2126 * channels[0]! + 0.7152 * channels[1]! + 0.0722 * channels[2]!;
    383. 

  383.         sum += l;
    384. 

  384.         sq += l * l;
    385. 

  385.       }
    386. 

  386.       const mean = sum / 1024;
    387. 

  387.       return {
    388. 

  388.         rows,
    389. 

  389.         image: {
    390. 

  390.           width: img.naturalWidth,
    391. 

  391.           height: img.naturalHeight,
    392. 

  392.           meanLuminance: mean,
    393. 

  393.           luminanceStdDev: Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, sq / 1024 - mean * mean)),
    394. 

  394.           sampleCount: 1024,
    395. 

  395.         },
    396. 

  396.       };
    397. 

  397.     }, payload);
    398. 

  398.     const base = {
    399. 

  399.       version: 1 as const,
    400. 

  400.       sourceIdentity: sourceIdentity(brief, source.sources),
    401. 

  401.       policy: measurementPolicy,
    402. 

  402.       provider: 'chromium' as const,
    403. 

  403.       runtime: `Chromium ${browser.version()}`,
    404. 

  404.       sources: source.sources,
    405. 

  405.       ...measured,
    406. 

  406.     };
    407. 

  407.     return DesignFieldMeasurementsSchema.parse({
    408. 

  408.       ...base,
    409. 

  409.       identity: hash(base),
    410. 

  410.     });
    411. 

  411.   } finally {
    412. 

  412.     await browser.close();
    413. 

  413.   }
    414. 

  414. }
    415. 

  415. const ref = (name: string): Expression => ({ ref: name });
    416. 

  416. const op = (
    417. 

  417.   name: 'add' | 'sub' | 'mul' | 'div' | 'min' | 'max' | 'abs' | 'lte' | 'if',
    418. 

  418.   ...args: Expression[]
    419. 

  419. ): Expression => ({ op: name, args });
    420. 

  420. const sum = (terms: Expression[]): Expression =>
    421. 

  421.   terms.length <= 16
    422. 

  422.     ? op('add', ...terms)
    423. 

  423.     : sum(
    424. 

  424.         Array.from({ length: Math.ceil(terms.length / 16) }, (_, i) =>
    425. 

  425.           op('add', ...terms.slice(i * 16, i * 16 + 16)),
    426. 

  426.         ),
    427. 

  427.       );
    428. 

  428. const eq = (name: string, value: number) =>
    429. 

  429.   op('min', op('lte', ref(name), value), op('lte', value, ref(name)));
    430. 

  430. const p = (
    431. 

  431.   source: string,
    432. 

  432.   kind: 'brief' | 'calculated' | 'hypothesis' = 'brief',
    433. 

  433. ) => ({ kind, source });
    434. 

  434. function validateMeasurements(brief: DesignFieldBrief, input: unknown) {
    435. 

  435.   const m = DesignFieldMeasurementsSchema.parse(input),
    436. 

  436.     { identity, ...base } = m;
    437. 

  437.   if (hash(base) !== identity)
    438. 

  438.     throw new Error('Measurement integrity mismatch');
    439. 

  439.   if (sourceIdentity(brief, m.sources) !== m.sourceIdentity)
    440. 

  440.     throw new Error(
    441. 

  441.       'Measurements do not bind current text/fonts/domains/scenarios',
    442. 

  442.     );
    443. 

  443.   if (
    444. 

  444.     m.sources.content !==
    445. 

  445.     hash({ title: brief.title, body: brief.body, action: brief.action })
    446. 

  446.   )
    447. 

  447.     throw new Error('Measurement content changed');
    448. 

  448.   const expected = designFieldMeasurementRequests(brief);
    449. 

  449.   if (m.rows.length !== expected.length)
    450. 

  450.     throw new Error('Incomplete typography measurement matrix');
    451. 

  451.   const rows = new Map(m.rows.map((r) => [`${r.scenario}:${r.key}`, r]));
    452. 

  452.   if (rows.size !== m.rows.length)
    453. 

  453.     throw new Error('Duplicate typography measurement');
    454. 

  454.   for (const req of expected) {
    455. 

  455.     const row = rows.get(`${req.scenario}:${req.key}`);
    456. 

  456.     if (!row || Math.abs(row.width - req.measure) > 0.05 || !row.fontLoaded)
    457. 

  457.       throw new Error('Missing or mismatched measured typography cell');
    458. 

  458.   }
    459. 

  459.   return m;
    460. 

  460. }
    461. 

  461. export const DesignFieldRunSchema = z
    462. 

  462.   .object({
    463. 

  463.     brief: DesignFieldBriefSchema,
    464. 

  464.     measurements: DesignFieldMeasurementsSchema,
    465. 

  465.     selector: RunDecisionWorkflowSchema.shape.selector,
    466. 

  466.     recipe: RunDecisionWorkflowSchema.shape.recipe,
    467. 

  467.     mode: z.enum(['search', 'replay']).default('search'),
    468. 

  468.   })
    469. 

  469.   .strict();
    470. 

  470. export function compileDesignField(input: {
    471. 

  471.   brief: unknown;
    472. 

  472.   measurements: unknown;
    473. 

  473.   selection?: 'mathematical' | 'local-selector';
    474. 

  474.   designContextIdentity?: string;
    475. 

  475. }) {
    476. 

  476.   const brief = DesignFieldBriefSchema.parse(input.brief),
    477. 

  477.     measurements = validateMeasurements(brief, input.measurements),
    478. 

  478.     selection = input.selection ?? 'mathematical';
    479. 

  479.   if (brief.designContext && !input.designContextIdentity)
    480. 

  480.     throw new Error(
    481. 

  481.       'Inspect the declared design context before compiling this field',
    482. 

  482.     );
    483. 

  483.   const commonIdentity = {
    484. 

  484.     designContext: input.designContextIdentity ?? 'none',
    485. 

  485.     measurement: measurements.identity,
    486. 

  486.     source: measurements.sourceIdentity,
    487. 

  487.     briefSource: measurements.sources.briefSource,
    488. 

  488.     compiler: 'design-field-v1',
    489. 

  489.   };
    490. 

  490.   const readingNames = [
    491. 

  491.     'titleSize',
    492. 

  492.     'bodySize',
    493. 

  493.     'contentMax',
    494. 

  494.     'gap',
    495. 

  495.     'leading',
    496. 

  496.   ] as const;
    497. 

  497.   const rows = designFieldMeasurementRequests(brief).filter(
    498. 

  498.     (r) => r.scenario === 'phone',
    499. 

  499.   );
    500. 

  500.   const lookup = (
    501. 

  501.     kind: 'title' | 'body',
    502. 

  502.     field: 'height' | 'lines' | 'overflowX',
    503. 

  503.   ) =>
    504. 

  504.     sum(
    505. 

  505.       rows
    506. 

  506.         .filter((r) => r.kind === kind)
    507. 

  507.         .map((r) =>
    508. 

  508.           op(
    509. 

  509.             'mul',
    510. 

  510.             eq(kind === 'title' ? 'titleSize' : 'bodySize', r.size),
    511. 

  511.             eq('contentMax', r.column),
    512. 

  512.             ...(kind === 'body' ? [eq('leading', r.leading)] : []),
    513. 

  513.             ref(`${r.key}_${field}`),
    514. 

  514.           ),
    515. 

  515.         ),
    516. 

  516.     );
    517. 

  517.   const derived = (
    518. 

  518.     id: string,
    519. 

  519.     expression: Expression,
    520. 

  520.     unit: string,
    521. 

  521.     source: string,
    522. 

  522.     kind: 'brief' | 'calculated' | 'hypothesis' = 'calculated',
    523. 

  523.   ) => ({ id, expression, unit, provenance: p(source, kind) });
    524. 

  524.   const factor = (
    525. 

  525.     id: string,
    526. 

  526.     expression: Expression,
    527. 

  527.     bounds: readonly [number, number],
    528. 

  528.     scale: number,
    529. 

  529.     weight: number,
    530. 

  530.     category: string,
    531. 

  531.     source: string,
    532. 

  532.     mode: 'hard' | 'preference' = 'preference',
    533. 

  533.   ) => ({
    534. 

  534.     id,
    535. 

  535.     expression,
    536. 

  536.     bands: [{ min: bounds[0], max: bounds[1] }],
    537. 

  537.     scale,
    538. 

  538.     weight,
    539. 

  539.     category,
    540. 

  540.     mode,
    541. 

  541.     provenance: p(source, mode === 'hard' ? 'calculated' : 'brief'),
    542. 

  542.   });
    543. 

  543.   const reading: EnergyProgram = {
    544. 

  544.     version: 1,
    545. 

  545.     id: 'field-reading',
    546. 

  546.     brief: { intent: brief.intent, source: brief.source },
    547. 

  547.     identity: commonIdentity,
    548. 

  548.     variables: readingNames.map((id) => ({
    549. 

  549.       id,
    550. 

  550.       label: DESIGN_FIELD_VARIABLE_LABELS[id],
    551. 

  551.       values: [...DESIGN_FIELD_DOMAINS[id]],
    552. 

  552.       unit: id === 'leading' ? 'ratio' : 'px',
    553. 

  553.     })),
    554. 

  554.     scenarios: DESIGN_FIELD_SCENARIOS.map((s) => ({
    555. 

  555.       id: s.id,
    556. 

  556.       values: {
    557. 

  557.         viewport: s.width,
    558. 

  558.         height: s.height,
    559. 

  559.         textScale: s.textScale,
    560. 

  560.         padding: padding(s.width),
    561. 

  561.         actionBudget: s.height * (s.textScale === 1 ? 0.98 : 1.8),
    562. 

  562.         ...Object.fromEntries(
    563. 

  563.           measurements.rows
    564. 

  564.             .filter((r) => r.scenario === s.id)
    565. 

  565.             .flatMap((r) =>
    566. 

  566.               ['height', 'lines', 'overflowX'].map((field) => [
    567. 

  567.                 `${r.key}_${field}`,
    568. 

  568.                 r[field as 'height' | 'lines' | 'overflowX'],
    569. 

  569.               ]),
    570. 

  570.             ),
    571. 

  571.         ),
    572. 

  572.       },
    573. 

  573.     })),
    574. 

  574.     derived: [
    575. 

  575.       derived(
    576. 

  576.         'titleHeight',
    577. 

  577.         lookup('title', 'height'),
    578. 

  578.         'px',
    579. 

  579.         'Actual DOM candidate table; same source font/content and declared scenario',
    580. 

  580.       ),
    581. 

  581.       derived(
    582. 

  582.         'bodyHeight',
    583. 

  583.         lookup('body', 'height'),
    584. 

  584.         'px',
    585. 

  585.         'Actual DOM candidate table',
    586. 

  586.       ),
    587. 

  587.       derived(
    588. 

  588.         'titleLines',
    589. 

  589.         lookup('title', 'lines'),
    590. 

  590.         'lines',
    591. 

  591.         'Actual DOM Range line groups',
    592. 

  592.       ),
    593. 

  593.       derived(
    594. 

  594.         'bodyLines',
    595. 

  595.         lookup('body', 'lines'),
    596. 

  596.         'lines',
    597. 

  597.         'Actual DOM Range line groups',
    598. 

  598.       ),
    599. 

  599.       derived(
    600. 

  600.         'overflow',
    601. 

  601.         op('max', lookup('title', 'overflowX'), lookup('body', 'overflowX')),
    602. 

  602.         'px',
    603. 

  603.         'Actual DOM Range horizontal overflow',
    604. 

  604.       ),
    605. 

  605.       derived(
    606. 

  606.         'effectiveTitle',
    607. 

  607.         op(
    608. 

  608.           'mul',
    609. 

  609.           op('min', ref('titleSize'), op('mul', ref('viewport'), 0.135)),
    610. 

  610.           ref('textScale'),
    611. 

  611.         ),
    612. 

  612.         'px',
    613. 

  613.         'Authored responsive title envelope',
    614. 

  614.       ),
    615. 

  615.       derived(
    616. 

  616.         'hierarchy',
    617. 

  617.         op(
    618. 

  618.           'div',
    619. 

  619.           ref('effectiveTitle'),
    620. 

  620.           op('mul', ref('bodySize'), ref('textScale')),
    621. 

  621.         ),
    622. 

  622.         'ratio',
    623. 

  623.         'Computed title/body ratio',
    624. 

  624.       ),
    625. 

  625.       derived(
    626. 

  626.         'gapRatio',
    627. 

  627.         op('div', ref('gap'), ref('bodySize')),
    628. 

  628.         'ratio',
    629. 

  629.         'Gap relative to body text',
    630. 

  630.       ),
    631. 

  631.       derived(
    632. 

  632.         'charactersPerLine',
    633. 

  633.         op('div', Array.from(brief.body).length, ref('bodyLines')),
    634. 

  634.         'characters/line',
    635. 

  635.         'Actual content length divided by measured line count; not a universal readability score',
    636. 

  636.       ),
    637. 

  637.       derived(
    638. 

  638.         'ctaBottom',
    639. 

  639.         op(
    640. 

  640.           'add',
    641. 

  641.           ref('padding'),
    642. 

  642.           ref('titleHeight'),
    643. 

  643.           ref('bodyHeight'),
    644. 

  644.           op('mul', ref('gap'), 2),
    645. 

  645.           44,
    646. 

  646.         ),
    647. 

  647.         'px',
    648. 

  648.         'Natural text height; task action before image',
    649. 

  649.       ),
    650. 

  650.       derived(
    651. 

  651.         'actionSlack',
    652. 

  652.         op('sub', ref('actionBudget'), ref('ctaBottom')),
    653. 

  653.         'px',
    654. 

  654.         'Authored first-action reach envelope',
    655. 

  655.       ),
    656. 

  656.     ],
    657. 

  657.     factors: [
    658. 

  658.       factor(
    659. 

  659.         'primary-font-and-wrap',
    660. 

  660.         ref('overflow'),
    661. 

  661.         [0, 0.75],
    662. 

  662.         1,
    663. 

  663.         1,
    664. 

  664.         'rendering',
    665. 

  665.         'Measured candidate text overflow tolerance; not whole composition verification',
    666. 

  666.         'hard',
    667. 

  667.       ),
    668. 

  668.       factor(
    669. 

  669.         'action-reach',
    670. 

  670.         ref('actionSlack'),
    671. 

  671.         [0, 5000],
    672. 

  672.         100,
    673. 

  673.         1,
    674. 

  674.         'task',
    675. 

  675.         'Authored action reach, enlarged text allowed additional vertical scroll',
    676. 

  676.         'hard',
    677. 

  677.       ),
    678. 

  678.       factor(
    679. 

  679.         'type-hierarchy',
    680. 

  680.         ref('hierarchy'),
    681. 

  681.         brief.targets.hierarchy,
    682. 

  682.         1,
    683. 

  683.         brief.weights.hierarchy,
    684. 

  684.         'hierarchy',
    685. 

  685.         brief.source,
    686. 

  686.       ),
    687. 

  687.       factor(
    688. 

  688.         'reading-density',
    689. 

  689.         op('mul', ref('charactersPerLine'), ref('textScale')),
    690. 

  690.         [40, 76],
    691. 

  691.         16,
    692. 

  692.         brief.weights.reading,
    693. 

  693.         'reading',
    694. 

  694.         'Authored reading preference, actual line count',
    695. 

  695.       ),
    696. 

  696.       factor(

  697.         'spatial-rhythm',

  698.         ref('gapRatio'),

  700.         0.7,
    701. 

  701.         brief.weights.density,
    702. 

  702.         'density',
    703. 

  703.         brief.source,
    704. 

  704.       ),
    705. 

  705.       factor(
    706. 

  706.         'headline-wrap',
    707. 

  707.         op('div', ref('titleLines'), ref('textScale')),
    708. 

  708.         [1, 2.4],
    709. 

  709.         1,
    710. 

  710.         0.6,
    711. 

  711.         'hierarchy',
    712. 

  712.         'Authored headline emphasis; line breaks measured, preference subjective',
    713.

    714. 

  714.     ],
    715. 

  715.   };
    716. 

  716.   const expressionNames = [
    717. 

  717.     'imageAspect',
    718. 

  718.     'imageShare',
    719. 

  719.     'roughness',

    721. 

  721.     'motionRole',

    723. 

  723.   const expression: EnergyProgram = {
    724. 

  724.     version: 1,
    725. 

  725.     id: 'field-expression',
    726. 

  726.     brief: { intent: brief.intent, source: brief.source },
    727. 

  727.     identity: commonIdentity,
    728. 

  728.     variables: expressionNames.map((id) => ({
    729. 

  729.       id,

    731. 

  731.       values: [...DESIGN_FIELD_DOMAINS[id]],
    732. 

  732.       unit: id === 'motionRole' ? 'role-index' : 'ratio',
    733. 

  733.       ...(id === 'motionRole'
    734. 

  734.         ? {
    735. 

  735.             valueAliases: DESIGN_FIELD_MOTION_ROLES.map((label, value) => ({
    736. 

  736.               value,
    737. 

  737.               label,
    738. 

  738.             })),
    739. 

  739.           }
    740. 

  740.         : {}),
    741. 

  741.     })),
    742. 

  742.     scenarios: DESIGN_FIELD_SCENARIOS.map((s) => ({
    743. 

  743.       id: s.id,
    744. 

  744.       values: {
    745. 

  745.         viewport: s.width,
    746. 

  746.         height: s.height,
    747. 

  747.         textScale: s.textScale,
    748. 

  748.         padding: padding(s.width),
    749. 

  749.         titleSize: 76,
    750. 

  750.         bodySize: 18,
    751. 

  751.         contentMax: 1080,
    752. 

  752.         gap: 32,
    753.

    754. 

  754.         sourceAspect: measurements.image.width / measurements.image.height,
    755. 

  755.         ...Object.fromEntries(
    756. 

  756.           measurements.rows
    757. 

  757.             .filter((r) => r.scenario === s.id)
    758. 

  758.             .flatMap((r) =>
    759. 

  759.               ['height', 'lines', 'overflowX'].map((field) => [
    760. 

  760.                 `${r.key}_${field}`,
    761. 

  761.                 r[field as 'height' | 'lines' | 'overflowX'],
    762. 

  762.               ]),
    763. 

  763.             ),
    764. 

  764.         ),

    766. 

  766.     })),
    767. 

  767.     derived: [

    904. 

  904.   const stages = [
    905. 

  905.     {

    909.
