  926.         maxExpressionOperations: 15000000,
    927. 

  927.         completion: 'require',
    928. 

  928.         parentTies: 'prefer-successor-targets',
    929. 

  929.       },
    930. 

  930.       selection,
    931. 

  931.       question:
    932. 

  932.         'Choose a bold readable type/space alternative for this synthetic brief. Compare the supplied successor witnesses: some equally ranked reading choices let image/finish/motion satisfy all authored targets, while others retain explicit scenario violations. Prefer a jointly satisfied continuation when these reading costs are identical, unless the stated art direction supplies a reason otherwise. Hard completion is required; preference guidance remains revisable. This is uncalibrated model judgment, not renderer or preference verification.',
    933. 

  933.       provenance: p(brief.source),
    934. 

  934.     },
    935. 

  935.     {
    936. 

  936.       id: 'expression',
    937. 

  937.       purpose:
    938. 

  938.         'Condition image, finish and motion roles on the selected type hierarchy, preserving deliberate nonzero tension',
    939. 

  939.       dependsOn: ['reading'],
    940. 

  940.       bindings: readingNames.map((variable) => ({
    941. 

  941.         input: variable,
    942. 

  942.         stage: 'reading',
    943. 

  943.         variable,
    944. 

  944.       })),
    945. 

  945.       contextKeys: ['direction'],
    946. 

  946.       program: expression,
    947. 

  947.       options: {
    948. 

  948.         method: 'exhaustive',
    949. 

  949.         maxEvaluations: 1000,
    950. 

  950.         maxExpressionOperations: 50000000,
    951. 

  951.         maxFactorObservations: 1000000,
    952. 

  952.         limit: 6,
    953. 

  953.         retention: 'pareto-diverse',
    954. 

  954.       },
    955. 

  955.       selection,
    956. 

  956.       question:
    957. 

  957.         'Choose among retained image/finish/motion alternatives, weighing their individual scenario tradeoffs against this synthetic brief. Equal cost vectors leave art direction underdetermined. A target band can remain unreachable under the chosen reading layout; do not call mathematical admissibility target satisfaction. This is uncalibrated model judgment, not a universal beauty score or appearance verification.',
    958. 

  958.       provenance: p(brief.source),
    959. 

  959.     },
    960. 

  960.   ];
    961. 

  961.   const workflow = DecisionWorkflowSchema.parse({
    962. 

  962.     version: 1,
    963. 

  963.     id: brief.id,
    964. 

  964.     context: { direction: brief.intent },
    965. 

  965.     stages,
    966. 

  966.     budgets: {
    967. 

  967.       maxEvaluations: 5000,
    968. 

  968.       maxGraphNodes: 5000,
    969. 

  969.       maxFactorObservations: 2250000,
    970. 

  970.       maxExpressionOperations: 115000000,
    971. 

  971.       maxSelectorCalls: 2,
    972. 

  972.       maxReadoutPasses: 2,
    973. 

  973.     },
    974. 

  974.   });
    975. 

  975.   return { brief, measurements, workflow };
    976. 

  976. }
    977. 

  977. export type DesignFieldLedgerEntry = {
    978. 

  978.   command: string;
    979. 

  979.   function: string;
    980. 

  980.   inputHash: string;
    981. 

  981.   resultHash: string;
    982. 

  982.   evidenceKind: string;
    983. 

  983.   runtime: string;
    984. 

  984.   elapsedMs: number;
    985. 

  985. };
    986. 

  986. /** Finite math remains the inner loop. Only the two retained frontiers may invoke the pinned local selector. */
    987. 

  987. export async function runDesignField(
    988. 

  988.   input: z.input<typeof DesignFieldRunSchema>,
    989. 

  989.   execution: SelectorExecutionOptions = {},
    990. 

  990. ): Promise<DesignFieldSession> {
    991. 

  991.   execution.signal?.throwIfAborted();
    992. 

  992.   const r = DesignFieldRunSchema.parse(input),
    993. 

  993.     source = await sourcesFor(r.brief);
    994. 

  994.   if (hash(source.sources) !== hash(r.measurements.sources))
    995. 

  995.     throw new Error(
    996. 

  996.       'Current source bytes/content do not match measured field inputs; recollect before search/replay',
    997. 

  997.     );
    998. 

  998.   if (r.selector && r.measurements.provider !== 'chromium')
    999. 

  999.     throw new Error(
    1000. 

  1000.       'Real selector field runs require actual browser input measurements',
    1001. 

  1001.     );
    1002. 

  1002.   const ledger: DesignFieldLedgerEntry[] = [];
    1003. 

  1003.   let contextIdentity: string | undefined;
    1004. 

  1004.   if (r.brief.designContext) {
    1005. 

  1005.     const contextInput = { file: r.brief.designContext };
    1006. 

  1006.     const t = performance.now();
    1007. 

  1007.     const context = await inspectDesignContext(contextInput);
    1008. 

  1008.     if (context.status !== 'found')
    1009. 

  1009.       throw new Error('Declared design context is missing');
    1010. 

  1010.     contextIdentity = context.sha256;
    1011. 

  1011.     ledger.push({
    1012. 

  1012.       command: 'context inspect',
    1013. 

  1013.       function: 'inspectDesignContext',
    1014. 

  1014.       inputHash: hash(contextInput),
    1015. 

  1015.       resultHash: hash(context),
    1016. 

  1016.       evidenceKind: 'authored-design-contract-byte-identity',
    1017. 

  1017.       runtime: 'Node',
    1018. 

  1018.       elapsedMs: round(performance.now() - t),
    1019. 

  1019.     });
    1020. 

  1020.   }
    1021. 

  1021.   const c = compileDesignField({
    1022. 

  1022.     ...r,
    1023. 

  1023.     selection: r.selector ? 'local-selector' : 'mathematical',
    1024. 

  1024.     ...(contextIdentity ? { designContextIdentity: contextIdentity } : {}),
    1025. 

  1025.   });
    1026. 

  1026.   const request: DecisionWorkflowInput = {
    1027. 

  1027.     workflow: c.workflow,
    1028. 

  1028.     mode: r.mode,
    1029. 

  1029.     ...(r.selector ? { selector: r.selector } : {}),
    1030. 

  1030.     ...(r.recipe ? { recipe: r.recipe } : {}),
    1031. 

  1031.   };
    1032. 

  1032.   const started = performance.now(),
    1033. 

  1033.     result = await runDecisionWorkflow(request, undefined, execution);
    1034. 

  1034.   ledger.push({
    1035. 

  1035.     command: 'decision run',
    1036. 

  1036.     function: 'runDecisionWorkflow',
    1037. 

  1037.     inputHash: hash(request),
    1038. 

  1038.     resultHash: hash(result),
    1039. 

  1039.     evidenceKind: r.selector
    1040. 

  1040.       ? 'numeric-search-and-local-selector'
    1041. 

  1041.       : 'numeric-search',
    1042. 

  1042.     runtime: 'Node; design-decision-workflow-v1',
    1043. 

  1043.     elapsedMs: round(performance.now() - started),
    1044. 

  1044.   });
    1045. 

  1045.   const analyses: Record<string, ReturnType<typeof analyzeEnergy>> = {};
    1046. 

  1046.   for (const stage of c.workflow.stages) {
    1047. 

  1047.     const selected = result.stages[stage.id];
    1048. 

  1048.     if (!selected?.assignment) continue;
    1049. 

  1049.     const program = structuredClone(stage.program);
    1050. 

  1050.     for (const binding of stage.bindings)
    1051. 

  1051.       for (const scenario of program.scenarios)
    1052. 

  1052.         scenario.values[binding.input] =
    1053. 

  1053.           result.stages[binding.stage]!.assignment![binding.variable]!;
    1054. 

  1054.     const analysisInput = { program, assignment: selected.assignment };
    1055. 

  1055.     const t = performance.now();
    1056. 

  1056.     analyses[stage.id] = analyzeEnergy(analysisInput);
    1057. 

  1057.     for (const effect of analyses[stage.id]!.effects)
    1058. 

  1058.       if (effect.variable === 'motionRole') effect.negativeEnergySlope = null;
    1059. 

  1059.     ledger.push({
    1060. 

  1060.       command: 'energy analyze',
    1061. 

  1061.       function: 'analyzeEnergy',
    1062. 

  1062.       inputHash: hash(analysisInput),
    1063. 

  1063.       resultHash: hash(analyses[stage.id]),
    1064. 

  1064.       evidenceKind: 'finite-difference-mathematics',
    1065. 

  1065.       runtime: 'Node; energy-v1',
    1066. 

  1066.       elapsedMs: round(performance.now() - t),
    1067. 

  1067.     });
    1068. 

  1068.   }
    1069. 

  1069.   const tokens = {
    1070. 

  1070.     ...result.stages.reading?.assignment,
    1071. 

  1071.     ...result.stages.expression?.assignment,
    1072. 

  1072.   };
    1073. 

  1073.   return {
    1074. 

  1074.     version: 1 as const,
    1075. 

  1075.     identity: hash({ workflow: c.workflow, recipe: result.recipe }),
    1076. 

  1076.     brief: c.brief,
    1077. 

  1077.     measurements: c.measurements,
    1078. 

  1078.     workflow: result,
    1079. 

  1079.     programs: c.workflow.stages.map((s) => s.program),
    1080. 

  1080.     analyses,
    1081. 

  1081.     tokens,
    1082. 

  1082.     ledger,
    1083. 

  1083.     rendererVerification: 'pending' as const,
    1084. 

  1084.     scope:
    1085. 

  1085.       'Source-bound typography tables are measured. Coupled full compositions and material/motion appearance remain unrendered until finalist verification. Preference pressures are dimensionless authored tradeoffs, not physical forces or beauty.',
    1086. 

  1086.   };
    1087. 

  1087. }
    1088. 

  1088. export interface DesignFieldSession {
    1089. 

  1089.   version: 1;
    1090. 

  1090.   identity: string;
    1091. 

  1091.   brief: DesignFieldBrief;
    1092. 

  1092.   measurements: DesignFieldMeasurements;
    1093. 

  1093.   workflow: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof runDecisionWorkflow>>;
    1094. 

  1094.   programs: EnergyProgram[];
    1095. 

  1095.   analyses: Record<string, ReturnType<typeof analyzeEnergy>>;
    1096. 

  1096.   tokens: Assignment;
    1097. 

  1097.   ledger: DesignFieldLedgerEntry[];
    1098. 

  1098.   rendererVerification: 'pending';
    1099. 

  1099.   scope: string;
    1100. 

  1100. }
    1101. 

  1101. /** Compare actual factor work at finite neighbours, retaining opposed effects instead of hiding them in a total. */
    1102. 

  1102. export function designFieldStageView(session: DesignFieldSession, id: string) {
    1103. 

  1103.   const stage = session.workflow.stages[id],
    1104. 

  1104.     analysis = session.analyses[id];
    1105. 

  1105.   if (!stage || !analysis) return null;
    1106. 

  1106.   const base = analysis.baseline;
    1107. 

  1107.   const search = (
    1108. 

  1108.     stage.evidence as { search?: ReturnType<typeof searchEnergy> } | null
    1109. 

  1109.   )?.search;
    1110. 

  1110.   return {
    1111. 

  1111.     id,
    1112. 

  1112.     status: stage.status,
    1113. 

  1113.     assignment: stage.assignment,
    1114. 

  1114.     energy: base.energy,
    1115. 

  1115.     hardFailures: base.hardFailures,
    1116. 

  1116.     factors: base.factors,
    1117. 

  1117.     retention: search?.retention ?? null,
    1118. 

  1118.     alternatives: stage.alternatives.map((a) => ({
    1119. 

  1119.       id: a.id,
    1120. 

  1120.       assignment: a.assignment,
    1121. 

  1121.       energy: a.energy,
    1122. 

  1122.       hardFailures: a.hardFailures,
    1123. 

  1123.       factors: a.factors,
    1124. 

  1124.       rendererVerification: 'pending',
    1125. 

  1125.     })),
    1126. 

  1126.     pressures: analysis.effects,
    1127. 

  1127.     work: stage.alternatives.map((a) => ({
    1128. 

  1128.       alternative: a.id,
    1129. 

  1129.       totalPreferenceChange: a.energy - base.energy,
    1130. 

  1130.       factors: a.factors
    1131. 

  1131.         .map((f, i) => ({
    1132. 

  1132.           id: f.id,
    1133. 

  1133.           scenario: f.scenario,
    1134. 

  1134.           energyChange: f.energy - base.factors[i]!.energy,
    1135. 

  1135.           valueChange: f.value - base.factors[i]!.value,
    1136. 

  1136.         }))
    1137. 

  1137.         .filter((f) => Math.abs(f.energyChange) > 1e-8),
    1138. 

  1138.     })),
    1139. 

  1139.   };
    1140. 

  1140. }
    1141. 

  1141. export const DesignFieldValidateSchema = z
    1142. 

  1142.   .object({
    1143. 

  1143.     brief: DesignFieldBriefSchema,
    1144. 

  1144.     measurements: DesignFieldMeasurementsSchema,
    1145. 

  1145.     tokens: z.record(z.string(), finite),
    1146. 

  1146.   })
    1147. 

  1147.   .strict();
    1148. 

  1148. /** Receipt identity calculation only; a digest is not proof that rendering occurred.
    1149. 

  1149.  * The full normalized brief deliberately invalidates on provenance edits as well
    1150. 

  1150.  * as rendering edits. Measurement identity binds the compared reference table.
    1151. 

  1151.  */
    1152. 

  1152. export function designFieldValidationIdentity(
    1153. 

  1153.   input: z.input<typeof DesignFieldValidateSchema>,
    1154. 

  1154.   runtime: string,
    1155. 

  1155. ) {
    1156. 

  1156.   const r = DesignFieldValidateSchema.parse(input);
    1157. 

  1157.   return hash({
    1158. 

  1158.     validator: 'design-field-finalist-v2',
    1159. 

  1159.     brief: r.brief,
    1160. 

  1160.     measurements: r.measurements.identity,
    1161. 

  1161.     sources: r.measurements.sources,
    1162. 

  1162.     tokens: r.tokens,
    1163. 

  1163.     scenarios: DESIGN_FIELD_SCENARIOS,
    1164. 

  1164.     runtime: z.string().min(1).parse(runtime),
    1165. 

  1165.   });
    1166. 

  1166. }
    1167. 

  1167. /** Layout-only verification of the exact generated finalist fixture; not a certificate for the surrounding gallery. */
    1168. 

  1168. export async function validateDesignFieldFinalist(
    1169. 

  1169.   input: z.input<typeof DesignFieldValidateSchema>,
    1170. 

  1170. ) {
    1171. 

  1171.   const r = DesignFieldValidateSchema.parse(input),
    1172. 

  1172.     source = await sourcesFor(r.brief);
    1173. 

  1173.   validateMeasurements(r.brief, r.measurements);
    1174. 

  1174.   if (hash(source.sources) !== hash(r.measurements.sources))
    1175. 

  1175.     throw new Error('Finalist sources changed');
    1176. 

  1176.   for (const [key, values] of Object.entries(DESIGN_FIELD_DOMAINS))
    1177. 

  1177.     if (!(values as readonly number[]).includes(r.tokens[key]!))
    1178. 

  1178.       throw new Error(`Invalid finalist token ${key}`);
    1179. 

  1179.   if (Object.keys(r.tokens).length !== Object.keys(DESIGN_FIELD_DOMAINS).length)
    1180. 

  1180.     throw new Error('Finalist must specify exactly the field tokens');
    1181. 

  1181.   const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true });
    1182. 

  1182.   try {
    1183. 

  1183.     const page = await browser.newPage();
    1184. 

  1184.     await page.route('**/*', (route) => route.abort());
    1185. 

  1185.     const scenarios = [];
    1186. 

  1186.     for (const s of DESIGN_FIELD_SCENARIOS) {
    1187. 

  1187.       await page.setViewportSize({ width: s.width, height: s.height });
    1188. 

  1188.       await page.setContent(
    1189. 

  1189.         '<!doctype html><html><head></head><body></body></html>',
    1190. 

  1190.       );
    1191. 

  1191.       const receipt = await page.evaluate(
    1192. 

  1192.         async ({ brief, tokens: t, s, titleFont, bodyFont, image }) => {
    1193. 

  1193.           const pad = s.width < 600 ? 20 : 36,
    1194. 

  1194.             available = Math.min(t.contentMax!, s.width - pad * 2),
    1195. 

  1195.             style = document.createElement('style');
    1196. 

  1196.           style.textContent = `@font-face{font-family:FieldTitle;src:url(data:font/ttf;base64,${titleFont});font-weight:${brief.titleFont.weight}}@font-face{font-family:FieldBody;src:url(data:font/ttf;base64,${bodyFont});font-weight:${brief.bodyFont.weight}}*{box-sizing:border-box}body{margin:0;background:#f3f1e8;color:#17201b}main{width:${available}px;margin:auto;padding-top:${pad}px}h1,p{padding:0;margin:0}h1{font:${brief.titleFont.weight} ${Math.min(t.titleSize!, s.width * 0.135) * s.textScale}px/${brief.typography.titleLeading} FieldTitle;letter-spacing:${brief.typography.titleTrackingEm}em;font-optical-sizing:${brief.titleFont.opticalSizing};font-variation-settings:${
    1197. 

  1197.             Object.entries(brief.titleFont.axes)
    1198. 

  1198.               .map(([tag, value]) => `"${tag}" ${value}`)
    1199. 

  1199.               .join(',') || 'normal'
    1200. 

  1200.           }}p{font:${brief.bodyFont.weight} ${t.bodySize! * s.textScale}px/${t.leading} FieldBody;letter-spacing:${brief.typography.bodyTrackingEm}em;font-optical-sizing:${brief.bodyFont.opticalSizing};font-variation-settings:${
    1201. 

  1201.             Object.entries(brief.bodyFont.axes)
    1202. 

  1202.               .map(([tag, value]) => `"${tag}" ${value}`)
    1203. 

  1203.               .join(',') || 'normal'
    1204. 

  1204.           };width:${Math.min(t.contentMax! * 0.55, available, 680)}px;margin-top:${t.gap}px}button{display:block;margin-top:${t.gap}px;min-height:44px;padding:8px 16px;font:16px FieldBody}img{display:block;width:${available * t.imageShare!}px;aspect-ratio:${t.imageAspect};object-fit:cover;object-position:${brief.image.focalX * 100}% ${brief.image.focalY * 100}%;margin-top:${t.gap}px}`;
    1205. 

  1205.           document.head.append(style);
    1206. 

  1206.           const main = document.createElement('main');
    1207. 

  1207.           for (const [tag, text] of [
    1208. 

  1208.             ['h1', brief.title],
    1209. 

  1209.             ['p', brief.body],
    1210. 

  1210.             ['button', brief.action],
    1211. 

  1211.           ]) {
    1212. 

  1212.             const el = document.createElement(tag!);
    1213. 

  1213.             el.textContent = text!;
    1214. 

  1214.             main.append(el);
    1215. 

  1215.           }
    1216. 

  1216.           const img = new Image();
    1217. 

  1217.           img.src = `data:image/svg+xml;base64,${image}`;
    1218. 

  1218.           img.alt = 'Original still life';
    1219. 

  1219.           main.append(img);
    1220. 

  1220.           document.body.append(main);
    1221. 

  1221.           await Promise.all([
    1222. 

  1222.             document.fonts.load(`${brief.titleFont.weight} 32px FieldTitle`),
    1223. 

  1223.             document.fonts.load(`${brief.bodyFont.weight} 20px FieldBody`),
    1224. 

  1224.             img.decode(),
    1225. 

  1225.           ]);
    1226. 

  1226.           await document.fonts.ready;
    1227. 

  1227.           const box = (el: Element) => {
    1228. 

  1228.             const b = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    1229. 

  1229.             return {
    1230. 

  1230.               x: b.x,
    1231. 

  1231.               y: b.y,
    1232. 

  1232.               width: b.width,
    1233. 

  1233.               height: b.height,
    1234. 

  1234.               right: b.right,
    1235. 

  1235.               bottom: b.bottom,
    1236. 

  1236.             };
    1237. 

  1237.           };
    1238. 

  1238.           const title = box(main.querySelector('h1')!),
    1239. 

  1239.             body = box(main.querySelector('p')!),
    1240. 

  1240.             action = box(main.querySelector('button')!),
    1241. 

  1241.             media = box(img);
    1242. 

  1242.           return {
    1243. 

  1243.             scenario: s.id,
    1244. 

  1244.             viewport: s,
    1245. 

  1245.             title,
    1246. 

  1246.             body,
    1247. 

  1247.             action,
    1248. 

  1248.             image: media,
    1249. 

  1249.             overflowX: Math.max(
    1250. 

  1250.               0,
    1251. 

  1251.               document.documentElement.scrollWidth - s.width,
    1252. 

  1252.             ),
    1253. 

  1253.             imageLoaded: img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0,
    1254. 

  1254.             fontLoaded:
    1255. 

  1255.               document.fonts.check(
    1256. 

  1256.                 `${brief.titleFont.weight} 32px FieldTitle`,
    1257. 

  1257.               ) &&
    1258. 

  1258.               document.fonts.check(`${brief.bodyFont.weight} 20px FieldBody`),
    1259. 

  1259.           };
    1260. 

  1260.         },
    1261. 

  1261.         {
    1262. 

  1262.           brief: r.brief,
    1263. 

  1263.           tokens: r.tokens,
    1264. 

  1264.           s,
    1265. 

  1265.           titleFont: source.bytes.titleFont.toString('base64'),
    1266. 

  1266.           bodyFont: source.bytes.bodyFont.toString('base64'),
    1267. 

  1267.           image: source.bytes.image.toString('base64'),
    1268. 

  1268.         },
    1269. 

  1269.       );
    1270. 

  1270.       const titleKey = `t${r.tokens.titleSize}c${r.tokens.contentMax}`,
    1271. 

  1271.         bodyKey = `b${r.tokens.bodySize}c${r.tokens.contentMax}l${String(r.tokens.leading).replace('.', '_')}`,
    1272. 

  1272.         title = r.measurements.rows.find(
    1273. 

  1273.           (row) => row.scenario === s.id && row.key === titleKey,
    1274. 

  1274.         )!,
    1275. 

  1275.         body = r.measurements.rows.find(
    1276. 

  1276.           (row) => row.scenario === s.id && row.key === bodyKey,
    1277. 

  1277.         )!;
    1278. 

  1278.       const checks = {
    1279. 

  1279.         horizontalContainment: receipt.overflowX <= 0.75,
    1280. 

  1280.         typographyMatchesTable:
    1281. 

  1281.           Math.abs(receipt.title.height - title.height) <= 0.75 &&
    1282. 

  1282.           Math.abs(receipt.body.height - body.height) <= 0.75,
    1283. 

  1283.         actionReach:
    1284. 

  1284.           receipt.action.bottom <=
    1285. 

  1285.           s.height * (s.textScale === 1 ? 0.98 : 1.8) + 0.75,
    1286. 

  1286.         contentLoaded: receipt.imageLoaded && receipt.fontLoaded,
    1287. 

  1287.       };
    1288. 

  1288.       scenarios.push({
    1289. 

  1289.         ...receipt,
    1290. 

  1290.         checks,
    1291. 

  1291.         passed: Object.values(checks).every(Boolean),
    1292. 

  1292.       });
    1293. 

  1293.     }
    1294. 

  1294.     const runtime = `Chromium ${browser.version()}`;
    1295. 

  1295.     return {
    1296. 

  1296.       identity: designFieldValidationIdentity(r, runtime),
    1297. 

  1297.       runtime,
    1298. 

  1298.       status: scenarios.every((s) => s.passed)
    1299. 

  1299.         ? ('passed' as const)
    1300. 

  1300.         : ('failed' as const),
    1301. 

  1301.       scenarios,
    1302. 

  1302.       scope:
    1303. 

  1303.         'Exact field finalist typography/image/action fixture only; layout and source loading verified. Whole gallery, native, physical devices, accessibility, material appearance and motion choreography require separate evidence.',
    1304. 

  1304.     };
    1305. 

  1305.   } finally {
    1306. 

  1306.     await browser.close();
    1307. 

  1307.   }
    1308. 

  1308. }

src/design-context.ts

SHA-256 1b53a8d5166722d46176ff1ac688647900d8966e341a4d69dd7736d4043a04d1

    

  1. import { open } from 'node:fs/promises';
    2. 

  2. import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
    3. 

  3. import path from 'node:path';
    4. 

  4. import { z } from 'zod';
    5. 

  5. 

  6. export const DesignContextSchema = z
    7. 

  7.   .object({
    8. 

  8.     file: z.string().min(1).default('DESIGN.md'),
    9. 

  9.     includeContent: z.boolean().default(false),
    10. 

  10.     expectedSha256: z
    11. 

  11.       .string()
    12. 

  12.       .regex(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/)
    13. 

  13.       .optional(),
    14. 

  14.     requiredSections: z
    15. 

  15.       .array(z.string().trim().min(1).max(120))
    16. 

  16.       .max(32)
    17. 

  17.       .default([]),
    18. 

  18.   })
    19. 

  19.   .strict();
    20. 

  20. 

  21. /** Read an authored contract without guessing tokens, rules or human approval from prose. */
    22. 

  22. export async function inspectDesignContext(input: unknown) {
    23. 

  23.   const r = DesignContextSchema.parse(input);
    24. 

  24.   const file = path.resolve(r.file);
    25. 

  25.   let bytes: Buffer;
    26. 

  26.   try {
    27. 

  27.     const handle = await open(file, 'r');
    28. 

  28.     try {
    29. 

  29.       const buffer = Buffer.alloc(65537);
    30. 

  30.       let used = 0;
    31. 

  31.       while (used < buffer.length) {
    32. 

  32.         const result = await handle.read(
    33. 

  33.           buffer,
    34. 

  34.           used,
    35. 

  35.           buffer.length - used,
    36. 

  36.           null,
    37. 

  37.         );
    38. 

  38.         if (!result.bytesRead) break;
    39. 

  39.         used += result.bytesRead;
    40. 

  40.       }
    41. 

  41.       bytes = buffer.subarray(0, used);
    42. 

  42.     } finally {
    43. 

  43.       await handle.close();
    44. 

  44.     }
    45. 

  45.   } catch (error) {
    46. 

  46.     if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== 'ENOENT') throw error;
    47. 

  47.     return {
    48. 

  48.       status: 'missing' as const,
    49. 

  49.       file,
    50. 

  50.       sha256: null,
    51. 

  51.       sections: [],
    52. 

  52.       missingSections: r.requiredSections,
    53. 

  53.       scope:
    54. 

  54.         'No design contract found. Establish a brief before authoring DESIGN.md; do not invent approval.',
    55. 

  55.     };
    56. 

  56.   }
    57. 

  57.   if (bytes.length > 65536)
    58. 

  58.     throw new Error(
    59. 

  59.       'Design context exceeds 64 KiB; use a focused DESIGN.md and linked references',
    60. 

  60.     );
    61. 

  61.   const content = new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true }).decode(bytes);
    62. 

  62.   const sha256 = createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');
    63. 

  63.   if (r.expectedSha256 && r.expectedSha256 !== sha256)
    64. 

  64.     throw new Error(
    65. 

  65.       'Design context changed; invalidate dependent choices and inspect the new contract',
    66. 

  66.     );
    67. 

  67.   const sections: { title: string; level: number; line: number }[] = [];
    68. 

  68.   let fence: string | null = null;
    69. 

  69.   for (const [index, line] of content.split(/\r?\n/u).entries()) {
    70. 

  70.     const marker = line.match(/^\s{0,3}(`{3,}|~{3,})/u)?.[1];
    71. 

  71.     if (marker) {
    72. 

  72.       if (!fence) fence = marker;
    73. 

  73.       else if (
    74. 

  74.         marker[0] === fence[0] &&
    75. 

  75.         marker.length >= fence.length &&
    76. 

  76.         /^\s{0,3}[`~]+\s*$/u.test(line)
    77. 

  77.       )
    78. 

  78.         fence = null;
    79. 

  79.       continue;
    80. 

  80.     }
    81. 

  81.     if (fence) continue;
    82. 

  82.     const heading = line.match(/^\s{0,3}(#{1,6})\s+(.+?)(?:\s+#+)?\s*$/u);
    83. 

  83.     if (heading)
    84. 

  84.       sections.push({
    85. 

  85.         title: heading[2]!,
    86. 

  86.         level: heading[1]!.length,
    87. 

  87.         line: index + 1,
    88. 

  88.       });
    89. 

  89.   }
    90. 

  90.   const names = new Set(sections.map((s) => s.title.toLowerCase()));
    91. 

  91.   return {
    92. 

  92.     status: 'found' as const,
    93. 

  93.     file,
    94. 

  94.     sha256,
    95. 

  95.     byteLength: bytes.length,
    96. 

  96.     sections: sections.slice(0, 64),
    97. 

  97.     totalSections: sections.length,
    98. 

  98.     omittedSections: Math.max(0, sections.length - 64),
    99. 

  99.     missingSections: r.requiredSections.filter(
    100. 

  100.       (s) => !names.has(s.toLowerCase()),
    101. 

  101.     ),
    102. 

  102.     ...(r.includeContent ? { content } : {}),
    103. 

  103.     scope:
    104. 

  104.       'Authored design contract and document structure only. No prose-to-constraint inference, aesthetic validity or human approval is implied. Bind its hash to dependent workflows.',
    105. 

  105.   };
    106. 

  106. }
    107.

  88. export async function selectChoice(
    89. 

  89.   input: z.input<typeof ChoiceRequestSchema>,
    90. 

  90.   renderer: Renderer,
    91. 

  91.   execution: SelectorExecutionOptions = {},
    92. 

  92. ) {
    93. 

  93.   execution.signal?.throwIfAborted();
    94. 

  94.   const request = ChoiceRequestSchema.parse(input);
    95. 

  95.   const profile = selectorProfiles[request.profile];
    96. 

  96.   if (
    97. 

  97.     new Set(request.candidates.map((c) => c.id)).size !==
    98. 

  98.     request.candidates.length
    99. 

  99.   )
    100. 

  100.     throw new Error('candidate ids must be unique');
    101. 

  101.   const fixedContent = (state: DesignState) =>
    102. 

  102.     hash(
    103. 

  103.       state.blocks.map((b) => ({
    104. 

  104.         id: b.id,
    105. 

  105.         role: b.role,
    106. 

  106.         text: b.text,
    107. 

  107.         language: b.language,
    108. 

  108.       })),
    109. 

  109.     );
    110.

    111. 

  111.     request.candidates.some(
    112. 

  112.       (c) =>
    113. 

  113.         fixedContent(c.state) !== fixedContent(request.candidates[0]!.state),
    114. 

  114.     )
    115. 

  115.   )
    116. 

  116.     throw new Error(
    117. 

  117.       'art-direction alternatives must preserve the same ordered content, roles and language',
    118. 

  118.     );

    120. 

  120.   const verified: {

    123. 

  123.     engine: string;
    124. 

  124.     scenarios: number;
    125.

    126. 

  126.   const rejected: { id: string; reasons: unknown[] }[] = [];
    127. 

  127.   const geometry = new Map<string, unknown>();
    128. 

  128.   // Never accept a caller's feasible flag or a model assertion in place of a fresh renderer receipt.
    129. 

  129.   for (const candidate of request.candidates) {
    130. 

  130.     execution.signal?.throwIfAborted();
    131. 

  131.     const reasons: unknown[] = [];
    132. 

  132.     const base = await renderer.measure(candidate.state, { spacing: true });
    133. 

  133.     const observations = [base];
    134. 

  134.     if (!base.feasible) reasons.push({ scenario: 'base', issues: base.issues });
    135. 

  135.     for (const width of request.widths)
    136. 

  136.       for (const textScale of request.textScales) {
    137.

  138.         s.viewport = { ...s.viewport, width, textScale };
    139. 

  139.         StateSchema.parse(s);

    141. 

  141.         observations.push(m);
    142. 

  142.         if (!m.feasible) reasons.push({ width, textScale, issues: m.issues });
    143. 

  143.       }

    159. 

  159.           maxLines: Math.max(
    160. 

  160.             ...observations.map((m) => m.blocks[index]!.lines.length),
    161. 

  161.           ),
    162. 

  162.         })),
    163. 

  163.       });
    164.

    166.

  284. 

  285. export async function checkpointDigest(file: string, signal?: AbortSignal) {
    286. 

  286.   const digest = createHash('sha256');
    287. 

  287.   for await (const chunk of createReadStream(file, { signal }))
    288. 

  288.     digest.update(chunk);
    289. 

  289.   return digest.digest('hex');
    290. 

  290. }

  292. export interface LocalOptionsPayload {

  297. export interface LocalOptionsRequest {
    298. 

  298.   payload: LocalOptionsPayload;
    299. 

  299.   gguf: string;
    300. 

  300.   profile: z.infer<typeof SelectorProfileSchema>;
    301. 

  301.   readout: 'single' | 'balanced';
    302. 

  302.   maxPromptTokens: number;
    303. 

  303.   auditDirectory?: string | undefined;
    304. 

  304. }
    305. 

  305. export async function selectLocalOptions(
    306. 

  306.   request: LocalOptionsRequest,
    307. 

  307.   execution: SelectorExecutionOptions = {},
    308. 

  308. ) {
    309. 

  309.   const result = await selectPinnedLocalOptions(request, undefined, execution);
    310. 

  310.   notifySelector(execution, { phase: 'completed', origin: 'process' });
    311. 

  311.   return result;
    312. 

  312. }
    313. 

  313. 

  314. /** One checkpoint digest per session; metadata and returned provenance guard every readout. */
    315. 

  315. export async function prepareLocalOptionsSession(
    316. 

  316.   gguf: string,
    317. 

  317.   execution: SelectorExecutionOptions = {},
    318. 

  318. ) {
    319. 

  319.   execution.signal?.throwIfAborted();
    320. 

  320.   const file = path.resolve(gguf);
    321. 

  321.   const signature = async () => {
    322. 

  322.     const value = await stat(file, { bigint: true });
    323. 

  323.     return `${value.dev}:${value.ino}:${value.size}:${value.mtimeNs}:${value.ctimeNs}`;
    324. 

  324.   };
    325. 

  325.   const before = await signature();
    326. 

  326.   notifySelector(execution, { phase: 'checkpoint-hash', origin: 'process' });
    327. 

  327.   const checkpointHash = await checkpointDigest(file, execution.signal);
    328. 

  328.   if (before !== (await signature()))
    329. 

  329.     throw new Error('Checkpoint changed while hashing');
    330. 

  330.   return {
    331. 

  331.     checkpointHash,
    332. 

  332.     async select(
    333. 

  333.       request: LocalOptionsRequest,
    334. 

  334.       options?: SelectorExecutionOptions,
    335. 

  335.     ) {
    336. 

  336.       if (path.resolve(request.gguf) !== file || before !== (await signature()))
    337. 

  337.         throw new Error('Prepared selector checkpoint changed');
    338. 

  338.       const result = await selectPinnedLocalOptions(request, checkpointHash, {
    339. 

  339.         ...execution,
    340. 

  340.         ...options,
    341. 

  341.       });
    342. 

  342.       if (before !== (await signature()))
    343. 

  343.         throw new Error('Prepared selector checkpoint changed during readout');
    344. 

  344.       notifySelector(
    345. 

  345.         { ...execution, ...options },
    346. 

  346.         { phase: 'completed', origin: 'process' },
    347. 

  347.       );
    348. 

  348.       return result;
    349. 

  349.     },
    350. 

  350.   };
    351. 

  351. }
    352. 

  352. async function selectPinnedLocalOptions(
    353. 

  353.   request: LocalOptionsRequest,
    354. 

  354.   pinnedCheckpointHash?: string,
    355. 

  355.   execution: SelectorExecutionOptions = {},
    356. 

  356. ) {
    357. 

  357.   execution.signal?.throwIfAborted();
    358. 

  358.   const profile = selectorProfiles[request.profile],
    359. 

  359.     readout = request.readout;
    360. 

  360.   if (
    361. 

  361.     !request.payload.options.length ||
    362.

    363. 

  363.     new Set(request.payload.options.map((o) => o.id)).size !==
    364. 

  364.       request.payload.options.length
    365.

  367.   if (readout === 'balanced' && request.payload.options.length > 6)

  369.   const ordered =
    370. 

  370.     readout === 'balanced'
    371. 

  371.       ? [...request.payload.options].sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id))
    372. 

  372.       : request.payload.options;

    374.

    379.

    380.

    381. 

  381.     checkpointHash,
    382. 

  382.     readout,
    383. 

  383.     profile: request.profile,
    384. 

  384.     maxPromptTokens: request.maxPromptTokens,
    385. 

  385.     payload,
    386. 

  386.   });
    387. 

  387.   const row = { id, ...payload };
    388. 

  388.   const rows =
    389. 

  389.     readout === 'single'
    390. 

  390.       ? [row]
    391. 

  391.       : ordered.map((_, offset) => ({
    392. 

  392.           ...row,
    393. 

  393.           id: hash({ id, rotation: offset }),
    394. 

  394.           options: [
    395. 

  395.             ...row.options.slice(offset),
    396. 

  396.             ...row.options.slice(0, offset),
    397. 

  397.           ],
    398. 

  398.         }));

  399.   const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'physics-semif-'));
    400. 

  400.   const auditDirectory = request.auditDirectory ?? dir;
    401. 

  401.   const diagnostic = (suffix: string) =>
    402. 

  402.     path.join(auditDirectory, `${id}.${suffix}`);
    403. 

  403.   let validatingOutput = false,
    404. 

  404.     retainFailure = false;
    405. 

  405.   try {
    406. 

  406.     const inputFile = path.join(dir, 'input.jsonl'),
    407. 

  407.       outputFile = path.join(dir, 'output.jsonl');
    408. 

  408.     const inputRows =
    409. 

  409.       rows.map((value) => JSON.stringify(value)).join('\n') + '\n';
    410. 

  410.     await mkdir(auditDirectory, { recursive: true });
    411. 

  411.     await writeFile(inputFile, inputRows);
    412. 

  412.     await writeFile(diagnostic('input.jsonl'), inputRows);
    413. 

  413.     const start = performance.now();
    414. 

  414.     const processResult = await streamSelectorProcess({
    415. 

  415.       executable: process.env.DESIGN_SEMIF_BIN ?? 'semif-score',
    416. 

  416.       args: [
    417. 

  417.         '--mode',
    418. 

  418.         'direct',
    419. 

  419.         '--backend',
    420. 

  420.         'llamacpp',
    421. 

  421.         '--model',
    422. 

  422.         profile.model,
    423. 

  423.         '--revision',
    424. 

  424.         profile.revision,
    425. 

  425.         '--gguf',
    426. 

  426.         path.resolve(request.gguf),
    427. 

  427.         '--input',
    428. 

  428.         inputFile,
    429. 

  429.         '--output',
    430. 

  430.         outputFile,
    431. 

  431.         '--max-tokens',
    432. 

  432.         String(request.maxPromptTokens),
    433. 

  433.         '--llama-threads',
    434. 

  434.         '8',
    435. 

  435.       ],
    436.

    437. 

  437.       stderrFile: diagnostic('stderr.txt'),
    438. 

  438.       rowIds: rows.map((row) => row.id),
    439. 

  439.       maxPromptTokens: request.maxPromptTokens,
    440. 

  440.       options: execution,
    441. 

  441.     });
    442. 

  442.     await writeFile(
    443. 

  443.       diagnostic('process.json'),
    444.

    445. 

  445.     );
    446. 

  446.     // Preserve partial provider output even when it exits or is cancelled mid-readout.
    447. 

  447.     try {
    448. 

  448.       await copyFile(outputFile, diagnostic('output.jsonl'));
    449. 

  449.     } catch (error) {
    450. 

  450.       if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== 'ENOENT') throw error;
    451. 

  451.     }
    452. 

  452.     if (
    453. 

  453.       processResult.cancelled ||
    454. 

  454.       processResult.code !== 0 ||
    455. 

  455.       processResult.processError
    456. 

  456.     ) {
    457. 

  457.       const phase = processResult.cancelled ? 'cancelled' : 'process';
    458. 

  458.       await writeFile(
    459. 

  459.         diagnostic('failure.json'),
    460. 

  460.         JSON.stringify({
    461. 

  461.           phase,
    462. 

  462.           ...processResult,
    463. 

  463.           note: 'No completed selection; raw pipes and any partial readout are preserved.',
    464. 

  464.         }) + '\n',
    465. 

  465.       );
    466. 

  466.       const error = new Error(
    467. 

  467.         `Local selector ${processResult.cancelled ? 'cancelled' : 'process failed'} (code=${processResult.code ?? 'none'}, signal=${processResult.signal ?? 'none'}). Diagnostics: ${diagnostic('failure.json')}`,
    468. 

  468.       );
    469. 

  469.       if (processResult.cancelled) error.name = 'AbortError';
    470. 

  470.       throw error;
    471. 

  471.     }
    472. 

  472.     validatingOutput = true;
    473. 

  473.     notifySelector(execution, { phase: 'validating', origin: 'process' });
    474. 

  474.     execution.signal?.throwIfAborted();
    475. 

  475.     // Preserve the raw file, but never load an unbounded provider result into memory.
    476. 

  476.     if ((await stat(outputFile)).size > 16 * 1024 * 1024)
    477. 

  477.       throw new Error('Selector readout exceeds the 16 MiB JSON result limit');
    478. 

  478.     const raw = await readFile(outputFile, 'utf8');
    479. 

  479.     const lines = raw.trim().split('\n');
    480. 

  480.     if (lines.length !== rows.length)
    481. 

  481.       throw new Error(
    482. 

  482.         'SemIf must return exactly one result per declared readout row',
    483. 

  483.       );
    484. 

  484.     const results = lines.map((line) => SemifResult.parse(JSON.parse(line)));
    485. 

  485.     for (const [index, result] of results.entries()) {
    486. 

  486.       if (
    487. 

  487.         result.id !== rows[index]!.id ||
    488. 

  488.         result.model.source !== profile.model ||
    489. 

  489.         result.model.revision !== profile.revision ||
    490. 

  490.         result.model.gguf.sha256 !== checkpointHash ||
    491. 

  491.         result.option_ids.length !== ordered.length ||
    492. 

  492.         new Set(result.option_ids).size !== ordered.length ||
    493. 

  493.         result.option_ids.some(
    494. 

  494.           (x) => !ordered.some((candidate) => candidate.id === x),
    495.

    497.

    499. 

  499.         Math.abs(

  500.           result.probabilities.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) - 1,
    501.

    502.

    504. 

  504.           'SemIf returned mismatched candidate/model provenance or malformed option probabilities',
    505.

    506.

  507.     const useLogits = results.every(
    508. 

  508.       (result) => result.option_logits !== undefined,
    509. 

  509.     );
    510. 

  510.     const scores = new Map(ordered.map((candidate) => [candidate.id, 0]));
    511. 

  511.     for (const result of results)
    512. 

  512.       for (const [index, candidateId] of result.option_ids.entries()) {
    513. 

  513.         const probability = result.probabilities[index]!;
    514. 

  514.         if (readout === 'balanced' && !useLogits && probability === 0)
    515. 

  515.           throw new Error(
    516. 

  516.             'Balanced readout needs finite option logits or strictly positive probabilities; zero readouts are not silently floored.',
    517. 

  517.           );
    518. 

  518.         const value =
    519. 

  519.           readout === 'single'
    520.

    521. 

  521.             : useLogits

  523.               : Math.log(probability);
    524. 

  524.         scores.set(
    525. 

  525.           candidateId,
    526. 

  526.           scores.get(candidateId)! + value / results.length,
    527. 

  527.         );
    528. 

  528.       }
    529. 

  529.     const selected = ordered.reduce((best, candidate) =>
    530. 

  530.       scores.get(candidate.id)! > scores.get(best.id)! ? candidate : best,
    531. 

  531.     ).id;
    532. 

  532.     const result = results[0]!;
    533. 

  533.     execution.signal?.throwIfAborted();

  534.     if (request.auditDirectory) {
    535.

    536. 

  536.       await writeFile(
    537. 

  537.         path.join(request.auditDirectory, `${id}.input.jsonl`),
    538. 

  538.         rows.map((value) => JSON.stringify(value)).join('\n') + '\n',
    539. 

  539.       );
    540. 

  540.       await writeFile(
    541. 

  541.         path.join(request.auditDirectory, `${id}.output.jsonl`),
    542. 

  542.         raw,
    543. 

  543.       );
    544. 

  544.     }
    545. 

  545.     return {
    546. 

  546.       selected,
    547. 

  547.       provenance: {
    548. 

  548.         profile: request.profile,
    549. 

  549.         readoutMode: readout,
    550. 

  550.         readoutPasses: rows.length,
    551. 

  551.         aggregation:
    552. 

  552.           readout === 'single'
    553. 

  553.             ? 'single restricted-option readout'
    554. 

  554.             : useLogits
    555. 

  555.               ? 'mean option logits across canonical cyclic rotations'
    556. 

  556.               : 'mean log option probability across canonical cyclic rotations',
    557. 

  557.         promptHashes: results.map((value) => value.prompt_sha256),
    558. 

  558.         experimental: profile.experimental,
    559. 

  559.         model: result.model,
    560. 

  560.         promptHash: result.prompt_sha256,
    561. 

  561.         readout: result.readout,
    562. 

  562.         latencyMs: performance.now() - start,
    563. 

  563.         execution: processResult,
    564. 

  564.       },
    565. 

  565.       caveat:
    566. 

  566.         'Option logits are used only to select a viable alternative. No confidence, validity or aesthetic score is exposed.',
    567. 

  567.     };
    568. 

  568.   } catch (error) {
    569. 

  569.     retainFailure = true;
    570. 

  570.     const cancelled =
    571. 

  571.       execution.signal?.aborted ||
    572. 

  572.       (error instanceof Error && error.name === 'AbortError');
    573. 

  573.     notifySelector(execution, {
    574. 

  574.       phase: cancelled ? 'cancelled' : 'failed',
    575. 

  575.       origin: 'process',
    576. 

  576.     });
    577. 

  577.     if (validatingOutput) {
    578. 

  578.       const reason =
    579. 

  579.         error instanceof Error
    580. 

  580.           ? error.message.slice(0, 500)
    581. 

  581.           : String(error).slice(0, 500);
    582. 

  582.       await writeFile(
    583. 

  583.         diagnostic('failure.json'),
    584. 

  584.         JSON.stringify({
    585. 

  585.           phase: cancelled ? 'cancelled' : 'output-validation',
    586. 

  586.           reason,
    587. 

  587.           note: 'No candidate was selected. Raw process output remains separate from compact context.',
    588. 

  588.         }) + '\n',
    589. 

  589.       );
    590. 

  590.       const failure = new Error(
    591. 

  591.         `Local selector ${cancelled ? 'cancelled' : 'output validation failed'}: ${reason} Diagnostics: ${diagnostic('failure.json')}`,
    592. 

  592.       );
    593. 

  593.       if (cancelled) failure.name = 'AbortError';
    594. 

  594.       throw failure;
    595. 

  595.     }
    596. 

  596.     throw error;
    597. 

  597.   } finally {
    598. 

  598.     if (!retainFailure || request.auditDirectory)
    599. 

  599.       await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
    600. 

  600.   }
    601. 

  601. }

  1. import { JsonExpansionBudget } from './json-expansion-budget.js';
    2. 

  2. /** Bounded session references avoid repeating content and renderer receipts in model context. */
    3. 

  3. export class ResultStore {
    4. 

  4.   private results = new Map<string, { value: unknown; bytes: number }>();
    5. 

  5.   private bytes = 0;
    6. 

  6.   private sequence = 0;
    7. 

  7.   constructor(
    8. 

  8.     private maxBytes = 16 * 1024 * 1024,
    9. 

  9.     private maxEntries = 32,
    10. 

  10.   ) {}
    11. 

  11.   put(value: unknown): string | null {
    12. 

  12.     const serialized = JSON.stringify(value),
    13. 

  13.       bytes = Buffer.byteLength(serialized);
    14. 

  14.     if (bytes > this.maxBytes) return null;
    15. 

  15.     while (
    16. 

  16.       this.results.size &&
    17. 

  17.       (this.bytes + bytes > this.maxBytes ||
    18. 

  18.         this.results.size >= this.maxEntries)
    19. 

  19.     ) {
    20. 

  20.       const key = this.results.keys().next().value!;
    21. 

  21.       this.bytes -= this.results.get(key)!.bytes;
    22. 

  22.       this.results.delete(key);
    23. 

  23.     }
    24. 

  24.     const id = `pod:${++this.sequence}`;
    25. 

  25.     this.results.set(id, { value: JSON.parse(serialized), bytes });
    26. 

  26.     this.bytes += bytes;
    27. 

  27.     return id;
    28. 

  28.   }
    29. 

  29.   resolve(value: unknown): unknown {
    30. 

  30.     return this.resolveValue(value, new JsonExpansionBudget(), 0);
    31. 

  31.   }
    32. 

  32.   private resolveValue(
    33. 

  33.     value: unknown,
    34. 

  34.     budget: JsonExpansionBudget,
    35. 

  35.     depth: number,
    36. 

  36.   ): unknown {
    37. 

  37.     if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    38. 

  38.       budget.add(value, depth);
    39. 

  39.       return value.map((item) => this.resolveValue(item, budget, depth + 1));
    40. 

  40.     }
    41. 

  41.     if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') {
    42. 

  42.       budget.add(value, depth);
    43. 

  43.       return value;
    44. 

  44.     }
    45. 

  45.     const object = value as Record<string, unknown>;
    46. 

  46.     if (Object.hasOwn(object, '$designRef')) {
    47. 

  47.       if (
    48. 

  48.         Object.keys(object).some(
    49. 

  49.           (key) => !['$designRef', 'pointer'].includes(key),
    50. 

  50.         )
    51. 

  51.       )
    52. 

  52.         throw new Error(
    53. 

  53.           'A result reference contains only $designRef and pointer',
    54. 

  54.         );
    55. 

  55.       const entry = this.results.get(String(object.$designRef));
    56. 

  56.       if (!entry)
    57. 

  57.         throw new Error(
    58. 

  58.           'Unknown or evicted session result; execute again or supply explicit JSON',
    59. 

  59.         );
    60. 

  60.       const pointer = object.pointer ?? '';
    61. 

  61.       if (
    62. 

  62.         typeof pointer !== 'string' ||
    63. 

  63.         (pointer !== '' && !pointer.startsWith('/')) ||
    64. 

  64.         /~(?![01])/u.test(pointer)
    65. 

  65.       )
    66. 

  66.         throw new Error('Result pointer must be an RFC 6901 JSON pointer');
    67. 

  67.       let selected = entry.value;
    68. 

  68.       if (pointer !== '')
    69. 

  69.         for (const part of pointer.slice(1).split('/')) {
    70. 

  70.           const key = part.replaceAll('~1', '/').replaceAll('~0', '~');
    71. 

  71.           if (
    72. 

  72.             !selected ||
    73. 

  73.             typeof selected !== 'object' ||
    74. 

  74.             !Object.hasOwn(selected, key)
    75. 

  75.           )
    76. 

  76.             throw new Error(`Result pointer does not exist: ${pointer}`);
    77. 

  77.           selected = (selected as Record<string, unknown>)[key];
    78. 

  78.         }
    79. 

  79.       return budget.clone(selected, depth);
    80. 

  80.     }
    81. 

  81.     budget.add(value, depth);
    82. 

  82.     return Object.fromEntries(
    83. 

  83.       Object.entries(object).map(([key, item]) => [
    84. 

  84.         key,
    85. 

  85.         this.resolveValue(item, budget, depth + 1),
    86. 

  86.       ]),
    87. 

  87.     );
    88. 

  88.   }
    89. 

  89. }
    90. 

  90. const record = (value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> =>
    91. 

  91.   value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)
    92. 

  92.     ? (value as Record<string, unknown>)
    93. 

  93.     : {};
    94. 

  94. const rows = (value: unknown): Record<string, unknown>[] =>
    95. 

  95.   Array.isArray(value) ? value.map(record) : [];
    96. 

  96. const compactText = (value: unknown, max = 160) =>
    97. 

  97.   typeof value === 'string' ? value.slice(0, max) : undefined;
    98. 

  98. const finiteNumber = (value: unknown) =>
    99. 

  99.   typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : undefined;
    100. 

  100. const boolean = (value: unknown) =>
    101. 

  101.   typeof value === 'boolean' ? value : undefined;
    102. 

  102. const missingSummary = (value: unknown) => {
    103. 

  103.   const entries = Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];
    104. 

  104.   return {
    105. 

  105.     missing: entries.slice(0, 16),
    106. 

  106.     missingCount: entries.length,
    107. 

  107.     omittedMissing: Math.max(0, entries.length - 16),
    108. 

  108.   };
    109. 

  109. };
    110. 

  110. const retentionSummary = (value: unknown) => {
    111. 

  111.   const r = record(value);
    112. 

  112.   if (!Object.keys(r).length) return null;
    113. 

  113.   const preferences = rows(r.preferences);
    114. 

  114.   return {
    115. 

  115.     policy: compactText(r.policy),
    116. 

  116.     admissibleAssignments: finiteNumber(r.admissibleAssignments),
    117. 

  117.     preferenceProfiles: finiteNumber(r.preferenceProfiles),
    118. 

  118.     retainedPreferenceProfiles: finiteNumber(r.retainedPreferenceProfiles),
    119. 

  119.     paretoProfiles:
    120. 

  120.       r.paretoProfiles === null ? null : finiteNumber(r.paretoProfiles),
    121. 

  121.     paretoComplete: boolean(r.paretoComplete),
    122. 

  122.     diversityComplete: boolean(r.diversityComplete),
    123. 

  123.     comparisons: finiteNumber(r.comparisons),
    124. 

  124.     maxComparisons: finiteNumber(r.maxComparisons),
    125. 

  125.     minimumEnergyAssignments: finiteNumber(r.minimumEnergyAssignments),
    126. 

  126.     jointlySatisfiedAssignments: finiteNumber(r.jointlySatisfiedAssignments),
    127. 

  127.     fallback: compactText(r.fallback),
    128. 

  128.     individuallyUnreachable: preferences
    129. 

  129.       .filter((p) => p.satisfiedAssignments === 0)
    130. 

  130.       .slice(0, 8)
    131. 

  131.       .map((p) => ({
    132. 

  132.         id: compactText(p.id),
    133. 

  133.         scenario: compactText(p.scenario),
    134. 

  134.         observedRange: Array.isArray(p.observedRange)
    135. 

  135.           ? p.observedRange.slice(0, 2).map(finiteNumber)
    136. 

  136.           : [],
    137. 

  137.         minimumResidual: finiteNumber(p.minimumResidual),
    138. 

  138.       })),
    139. 

  139.     individuallyUnreachableCount: preferences.filter(
    140. 

  140.       (p) => p.satisfiedAssignments === 0,
    141. 

  141.     ).length,
    142. 

  142.     scope: compactText(r.scope, 400),
    143. 

  143.   };
    144. 

  144. };
    145. 

  145. const stageEvidenceSummary = (value: unknown) => {
    146. 

  146.   const evidence = record(value),
    147. 

  147.     search = record(evidence.search),
    148. 

  148.     lookahead = record(evidence.lookahead);
    149. 

  149.   return {
    150. 

  150.     retention: retentionSummary(search.retention),
    151. 

  151.     reusedLookaheadSearch: boolean(evidence.reusedLookaheadSearch) ?? false,
    152. 

  152.     lookahead: Object.keys(lookahead).length
    153. 

  153.       ? {
    154. 

  154.           engine: compactText(lookahead.engine),
    155. 

  155.           policy: {
    156. 

  156.             completion: compactText(record(lookahead.policy).completion),
    157. 

  157.             parentTies: compactText(record(lookahead.policy).parentTies),
    158. 

  158.             maxBranches: finiteNumber(record(lookahead.policy).maxBranches),
    159. 

  159.             maxEvaluations: finiteNumber(
    160. 

  160.               record(lookahead.policy).maxEvaluations,
    161. 

  161.             ),
    162. 

  162.           },
    163. 

  163.           uncoveredParents: Array.isArray(lookahead.uncoveredParents)
    164. 

  164.             ? lookahead.uncoveredParents.slice(0, 8).map((v) => compactText(v))
    165. 

  165.             : [],
    166. 

  166.           branches: rows(lookahead.branches)
    167. 

  167.             .slice(0, 8)
    168. 

  168.             .map((b) => ({
    169. 

  169.               parent: compactText(record(b.parent).id),
    170. 

  170.               completion: compactText(b.completion),
    171. 

  171.               jointlySatisfied: boolean(b.jointlySatisfied),
    172. 

  172.               successors: rows(b.successors)
    173. 

  173.                 .slice(0, 8)
    174. 

  174.                 .map((s) => ({
    175. 

  175.                   stage: compactText(s.stage),
    176. 

  176.                   outcome: compactText(s.outcome),
    177. 

  177.                   complete: boolean(
    178. 

  178.                     record(record(s.search).statistics).complete,
    179. 

  179.                   ),
    180. 

  180.                   witness:
    181. 

  181.                     compactText(record(record(s.search).witness).id) ?? null,
    182. 

  182.                   preferenceTargetsSatisfied:
    183. 

  183.                     boolean(
    184. 

  184.                       record(record(s.search).witness)
    185. 

  185.                         .preferenceTargetsSatisfied,
    186. 

  186.                     ) ?? null,
    187. 

  187.                 })),
    188. 

  188.             })),
    189. 

  189.           scope: compactText(lookahead.scope, 400),
    190. 

  190.         }
    191. 

  191.       : null,
    192. 

  192.   };
    193. 

  193. };
    194. 

  194. // Recipes are caller supplied and integrity hashes do not authenticate metadata.
    195. 

  195. // Keep arbitrary notes/prompts in the exact receipt, never in compact context.
    196. 

  196. const selectionSummary = (value: unknown) => {
    197. 

  197.   if (value === null || value === undefined) return null;
    198. 

  198.   const s = record(value),
    199. 

  199.     p = record(s.provenance),
    200. 

  200.     m = record(p.model);
    201. 

  201.   const text = (v: unknown, max = 160) =>
    202. 

  202.     typeof v === 'string' ? v.slice(0, max) : undefined;
    203. 

  203.   const number = (v: unknown) =>
    204. 

  204.     typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) ? v : undefined;
    205. 

  205.   const bool = (v: unknown) => (typeof v === 'boolean' ? v : undefined);
    206. 

  206.   return {
    207. 

  207.     selected: text(s.selected),
    208. 

  208.     policy: text(s.policy, 240),
    209. 

  209.     modelCalled: bool(s.modelCalled),
    210. 

  210.     testInjected: bool(s.testInjected),
    211. 

  211.     requestedReadoutPasses: number(s.requestedReadoutPasses),
    212. 

  212.     provenance: {
    213. 

  213.       profile: text(p.profile),
    214. 

  214.       readoutMode: text(p.readoutMode),
    215. 

  215.       readoutPasses: number(p.readoutPasses),
    216. 

  216.       promptHash: text(p.promptHash, 64),
    217. 

  217.       model: {
    218. 

  218.         source: text(m.source),
    219. 

  219.         revision: text(m.revision),
    220. 

  220.         backend: text(m.backend),
    221. 

  221.         ggufSha256: text(record(m.gguf).sha256, 64),
    222. 

  222.       },
    223. 

  223.       latencyMs: number(p.latencyMs),
    224. 

  224.     },
    225. 

  225.   };
    226. 

  226. };
    227. 

  227. export function summarizeResult(
    228. 

  228.   command: string,
    229. 

  229.   result: unknown,
    230. 

  230.   resultRef: string | null,
    231. 

  231. ): { resultRef: string | null; summary: unknown; referenceScope: string } {
    232. 

  232.   const r = record(result);
    233. 

  233.   let summary: unknown = result;
    234. 

  234.   if (command === 'context inspect')
    235. 

  235.     summary = {
    236. 

  236.       status: r.status,
    237. 

  237.       file: r.file,
    238. 

  238.       sha256: r.sha256,
    239. 

  239.       byteLength: r.byteLength,
    240. 

  240.       sections: rows(r.sections)
    241. 

  241.         .slice(0, 32)
    242. 

  242.         .map((s) => ({
    243. 

  243.           title: String(s.title).slice(0, 160),
    244. 

  244.           titleCharacters: String(s.title).length,
    245. 

  245.           level: s.level,
    246. 

  246.           line: s.line,
    247. 

  247.         })),
    248. 

  248.       summarizedSections: Math.min(32, rows(r.sections).length),
    249. 

  249.       totalSections: r.totalSections,
    250. 

  250.       omittedSections: r.omittedSections,
    251. 

  251.       missingSections: r.missingSections,
    252. 

  252.       scope: r.scope,
    253. 

  253.     };
    254. 

  254.   else if (command === 'field measure')
    255. 

  255.     summary = {
    256. 

  256.       identity: r.identity,
    257. 

  257.       sourceIdentity: r.sourceIdentity,
    258. 

  258.       provider: r.provider,
    259. 

  259.       runtime: r.runtime,
    260. 

  260.       sources: r.sources,
    261. 

  261.       cells: rows(r.rows).length,
    262. 

  262.       image: r.image,
    263. 

  263.       policy: r.policy,
    264. 

  264.     };
    265. 

  265.   else if (command === 'kernel compile')
    266. 

  266.     summary = {
    267. 

  267.       identity: r.identity,
    268. 

  268.       inputIdentity: r.inputIdentity,
    269. 

  269.       stages: rows(record(r.workflow).stages).map((s) => ({
    270. 

  270.         id: s.id,
    271. 

  271.         dependsOn: s.dependsOn,
    272. 

  272.         selection: s.selection,
    273. 

  273.       })),
    274. 

  274.       outputs: rows(r.outputs).map((o) => ({
    275. 

  275.         stage: o.stage,
    276. 

  276.         name: o.name,
    277. 

  277.         unit: o.unit,
    278. 

  278.       })),
    279. 

  279.       obligationCount: rows(r.obligations).length,
    280. 

  280.       scope: r.scope,
    281. 

  281.       workflow: resultRef
    282. 

  282.         ? { $designRef: resultRef, pointer: '/workflow' }
    283. 

  283.         : null,
    284. 

  284.     };
    285. 

  285.   else if (command === 'bindings check')
    286. 

  286.     summary = {
    287. 

  287.       identity: r.identity ?? r.manifestIdentity,
    288. 

  288.       current: r.current,
    289. 

  289.       complete: r.complete ?? false,
    290. 

  290.       verified: r.verified ?? false,
    291. 

  291.       entries: rows(r.entries)
    292. 

  292.         .slice(0, 24)
    293. 

  293.         .map((e) => ({
    294. 

  294.           id: e.id,
    295. 

  295.           status: e.status ?? 'expected-only',
    296. 

  296.           unit: e.unit,
    297. 

  297.           target: e.target,
    298. 

  298.         })),
    299. 

  299.       entryCount: rows(r.entries).length,
    300. 

  300.       omittedEntries: Math.max(0, rows(r.entries).length - 24),
    301. 

  301.       scope: r.scope,
    302. 

  302.     };
    303. 

  303.   else if (command === 'field run')
    304. 

  304.     summary = {
    305. 

  305.       identity: r.identity,
    306. 

  306.       tokens: r.tokens,
    307. 

  307.       rendererVerification: r.rendererVerification,
    308. 

  308.       scope: r.scope,
    309. 

  309.       measurement: {
    310. 

  310.         identity: record(r.measurements).identity,
    311. 

  311.         provider: record(r.measurements).provider,
    312. 

  312.         cells: rows(record(r.measurements).rows).length,
    313. 

  313.       },
    314. 

  314.       workflow: {
    315. 

  315.         status: record(r.workflow).status,
    316. 

  316.         statistics: record(r.workflow).statistics,
    317. 

  317.         stages: Object.fromEntries(
    318. 

  318.           Object.entries(record(record(r.workflow).stages)).map(([id, v]) => {
    319. 

  319.             const s = record(v);
    320. 

  320.             return [
    321. 

  321.               id,
    322. 

  322.               {
    323. 

  323.                 status: s.status,
    324. 

  324.                 assignment: s.assignment,
    325. 

  325.                 alternatives: rows(s.alternatives).length,
    326. 

  326.                 selection: selectionSummary(s.selection),
    327. 

  327.                 evidence: stageEvidenceSummary(s.evidence),
    328. 

  328.               },
    329. 

  329.             ];
    330. 

  330.           }),
    331. 

  331.         ),
    332. 

  332.       },
    333. 

  333.       ledger: rows(r.ledger)
    334. 

  334.         .slice(0, 16)
    335. 

  335.         .map((l) => ({
    336. 

  336.           command: l.command,
    337. 

  337.           inputHash: l.inputHash,
    338. 

  338.           resultHash: l.resultHash,
    339. 

  339.           evidenceKind: l.evidenceKind,
    340. 

  340.           elapsedMs: l.elapsedMs,
    341. 

  341.         })),
    342. 

  342.       recipe: resultRef
    343. 

  343.         ? { $designRef: resultRef, pointer: '/workflow/recipe' }
    344. 

  344.         : null,
    345. 

  345.     };
    346. 

  346.   else if (command === 'field validate')
    347. 

  347.     summary = {
    348. 

  348.       identity: r.identity,
    349. 

  349.       runtime: r.runtime,
    350. 

  350.       status: r.status,
    351. 

  351.       scenarios: rows(r.scenarios).map((s) => ({
    352. 

  352.         scenario: s.scenario,
    353. 

  353.         checks: s.checks,
    354. 

  354.         passed: s.passed,
    355. 

  355.       })),
    356. 

  356.       scope: r.scope,
    357. 

  357.     };
    358. 

  358.   else if (command === 'validation plan')
    359. 

  359.     summary = {
    360. 

  360.       identity: r.identity,
    361. 

  361.       designIdentity: record(r.plan).designIdentity,
    362. 

  362.       scenarios: rows(record(r.plan).scenarios).map((s) => s.id),
    363. 

  363.       phases: rows(record(r.plan).phases).map((p) => ({
    364. 

  364.         id: p.id,
    365. 

  365.         requirements: rows(p.requirements).length,
    366. 

  366.       })),
    367. 

  367.       scope: r.scope,
    368. 

  368.     };
    369. 

  369.   else if (command === 'validation check')
    370. 

  370.     summary = {
    371. 

  371.       planIdentity: r.planIdentity,
    372. 

  372.       designIdentity: r.designIdentity,
    373. 

  373.       state: r.state,
    374. 

  374.       complete: r.complete,
    375. 

  375.       phases: rows(r.phases).map((p) => ({
    376. 

  376.         id: p.id,
    377. 

  377.         ready: p.ready,
    378. 

  378.         requirements: rows(p.requirements).length,
    379. 

  379.       })),
    380. 

  380.       outstandingCount: rows(r.next).length,
    381. 

  381.       next: rows(r.next)
    382. 

  382.         .slice(0, 12)
    383. 

  383.         .map((x) => ({
    384. 

  384.           id: x.id,
    385. 

  385.           scenario: x.scenario,
    386. 

  386.           tool: x.tool,
    387. 

  387.           status: x.status,
    388. 

  388.           resultRef: x.resultRef,
    389. 

  389.         })),
    390. 

  390.       recordedUnsupported: rows(r.recordedUnsupported).map((x) => x.id),
    391. 

  391.       neuralCalls: r.neuralCalls,
    392. 

  392.       scope: r.scope,
    393. 

  393.     };
    394. 

  394.   else if (command === 'material candidates')
    395. 

  395.     summary = {
    396. 

  396.       paretoIds: r.paretoIds,
    397. 

  397.       selectedIds: r.selectedIds,
    398. 

  398.       unresolvedIds: r.unresolvedIds,
    399. 

  399.       dominatedIds: r.dominatedIds,
    400. 

  400.       candidateCount: rows(r.candidates).length,
    401. 

  401.       candidates: rows(r.candidates)
    402. 

  402.         .slice(0, 8)
    403. 

  403.         .map((c) => ({
    404. 

  404.           id: c.id,
    405. 

  405.           residuals: rows(c.residuals).map((x) => ({
    406. 

  406.             metric: x.metric,
    407. 

  407.             value: x.value,
    408. 

  408.             residual: x.residual,
    409. 

  409.           })),
    410. 

  410.           comparisonStatus: c.comparisonStatus,
    411. 

  411.         })),
    412. 

  412.       selection: r.selection,
    413. 

  413.       empiricalPreference: r.empiricalPreference,
    414. 

  414.     };
    415. 

  415.   else if (command === 'material pair')
    416. 

  416.     summary = {
    417. 

  417.       pair: r.pair,
    418. 

  418.       descriptors: Object.fromEntries(
    419. 

  419.         Object.entries(record(r.descriptors)).map(([id, v]) => [
    420. 

  420.           id,
    421. 

  421.           { value: record(v).value, units: record(v).units },
    422. 

  422.         ]),
    423. 

  423.       ),
    424. 

  424.       physicalModelChecks: r.physicalModelChecks,
    425. 

  425.       brief: r.brief
    426. 

  426.         ? {
    427. 

  427.             id: record(r.brief).id,
    428. 

  428.             status: record(r.brief).status,
    429. 

  429.             bands: rows(record(r.brief).bands).map((b) => ({
    430. 

  430.               metric: b.metric,
    431. 

  431.               value: b.value,
    432. 

  432.               min: b.min,
    433. 

  433.               max: b.max,
    434. 

  434.               status: b.status,
    435. 

  435.             })),
    436. 

  436.           }
    437. 

  437.         : null,
    438. 

  438.       textOverlayLegibility: r.textOverlayLegibility,
    439. 

  439.       empiricalPreference: r.empiricalPreference,
    440. 

  440.       limits: r.limits,
    441. 

  441.     };
    442. 

  442.   else if (command === 'wireframe inspect')
    443. 

  443.     summary = {
    444. 

  444.       id: record(r.wireframe).id,
    445. 

  445.       screen: record(r.wireframe).screen,
    446. 

  446.       variables: record(r.wireframe).variables,
    447. 

  447.       nodes: rows(r.nodes).length,
    448. 

  448.       relations: rows(r.relations).length,
    449. 

  449.       issues: rows(r.relations)
    450. 

  450.         .filter(
    451. 

  451.           (x) => x.status === 'unknown' || x.status === 'outside-authored-band',
    452. 

  452.         )
    453. 

  453.         .slice(0, 12),
    454. 

  454.       scope: r.scope,
    455. 

  455.     };
    456. 

  456.   else if (command === 'decision run')
    457. 

  457.     summary = {
    458. 

  458.       state: r.state,
    459. 

  459.       status: r.status,
    460. 

  460.       nextStage: r.nextStage,
    461. 

  461.       order: r.order,
    462. 

  462.       statistics: r.statistics,
    463. 

  463.       stages: Object.fromEntries(
    464. 

  464.         Object.entries(record(r.stages)).map(([id, v]) => {
    465. 

  465.           const s = record(v);
    466. 

  466.           return [
    467. 

  467.             id,
    468. 

  468.             {
    469. 

  469.               status: s.status,
    470. 

  470.               assignment: s.assignment,
    471. 

  471.               reason: compactText(s.reason, 500),
    472. 

  472.               alternatives: rows(s.alternatives).length,
    473. 

  473.               rendererVerification: s.rendererVerification,
    474. 

  474.               selection: selectionSummary(s.selection),
    475. 

  475.               evidence: stageEvidenceSummary(s.evidence),
    476. 

  476.             },
    477. 

  477.           ];
    478. 

  478.         }),
    479. 

  479.       ),
    480. 

  480.       recipe: resultRef ? { $designRef: resultRef, pointer: '/recipe' } : null,
    481. 

  481.       scope: r.scope,
    482. 

  482.     };
    483. 

  483.   else if (command === 'motion dynamics')
    484. 

  484.     summary = {
    485. 

  485.       initial: r.initial,
    486. 

  486.       final: r.final,
    487. 

  487.       balanceResidual: r.balanceResidual,
    488. 

  488.       sampleCount: rows(r.samples).length,
    489. 

  489.       provenance: r.provenance,
    490. 

  490.     };
    491. 

  491.   else if (command === 'motion timeline')
    492. 

  492.     summary = {
    493. 

  493.       min: r.min,
    494. 

  494.       max: r.max,
    495. 

  495.       durationSeconds: r.durationSeconds,
    496. 

  496.       sampleCount: Array.isArray(r.values) ? r.values.length : 0,
    497. 

  497.       provenance: r.provenance,
    498. 

  498.     };
    499. 

  499.   else if (command === 'surface css')
    500. 

  500.     summary = {
    501. 

  501.       version: r.version,
    502. 

  502.       input: r.input,
    503. 

  503.       material: r.material,
    504. 

  504.       descriptors: r.descriptors,
    505. 

  505.       contrast: r.contrast,
    506. 

  506.       limits: r.limits,
    507. 

  507.       recipe: resultRef ? { $designRef: resultRef } : null,
    508. 

  508.       note: 'Full CSS and variables remain in the result reference or CLI output.',
    509. 

  509.     };
    510. 

  510.   else if (command === 'surface light')
    511. 

  511.     summary = {
    512. 

  512.       normal: r.normal,
    513. 

  513.       lightDirection: r.lightDirection,
    514. 

  514.       descriptors: r.descriptors,
    515. 

  515.       cssVariables: r.cssVariables,
    516. 

  516.       model: r.model,
    517. 

  517.     };
    518. 

  518.   else if (command === 'surface textures')
    519. 

  519.     summary = {
    520. 

  520.       width: r.width,
    521. 

  521.       height: r.height,
    522. 

  522.       parameters: r.parameters,
    523. 

  523.       encoding: r.encoding,
    524. 

  524.       algorithm: r.algorithm,
    525. 

  525.       roughnessMapSemantics: r.roughnessMapSemantics,
    526. 

  526.       orientation: r.orientation,
    527. 

  527.       note: 'Texture bytes remain in the exact result reference; request only the needed channel.',
    528. 

  528.     };
    529. 

  529.   else if (command === 'prose analyze')
    530. 

  530.     summary = {
    531. 

  531.       purpose: r.purpose,
    532. 

  532.       sections: rows(r.facts).length,
    533. 

  533.       warningCount: rows(r.warnings).length,
    534. 

  534.       warnings: rows(r.warnings).slice(0, 12),
    535. 

  535.       journeys: rows(r.journeys).map((j) => ({
    536. 

  536.         id: j.id,
    537. 

  537.         reachable: j.reachable,
    538. 

  538.         clicks: j.clicks,
    539. 

  539.         estimatedSeconds: j.estimatedSeconds,
    540. 

  540.       })),
    541. 

  541.       aggregate: r.aggregate,
    542. 

  542.       limits: r.limits,
    543. 

  543.     };
    544. 

  544.   else if (command === 'media analyze')
    545. 

  545.     summary = {
    546. 

  546.       file: r.file,
    547. 

  547.       sha256: r.sha256,
    548. 

  548.       width: r.width,
    549. 

  549.       height: r.height,
    550.

  141. const StateInput = z.object({ state: StateSchema });
    142. 

  142. const TypographyInput = StateInput.extend({
    143. 

  143.   blockId: z.string(),
    144. 

  144.   render: z.boolean().default(true),
    145. 

  145.   engine: z.enum(['chromium', 'webkit']).default('chromium'),
    146. 

  146.   lineLimit: z.number().int().min(1).max(256).default(64),
    147. 

  147. });
    148. 

  148. 

  149. /** Match the responsive renderer's effective font size and available text width. */
    150. 

  150. function effectiveText(state: z.infer<typeof StateSchema>, blockId: string) {
    151. 

  151.   const resolved = resolveLayout(state);
    152. 

  152.   const block = resolved.state.blocks.find((b) => b.id === blockId);
    153. 

  153.   if (!block) throw new Error(`unknown block ${blockId}`);
    154. 

  154.   return {
    155. 

  155.     block: { ...block, size: block.size * state.viewport.textScale },
    156. 

  156.     resolution: {
    157. 

  157.       pattern: resolved.pattern,
    158. 

  158.       textScale: state.viewport.textScale,
    159. 

  159.       authoredSize: state.blocks.find((b) => b.id === blockId)!.size,
    160. 

  160.       effectiveSize: block.size * state.viewport.textScale,
    161. 

  161.       effectiveMeasure: block.measure,
    162. 

  162.       notes: resolved.notes,
    163. 

  163.     },
    164. 

  164.   };
    165. 

  165. }
    166.
