  453. export async function probeTypographyAxes(
    454. 

  454.   block: TextBlock,
    455. 

  455.   options: TypographyAxisOptions &
    456. 

  456.     TypographyBrowserOptions & { render?: boolean } = {},
    457. 

  457. ) {
    458. 

  458.   const plan = typographyAxisCandidates(block, options);
    459. 

  459.   const launched = options.render === false ? null : await launch(options);
    460. 

  460.   try {
    461. 

  461.     const results = [];
    462. 

  462.     for (const candidate of plan.candidates) {
    463. 

  463.       const inspection = inspectTypography(candidate.block);
    464. 

  464.       const browser = launched
    465. 

  465.         ? await inspectWithBrowser(
    466. 

  466.             launched.browser,
    467. 

  467.             launched.engine,
    468. 

  468.             candidate.block,
    469. 

  469.             options,
    470. 

  470.           )
    471. 

  471.         : null;
    472. 

  472.       results.push({
    473. 

  473.         id: candidate.id,
    474. 

  474.         axis: candidate.axis,
    475. 

  475.         point: candidate.point,
    476. 

  476.         value: candidate.value,
    477. 

  477.         inputHash: inspection.inputHash,
    478. 

  478.         resolvedForFontkit: inspection.axes.resolvedForFontkit,
    479. 

  479.         advance: inspection.shaping.advance,
    480. 

  480.         outlineBounds: inspection.shaping.outlineBounds,
    481. 

  481.         referenceGlyphs: inspection.referenceGlyphs,
    482. 

  482.         renderer: browser
    483. 

  483.           ? {
    484. 

  484.               runtime: browser.runtime,
    485. 

  485.               fontLoading: browser.fontLoading,
    486. 

  486.               box: browser.box,
    487. 

  487.               lineBreakSignature: browser.lineBreakSignature,
    488. 

  488.               totalLines: browser.totalLines,
    489. 

  489.               lines: browser.lines.map((line) => ({
    490. 

  490.                 start: line.start,
    491. 

  491.                 end: line.end,
    492. 

  492.                 rangeBounds: line.rangeBounds,
    493. 

  493.                 baseline: line.baseline,
    494. 

  494.               })),
    495. 

  495.               baselineDeltas: browser.baselineDeltas,
    496. 

  496.               omittedLines: browser.omittedLines,
    497. 

  497.             }
    498. 

  498.           : null,
    499. 

  499.       });
    500. 

  500.     }
    501. 

  501.     const base = results[0]!;
    502. 

  502.     return {
    503. 

  503.       fontHash: plan.fontHash,
    504. 

  504.       axes: plan.axes,
    505. 

  505.       totalCandidates: plan.totalCandidates,
    506. 

  506.       omittedCandidates: plan.omittedCandidates,
    507. 

  507.       method: plan.method,
    508. 

  508.       results: results.map((result) => ({
    509. 

  509.         ...result,
    510. 

  510.         effect: {
    511. 

  511.           advanceDelta: result.advance - base.advance,
    512. 

  512.           outlineChanged:
    513. 

  513.             hash(result.outlineBounds) !== hash(base.outlineBounds),
    514. 

  514.           rendererLineBreaksChanged:
    515. 

  515.             result.renderer && base.renderer
    516. 

  516.               ? hash(result.renderer.lineBreakSignature) !==
    517. 

  517.                 hash(base.renderer.lineBreakSignature)
    518. 

  518.               : null,
    519. 

  519.           rendererHeightDelta:
    520. 

  520.             result.renderer && base.renderer
    521. 

  521.               ? result.renderer.box.height - base.renderer.box.height
    522. 

  522.               : null,
    523. 

  523.           rendererGeometryChanged:
    524. 

  524.             result.renderer && base.renderer
    525. 

  525.               ? hash(result.renderer.lines) !== hash(base.renderer.lines)
    526. 

  526.               : null,
    527. 

  527.         },
    528. 

  528.       })),
    529. 

  529.       limits: [
    530. 

  530.         'Sampled endpoints are not an interpolation model or a claim that every axis affects this text.',
    531. 

  531.         'DOM geometry may be unchanged while outlines/paint change; no raster paint bounds are extracted.',
    532. 

  532.         'Browser computed style does not reveal resolved variation coordinates; endpoint geometry supplies empirical evidence only.',
    533. 

  533.       ],
    534. 

  534.     };
    535. 

  535.   } finally {
    536. 

  536.     await launched?.browser.close();
    537. 

  537.   }
    538. 

  538. }

  1. /** Browser-only, horizontal, uniform-style text inspection. No font-table baseline inference. */
    2. 

  2. export function collectTypographyElement(el: HTMLElement, lineLimit = 64) {
    3. 

  3.   const text = el.textContent ?? '';
    4. 

  4.   const style = getComputedStyle(el);
    5. 

  5.   const origin = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    6. 

  6.   const segments = [
    7. 

  7.     ...new Intl.Segmenter(el.lang || 'en', { granularity: 'grapheme' }).segment(
    8. 

  8.       text,
    9. 

  9.     ),
    10. 

  10.   ];
    11. 

  11.   const rect = (r: DOMRect) => ({
    12. 

  12.     x: r.x - origin.x,
    13. 

  13.     y: r.y - origin.y,
    14. 

  14.     width: r.width,
    15. 

  15.     height: r.height,
    16. 

  16.   });
    17. 

  17.   // Re-walk after a probe splits a text node; offsets remain UTF-16 text offsets.
    18. 

  18.   function point(offset: number) {
    19. 

  19.     const walker = document.createTreeWalker(el, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
    20. 

  20.     let node: Node | null,
    21. 

  21.       remaining = offset;
    22. 

  22.     while ((node = walker.nextNode())) {
    23. 

  23.       const length = node.textContent!.length;
    24. 

  24.       if (remaining <= length) return { node, offset: remaining };
    25. 

  25.       remaining -= length;
    26. 

  26.     }
    27. 

  27.     throw new Error('Text offset outside inspected element');
    28. 

  28.   }
    29. 

  29.   function samples() {
    30. 

  30.     return segments.map((segment) => {
    31. 

  31.       const range = document.createRange();
    32. 

  32.       const a = point(segment.index),
    33. 

  33.         b = point(segment.index + segment.segment.length);
    34. 

  34.       range.setStart(a.node, a.offset);
    35. 

  35.       range.setEnd(b.node, b.offset);
    36. 

  36.       const fragments = [...range.getClientRects()].filter((r) => r.height > 0);
    37. 

  37.       const nonzero = fragments.filter((r) => r.width > 0);
    38. 

  38.       return {
    39. 

  39.         start: segment.index,
    40. 

  40.         end: segment.index + segment.segment.length,
    41. 

  41.         text: segment.segment,
    42. 

  42.         rects: (nonzero.length ? nonzero : fragments).map(rect),
    43. 

  43.       };
    44. 

  44.     });
    45. 

  45.   }
    46. 

  46.   const original = samples();
    47. 

  47.   const groups: {
    48. 

  48.     y: number;
    49. 

  49.     height: number;
    50. 

  50.     start: number;
    51. 

  51.     end: number;
    52. 

  52.     fragments: { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }[];
    53. 

  53.   }[] = [];
    54. 

  54.   for (const sample of original)
    55. 

  55.     for (const r of sample.rects) {
    56. 

  56.       if (!r.height) continue;
    57. 

  57.       // Range fragments describe font/selection boxes, not CSS line boxes or raster ink.
    58. 

  58.       let group = groups.find(
    59. 

  59.         (g) =>
    60. 

  60.           Math.abs(g.y - r.y) < 0.25 && Math.abs(g.height - r.height) < 0.25,
    61. 

  61.       );
    62. 

  62.       if (!group) {
    63. 

  63.         group = {
    64. 

  64.           y: r.y,
    65. 

  65.           height: r.height,
    66. 

  66.           start: sample.start,
    67. 

  67.           end: sample.end,
    68. 

  68.           fragments: [],
    69. 

  69.         };
    70. 

  70.         groups.push(group);
    71. 

  71.       }
    72. 

  72.       group.start = Math.min(group.start, sample.start);
    73. 

  73.       group.end = Math.max(group.end, sample.end);
    74. 

  74.       group.fragments.push(r);
    75. 

  75.     }
    76. 

  76.   groups.sort((a, b) => a.y - b.y);
    77. 

  77.   const tolerance = 1 / 64; // CSS layout quantization tolerance, not a paint tolerance.
    78. 

  78.   const maxProbeGraphemeMeasurements = 131072;
    79. 

  79.   let probeGraphemeMeasurements = 0,
    80. 

  80.     probeBudgetExhausted = false;
    81. 

  81.   function unchanged() {
    82. 

  82.     const current = samples(),
    83. 

  83.       box = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    84. 

  84.     return (
    85. 

  85.       Math.abs(box.height - origin.height) <= tolerance &&
    86. 

  86.       Math.abs(box.width - origin.width) <= tolerance &&
    87. 

  87.       current.every((sample, i) => {
    88. 

  88.         const before = original[i]!;
    89. 

  89.         return (
    90. 

  90.           sample.rects.length === before.rects.length &&
    91. 

  91.           sample.rects.every((r, j) => {
    92. 

  92.             const p = before.rects[j]!;
    93. 

  93.             return ['x', 'y', 'width', 'height'].every(
    94. 

  94.               (k) =>
    95. 

  95.                 Math.abs(r[k as keyof typeof r] - p[k as keyof typeof p]) <=
    96. 

  96.                 tolerance,
    97. 

  97.             );
    98. 

  98.           })
    99. 

  99.         );
    100. 

  100.       })
    101. 

  101.     );
    102. 

  102.   }
    103. 

  103.   const lines = groups.slice(0, lineLimit).map((g, index) => {
    104. 

  104.     const members = original.filter(
    105. 

  105.       (s) => s.start >= g.start && s.end <= g.end && /\S/u.test(s.text),
    106. 

  106.     );
    107. 

  107.     const first = members[0],
    108. 

  108.       last = members.at(-1);
    109. 

  109.     // Try boundaries first; an inline-block inside a joined word can alter shaping.
    110. 

  110.     const offsets = [
    111. 

  111.       ...new Set(
    112. 

  112.         [
    113. 

  113.           last?.end,
    114. 

  114.           first?.start,
    115. 

  115.           first?.end,
    116. 

  116.           g.end,
    117. 

  117.           ...members
    118. 

  118.             .filter((s) => /\s/u.test(text[s.end] ?? ''))
    119. 

  119.             .map((s) => s.end),
    120. 

  120.         ].filter((n): n is number => n !== undefined),
    121. 

  121.       ),
    122. 

  122.     ].slice(0, 8);
    123. 

  123.     let baseline: number | null = null,
    124. 

  124.       probeOffset: number | null = null;
    125. 

  125.     const attempts: {
    126. 

  126.       offset: number;
    127. 

  127.       geometryUnchanged: boolean;
    128. 

  128.       onThisLine: boolean;
    129. 

  129.       ownerFragments: { y: number; height: number }[];
    130. 

  130.     }[] = [];
    131. 

  131.     for (const offset of offsets) {
    132. 

  132.       if (
    133. 

  133.         probeGraphemeMeasurements + segments.length >
    134. 

  134.         maxProbeGraphemeMeasurements
    135. 

  135.       ) {
    136. 

  136.         probeBudgetExhausted = true;
    137. 

  137.         break;
    138. 

  138.       }
    139. 

  139.       probeGraphemeMeasurements += segments.length;
    140. 

  140.       const p = point(offset),
    141. 

  141.         range = document.createRange();
    142. 

  142.       range.setStart(p.node, p.offset);
    143. 

  143.       range.collapse(true);
    144. 

  144.       const marker = document.createElement('span');
    145. 

  145.       // An empty inline-block with hidden overflow has its baseline at its bottom margin edge.
    146. 

  146.       marker.style.cssText =
    147. 

  147.         'display:inline-block;width:0;height:0;padding:0;margin:0;border:0;overflow:hidden;line-height:0;vertical-align:baseline';
    148. 

  148.       const owner = document.createElement('span');
    149. 

  149.       owner.style.cssText =
    150. 

  150.         'display:inline;padding:0;margin:0;border:0;vertical-align:baseline';
    151. 

  151.       owner.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    152. 

  152.       owner.append(marker);
    153. 

  153.       range.insertNode(owner);
    154. 

  154.       const y = marker.getBoundingClientRect().bottom - origin.y;
    155. 

  155.       const same = unchanged();
    156. 

  156.       // A baseline can fall inside several overlapping selection boxes. The
    157. 

  157.       // inline wrapper exposes which font fragment actually owns this marker.
    158. 

  158.       const ownerFragments = [...owner.getClientRects()].map((r) => ({
    159. 

  159.         y: r.y - origin.y,
    160. 

  160.         height: r.height,
    161. 

  161.       }));
    162. 

  162.       const onThisLine =
    163. 

  163.         ownerFragments.length === 1 &&
    164. 

  164.         Math.abs(ownerFragments[0]!.y - g.y) <= tolerance &&
    165. 

  165.         Math.abs(ownerFragments[0]!.height - g.height) <= tolerance;
    166. 

  166.       attempts.push({
    167. 

  167.         offset,
    168. 

  168.         geometryUnchanged: same,
    169. 

  169.         onThisLine,
    170. 

  170.         ownerFragments,
    171. 

  171.       });
    172. 

  172.       owner.remove();
    173. 

  173.       el.normalize();
    174. 

  174.       if (same && onThisLine) {
    175. 

  175.         baseline = y;
    176. 

  176.         probeOffset = offset;
    177. 

  177.         break;
    178. 

  178.       }
    179. 

  179.     }
    180. 

  180.     const left = Math.min(...g.fragments.map((r) => r.x)),
    181. 

  181.       right = Math.max(...g.fragments.map((r) => r.x + r.width));
    182. 

  182.     return {
    183. 

  183.       index,
    184. 

  184.       start: g.start,
    185. 

  185.       end: g.end,
    186. 

  186.       text: text.slice(g.start, g.end),
    187. 

  187.       rangeBounds: { x: left, y: g.y, width: right - left, height: g.height },
    188. 

  188.       baseline,
    189. 

  189.       baselineSource:
    190. 

  190.         baseline === null
    191. 

  191.           ? probeBudgetExhausted
    192. 

  192.             ? 'unavailable: probe budget exhausted'
    193. 

  193.             : 'unavailable: no geometry-preserving probe'
    194. 

  194.           : 'DOM zero-size baseline-aligned inline-block',
    195. 

  195.       probeOffset,
    196. 

  196.       attempts,
    197. 

  197.       lineBox: null as null,
    198. 

  198.     };
    199. 

  199.   });
    200. 

  200.   const canvas = document.createElement('canvas'),
    201. 

  201.     ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')!;
    202. 

  202.   ctx.font = `${style.fontStyle} ${style.fontWeight} ${style.fontSize} ${style.fontFamily}`;
    203. 

  203.   ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
    204. 

  204.   ctx.textAlign = 'left';
    205. 

  205.   ctx.direction = style.direction as CanvasDirection;
    206. 

  206.   ctx.fontKerning = style.fontKerning as CanvasFontKerning;
    207. 

  207.   if ('letterSpacing' in ctx)
    208. 

  208.     ctx.letterSpacing =
    209. 

  209.       style.letterSpacing === 'normal' ? '0px' : style.letterSpacing;
    210. 

  210.   const canvasLines = lines.map((line) => {
    211. 

  211.     const m = ctx.measureText(line.text);
    212. 

  212.     return {
    213. 

  213.       index: line.index,
    214. 

  214.       width: m.width,
    215. 

  215.       actualBoundingBoxLeft: m.actualBoundingBoxLeft,
    216. 

  216.       actualBoundingBoxRight: m.actualBoundingBoxRight,
    217. 

  217.       actualBoundingBoxAscent: m.actualBoundingBoxAscent,
    218. 

  218.       actualBoundingBoxDescent: m.actualBoundingBoxDescent,
    219. 

  219.       fontBoundingBoxAscent: Number.isFinite(m.fontBoundingBoxAscent)
    220. 

  220.         ? m.fontBoundingBoxAscent
    221. 

  221.         : null,
    222. 

  222.       fontBoundingBoxDescent: Number.isFinite(m.fontBoundingBoxDescent)
    223. 

  223.         ? m.fontBoundingBoxDescent
    224. 

  224.         : null,
    225. 

  225.       alphabeticBaseline: Number.isFinite(m.alphabeticBaseline)
    226. 

  226.         ? m.alphabeticBaseline
    227. 

  227.         : null,
    228. 

  228.       hangingBaseline: Number.isFinite(m.hangingBaseline)
    229. 

  229.         ? m.hangingBaseline
    230. 

  230.         : null,
    231. 

  231.       ideographicBaseline: Number.isFinite(m.ideographicBaseline)
    232. 

  232.         ? m.ideographicBaseline
    233. 

  233.         : null,
    234. 

  234.     };
    235. 

  235.   });
    236. 

  236.   const allRects = original.flatMap((sample) => sample.rects);
    237. 

  237.   const rangeUnion = allRects.length
    238. 

  238.     ? {
    239. 

  239.         x: Math.min(...allRects.map((r) => r.x)),
    240. 

  240.         y: Math.min(...allRects.map((r) => r.y)),
    241. 

  241.         right: Math.max(...allRects.map((r) => r.x + r.width)),
    242. 

  242.         bottom: Math.max(...allRects.map((r) => r.y + r.height)),
    243. 

  243.       }
    244. 

  244.     : null;
    245. 

  245.   return {
    246. 

  246.     unit: 'css-px',
    247. 

  247.     coordinateSpace: 'local to text element border-box; x right, y down',
    248. 

  248.     box: { x: 0, y: 0, width: origin.width, height: origin.height },
    249. 

  249.     rangeUnion,
    250. 

  250.     layoutOverflow: {
    251. 

  251.       scrollWidth: el.scrollWidth,
    252. 

  252.       scrollHeight: el.scrollHeight,
    253. 

  253.       clientWidth: el.clientWidth,
    254. 

  254.       clientHeight: el.clientHeight,
    255. 

  255.       scrollOverflowX: Math.max(0, el.scrollWidth - el.clientWidth),
    256. 

  256.       scrollOverflowY: Math.max(0, el.scrollHeight - el.clientHeight),
    257. 

  257.       rangeBeyondRight: rangeUnion
    258. 

  258.         ? Math.max(0, rangeUnion.right - origin.width)
    259. 

  259.         : 0,
    260. 

  260.       rangeBeyondBottom: rangeUnion
    261. 

  261.         ? Math.max(0, rangeUnion.bottom - origin.height)
    262. 

  262.         : 0,
    263. 

  263.       overflowX: style.overflowX,
    264. 

  264.       overflowY: style.overflowY,
    265. 

  265.       source:
    266. 

  266.         'integer CSSOM scroll/client dimensions plus subpixel Range union; not raster paint clipping',
    267. 

  267.     },
    268. 

  268.     computed: {
    269. 

  269.       fontFamily: style.fontFamily,
    270. 

  270.       fontSize: style.fontSize,
    271. 

  271.       fontWeight: style.fontWeight,
    272. 

  272.       fontVariationSettings: style.fontVariationSettings,
    273. 

  273.       fontOpticalSizing: style.fontOpticalSizing,
    274. 

  274.       fontFeatureSettings: style.fontFeatureSettings,
    275. 

  275.       fontKerning: style.fontKerning,
    276. 

  276.       letterSpacing: style.letterSpacing,
    277. 

  277.       lineHeight: style.lineHeight,
    278. 

  278.       direction: style.direction,
    279.

    280. 

  280.     lines,
    281.

    282. 

  282.     omittedLines: Math.max(0, groups.length - lines.length),
    283. 

  283.     lineGrouping:
    284. 

  284.       'Range fragments grouped by top and height. Fallback with different metrics can split one physical line into groups; this count alone cannot prove physical line count.',
    285.

    297. 

  297.       exhausted: probeBudgetExhausted,
    298.

    299. 

  299.     },

    304. 

  304.       variationAndFeatureParityWithDOM:
    305.

  1. import {
    2. 

  2.   MotionAnalyzeSchema,
    3. 

  3.   sampleMotion,
    4. 

  4.   type MotionInput,
    5. 

  5. } from './motion.js';
    6. 

  6. import { z } from 'zod';
    7. 

  7. 

  8. export type DrivenMotionOptions = {
    9. 

  9.   spring?: MotionInput['spring'];
    10. 

  10.   position: number;
    11. 

  11.   target?: number;
    12. 

  12.   velocity?: number;
    13. 

  13. };
    14. 

  14. export type DrivenMotionState = {
    15. 

  15.   position: number;
    16. 

  16.   velocity: number;
    17. 

  17.   target: number;
    18. 

  18.   energy: number;
    19. 

  19.   initialEnergy: number;
    20. 

  20.   targetWork: number;
    21. 

  21.   dissipated: number;
    22. 

  22.   elapsedSeconds: number;
    23. 

  23. };
    24. 

  24. 

  25. function finite(value: number, name: string) {
    26. 

  26.   if (!Number.isFinite(value)) throw new RangeError(`${name} must be finite`);
    27. 

  27.   return value;
    28. 

  28. }
    29. 

  29. function stepTime(value: number) {
    30. 

  30.   finite(value, 'dtSeconds');
    31. 

  31.   if (value < 0 || value > 30)
    32. 

  32.     throw new RangeError('dtSeconds must be in [0,30]');
    33. 

  33. }
    34. 

  34. 

  35. /** Exact held-target propagation. Retarget between steps; targets may do signed work.
    36. 

  36.  * No clock, DOM, layout reads, React updates or renderer dependency. */
    37. 

  37. export function createDrivenMotion(options: DrivenMotionOptions) {
    38. 

  38.   const parsed = MotionAnalyzeSchema.parse({
    39. 

  39.     ...(options.spring === undefined ? {} : { spring: options.spring }),
    40. 

  40.     coordinate: {
    41. 

  41.       unit: 'normalized',
    42. 

  42.       from: options.position,
    43. 

  43.       to: options.target ?? options.position,
    44. 

  44.       initialVelocity: options.velocity ?? 0,
    45. 

  45.     },
    46. 

  46.   });
    47. 

  47.   const { mass: m, stiffness: k, damping: c } = parsed.spring;
    48. 

  48.   const alpha = c / (2 * m),
    49. 

  49.     omega2 = k / m,
    50. 

  50.     delta = alpha * alpha - omega2;
    51. 

  51.   let x = parsed.coordinate.from,
    52. 

  52.     v = parsed.coordinate.initialVelocity;
    53. 

  53.   let target = parsed.coordinate.to,
    54. 

  54.     targetWork = 0,
    55. 

  55.     dissipated = 0,
    56. 

  56.     elapsedSeconds = 0;
    57. 

  57.   const energy = () => (m * v * v + k * (x - target) ** 2) / 2;
    58. 

  58.   const initialEnergy = energy();
    59. 

  59.   const snapshot = (): DrivenMotionState => ({
    60. 

  60.     position: x,
    61. 

  61.     velocity: v,
    62. 

  62.     target,
    63. 

  63.     energy: energy(),
    64. 

  64.     initialEnergy,
    65. 

  65.     targetWork,
    66. 

  66.     dissipated,
    67. 

  67.     elapsedSeconds,
    68. 

  68.   });
    69. 

  69.   return {
    70. 

  70.     snapshot,
    71. 

  71.     retarget(nextTarget: number) {
    72. 

  72.       finite(nextTarget, 'target');
    73. 

  73.       if (Math.abs(nextTarget) > 1e6)
    74. 

  74.         throw new RangeError('target must be in [-1e6,1e6]');
    75. 

  75.       // An instantaneous anchor displacement changes potential, never x or v.
    76. 

  76.       targetWork += (k * ((x - nextTarget) ** 2 - (x - target) ** 2)) / 2;
    77. 

  77.       target = nextTarget;
    78. 

  78.       return snapshot();
    79. 

  79.     },
    80. 

  80.     advance(dtSeconds: number) {
    81. 

  81.       stepTime(dtSeconds);
    82. 

  82.       if (dtSeconds === 0) return snapshot();
    83. 

  83.       const before = energy(),
    84. 

  84.         y = x - target;
    85. 

  85.       // Same stable closed form as sampleMotion, compiled once rather than parsing per tick.
    86. 

  86.       let C: number, S: number;
    87. 

  87.       if (delta < 0) {
    88. 

  88.         const beta = Math.sqrt(-delta),
    89. 

  89.           angle = beta * dtSeconds;
    90. 

  90.         const envelope = Math.exp(-alpha * dtSeconds);
    91. 

  91.         C = envelope * Math.cos(angle);
    92. 

  92.         S =
    93. 

  93.           envelope *
    94. 

  94.           (Math.abs(angle) < 1e-4
    95. 

  95.             ? dtSeconds * (1 - (angle * angle) / 6 + angle ** 4 / 120)
    96. 

  96.             : Math.sin(angle) / beta);
    97. 

  97.       } else if (delta === 0) {
    98. 

  98.         C = Math.exp(-alpha * dtSeconds);
    99. 

  99.         S = dtSeconds * C;
    100. 

  100.       } else {
    101. 

  101.         const d = Math.sqrt(delta),
    102. 

  102.           slow = -omega2 / (alpha + d);
    103. 

  103.         const eSlow = Math.exp(slow * dtSeconds);
    104. 

  104.         C = (eSlow + Math.exp(-(alpha + d) * dtSeconds)) / 2;
    105. 

  105.         S = (eSlow * -Math.expm1(-2 * d * dtSeconds)) / (2 * d);
    106. 

  106.       }
    107. 

  107.       const nextX = target + y * C + (v + alpha * y) * S;
    108. 

  108.       const nextV = v * C - (alpha * v + omega2 * y) * S;
    109. 

  109.       finite(nextX, 'propagated position');
    110. 

  110.       finite(nextV, 'propagated velocity');
    111. 

  111.       x = nextX;
    112. 

  112.       v = nextV;
    113. 

  113.       // Small signed roundoff remains visible in this analytic energy balance.
    114. 

  114.       dissipated += before - energy();
    115. 

  115.       elapsedSeconds += dtSeconds;
    116. 

  116.       return snapshot();
    117. 

  117.     },
    118. 

  118.   };
    119. 

  119. }
    120.

  121. export const MotionTimelineSchema = z
    122. 

  122.   .object({
    123. 

  123.     motion: MotionAnalyzeSchema,
    124. 

  124.     min: z.number().finite().min(-1e6).max(1e6),
    125. 

  125.     max: z.number().finite().min(-1e6).max(1e6),
    126. 

  126.     sampleCount: z.number().int().min(2).max(2048).optional(),
    127. 

  127.   })
    128. 

  128.   .strict()
    129. 

  129.   .refine((v) => v.min <= v.max, 'min must not exceed max');
    130. 

  130. export type MotionTimelineOptions = z.input<typeof MotionTimelineSchema>;

  132. /** Offline table for bounded property/timeline channels. Projection is an explicit
    133. 

  133.  * presentation policy, not a claim that the projected samples obey spring energy. */
    134. 

  134. export function sampleMotionTimeline(options: MotionTimelineOptions) {
    135. 

  135.   options = MotionTimelineSchema.parse(options);
    136. 

  136.   const motion = MotionAnalyzeSchema.parse({
    137. 

  137.     ...options.motion,
    138. 

  138.     ...(options.sampleCount === undefined
    139. 

  139.       ? {}
    140. 

  140.       : { sampleCount: options.sampleCount }),
    141. 

  141.   });
    142. 

  142.   let projectedSamples = 0;
    143. 

  143.   const values = Array.from({ length: motion.sampleCount }, (_, i) => {
    144. 

  144.     const raw = sampleMotion(
    145. 

  145.       motion,
    146. 

  146.       (motion.durationSeconds * i) / (motion.sampleCount - 1),
    147. 

  147.     ).position;
    148. 

  148.     const value = Math.min(options.max, Math.max(options.min, raw));
    149. 

  149.     if (value !== raw) projectedSamples++;
    150. 

  150.     return value;
    151. 

  151.   });
    152. 

  152.   return {
    153. 

  153.     values,
    154. 

  154.     min: options.min,
    155. 

  155.     max: options.max,
    156. 

  156.     durationSeconds: motion.durationSeconds,
    157. 

  157.     provenance: {
    158. 

  158.       kind: 'sampled-bounded-motion' as const,
    159. 

  159.       model: 'sampleMotion analytic stationary spring',
    160. 

  160.       interpolation: 'piecewise-linear' as const,
    161. 

  161.       projection: 'clamp sampled positions to caller bounds' as const,
    162. 

  162.       projectedSamples,
    163. 

  163.       sampleCount: values.length,
    164. 

  164.       endpointPolicy:
    165. 

  165.         'retain sampled endpoint; no forced snap to target' as const,
    166. 

  166.       limitation:
    167. 

  167.         'Presentation samples only: projection/interpolation do not preserve spring energy or certify continuous source extrema.',
    168. 

  168.     },
    169. 

  169.   };
    170. 

  170. }
    171. 

  171. export type MotionTimeline = ReturnType<typeof sampleMotionTimeline>;
    172. 

  172. 

  173. /** O(1) lookup; use a table produced by sampleMotionTimeline. Progress is clamped. */
    174. 

  174. export function sampleTimelineProgress(
    175. 

  175.   table: MotionTimeline,
    176. 

  176.   progress: number,
    177. 

  177. ) {
    178. 

  178.   finite(progress, 'progress');
    179. 

  179.   const index = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, progress)) * (table.values.length - 1);
    180. 

  180.   const lo = Math.floor(index),
    181. 

  181.     hi = Math.min(lo + 1, table.values.length - 1);
    182. 

  182.   return (
    183. 

  183.     table.values[lo]! * (1 - (index - lo)) + table.values[hi]! * (index - lo)
    184. 

  184.   );
    185. 

  185. }
    186. 

  186. 

  187. /** Increasing or decreasing authored scroll ranges; geometry is supplied by the host. */
    188. 

  188. export function scrollProgress(position: number, start: number, end: number) {
    189. 

  189.   finite(position, 'position');
    190. 

  190.   finite(start, 'start');
    191. 

  191.   finite(end, 'end');
    192. 

  192.   if (start === end)
    193. 

  193.     throw new RangeError('scroll range must have nonzero length');
    194. 

  194.   return Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (position - start) / (end - start)));
    195. 

  195. }
    196. 

  196. 

  197. /** Exact first-order hold response. Rate is per second, not a per-frame lerp. */
    198. 

  198. export function dampMotionValue(
    199. 

  199.   current: number,
    200. 

  200.   target: number,
    201. 

  201.   rate: number,
    202. 

  202.   dtSeconds: number,
    203. 

  203. ) {
    204. 

  204.   finite(current, 'current');
    205. 

  205.   finite(target, 'target');
    206. 

  206.   finite(rate, 'rate');
    207. 

  207.   stepTime(dtSeconds);
    208. 

  208.   if (rate < 0) throw new RangeError('rate must be nonnegative');
    209. 

  209.   const weight = -Math.expm1(-rate * dtSeconds);
    210. 

  210.   // Convex form also handles opposite, very large finite inputs without overflow.
    211. 

  211.   return current * (1 - weight) + target * weight;
    212. 

  212. }
    213. 

  213. 

  214. const coordinate = z.number().finite().min(-1e6).max(1e6);
    215. 

  215. export const MotionDynamicsSchema = z
    216. 

  216.   .object({
    217. 

  217.     spring: MotionAnalyzeSchema.shape.spring,
    218. 

  218.     position: coordinate,
    219. 

  219.     target: coordinate.optional(),
    220. 

  220.     velocity: coordinate.optional(),
    221. 

  221.     steps: z
    222. 

  222.       .array(
    223. 

  223.         z
    224. 

  224.           .object({
    225. 

  225.             dtSeconds: z.number().finite().min(0).max(30),
    226. 

  226.             target: coordinate.optional(),
    227. 

  227.           })
    228. 

  228.           .strict(),
    229. 

  229.       )
    230. 

  230.       .min(1)
    231. 

  231.       .max(2048),
    232. 

  232.   })
    233. 

  233.   .strict();
    234. 

  234. export type MotionDynamicsInput = z.input<typeof MotionDynamicsSchema>;
    235. 

  235. 

  236. /** Bounded serializable trace for CLI/MCP inspection; retarget occurs before each step. */
    237. 

  237. export function analyzeMotionDynamics(input: MotionDynamicsInput) {
    238. 

  238.   const request = MotionDynamicsSchema.parse(input);
    239. 

  239.   const driver = createDrivenMotion({
    240. 

  240.     spring: request.spring,
    241. 

  241.     position: request.position,
    242. 

  242.     ...(request.target === undefined ? {} : { target: request.target }),
    243. 

  243.     ...(request.velocity === undefined ? {} : { velocity: request.velocity }),
    244. 

  244.   });
    245. 

  245.   const initial = driver.snapshot();
    246. 

  246.   const samples = request.steps.map((step) => {
    247. 

  247.     if (step.target !== undefined) driver.retarget(step.target);
    248. 

  248.     return driver.advance(step.dtSeconds);
    249. 

  249.   });
    250. 

  250.   const final = driver.snapshot();
    251. 

  251.   return {
    252. 

  252.     initial,
    253. 

  253.     final,
    254. 

  254.     samples,
    255. 

  255.     balanceResidual:
    256. 

  256.       final.energy - (initial.energy + final.targetWork - final.dissipated),
    257. 

  257.     provenance: {
    258. 

  258.       kind: 'analytic-held-target-segments' as const,
    259. 

  259.       model: 'm*x″+c*x′+k*(x-target)=0 between target changes',
    260. 

  260.       targetWork:
    261. 

  261.         'k/2*((x-newTarget)^2-(x-oldTarget)^2); x and velocity continuous',
    262. 

  262.       dissipation:
    263. 

  263.         'sum of before-minus-after segment energy; signed floating-point roundoff retained',
    264. 

  264.       assumptions:
    265. 

  265.         'No collision, clamp or external force; target is held within each step. UI energy units are abstract.',
    266. 

  266.       limitation:
    267. 

  267.         'Partition invariance holds only for the same timed target schedule; sampled scroll retarget schedules can differ with frame cadence.',
    268. 

  268.     },
    269. 

  269.   };
    270. 

  270. }

  55. const dot = (a: SurfaceRGB, b: SurfaceRGB) =>
    56. 

  56.   a[0] * b[0] + a[1] * b[1] + a[2] * b[2];

  57. const normalized = (v: SurfaceRGB): SurfaceRGB => {
    58. 

  58.   const length = Math.hypot(...v);
    59. 

  59.   return v.map((n) => n / length) as SurfaceRGB;
    60. 

  60. };

  62. /** Single directional-light response in linear RGB, before exposure or display encoding. */
    63. 

  63. export function probeSurface(input: unknown) {
    64. 

  64.   const r = SurfaceProbeSchema.parse(input);
    65. 

  65.   const n = normalized(r.normal),
    66. 

  66.     v = normalized(r.view),
    67. 

  67.     l = normalized(r.light);
    68. 

  68.   const nl = clamp(dot(n, l)),
    69. 

  69.     nv = clamp(dot(n, v));
    70. 

  70.   const sum = l.map((c, i) => c + v[i]!) as SurfaceRGB;
    71. 

  71.   const h = Math.hypot(...sum) > 1e-8 ? normalized(sum) : n;
    72. 

  72.   const nh = clamp(dot(n, h)),
    73. 

  73.     vh = clamp(dot(v, h));
    74. 

  74.   // Exact zero roughness is a delta distribution, which cannot be sampled as a finite BRDF.
    75. 

  75.   const alpha = Math.max(0.0004, r.surface.roughness ** 2),
    76. 

  76.     a2 = alpha ** 2;
    77. 

  77.   const denominator = nh * nh * (a2 - 1) + 1;
    78. 

  78.   const distribution = a2 / (Math.PI * denominator * denominator);
    79. 

  79.   const g1 = (cosine: number) =>
    80. 

  80.     cosine <= 0
    81. 

  81.       ? 0
    82. 

  82.       : (2 * cosine) / (cosine + Math.sqrt(a2 + (1 - a2) * cosine * cosine));
    83. 

  83.   const geometry = g1(nl) * g1(nv);
    84. 

  84.   const f0 = r.surface.baseColor.map(
    85. 

  85.     (c) => 0.04 * (1 - r.surface.metalness) + c * r.surface.metalness,
    86. 

  86.   ) as SurfaceRGB;
    87. 

  87.   const fresnel = f0.map((f) => f + (1 - f) * (1 - vh) ** 5) as SurfaceRGB;
    88. 

  88.   const lit = nl > 0 && nv > 0;
    89. 

  89.   const diffuse = r.surface.baseColor.map((c, i) =>
    90. 

  90.     lit ? ((1 - fresnel[i]!) * (1 - r.surface.metalness) * c) / Math.PI : 0,
    91. 

  91.   ) as SurfaceRGB;
    92. 

  92.   // Cancel NdotL/NdotV algebraically before evaluation: grazing products can underflow.
    93. 

  93.   const visibilityDenominator =
    94. 

  94.     (nl + Math.sqrt(a2 + (1 - a2) * nl * nl)) *
    95. 

  95.     (nv + Math.sqrt(a2 + (1 - a2) * nv * nv));
    96. 

  96.   const specular = fresnel.map((f) =>
    97. 

  97.     lit ? (distribution * f) / visibilityDenominator : 0,
    98. 

  98.   ) as SurfaceRGB;
    99. 

  99.   const radiance = diffuse.map(
    100. 

  100.     (d, i) => (d + specular[i]!) * nl * r.irradiance[i]!,
    101. 

  101.   ) as SurfaceRGB;
    102. 

  102.   return {
    103. 

  103.     radiance,
    104. 

  104.     diffuseBrdf: diffuse,
    105. 

  105.     specularBrdf: specular,
    106. 

  106.     terms: {
    107. 

  107.       nDotL: nl,
    108. 

  108.       nDotV: nv,
    109. 

  109.       nDotH: nh,
    110. 

  110.       vDotH: vh,
    111. 

  111.       alpha,
    112. 

  112.       distribution,
    113. 

  113.       geometry,
    114. 

  114.       fresnel,
    115. 

  115.       f0,
    116. 

  116.     },
    117. 

  117.     units: 'relative-linear-rgb' as const,
    118. 

  118.     algorithm: 'lambert-ggx-schlick-separable-smith-v1',
    119. 

  119.     assumptions: [
    120. 

  120.       'baseColor is linear sRGB reflectance; directions point away from the surface in one coordinate space.',
    121. 

  121.       'irradiance is incident directional-light RGB before multiplication by N dot L, in relative units.',
    122. 

  122.       'Isotropic GGX; alpha=max(roughness squared,0.0004); Schlick Fresnel; dielectric F0=0.04; separable exact Smith G1.',
    123. 

  123.       'Diffuse uses (1-Fresnel)*(1-metalness)*baseColor/pi; no multiple scattering, environment, shadows, occlusion, transmission or emission.',
    124. 

  124.       'The inexpensive diffuse/specular coupling is not an exact energy-conservation model (Khronos B.3.6.3).',
    125. 

  125.       'No luminance calibration, perceptual quality score, display encoding or Three.js pixel-parity claim.',
    126. 

  126.     ],
    127. 

  127.     sources: surfaceSources,
    128. 

  128.   };

  186. /** Seeded seamless height-field normal/roughness textures; contains no implied material identity. */
    187. 

  187. export function generateSurfaceTextures(input: unknown = {}) {
    188. 

  188.   const r = SurfaceTextureSchema.parse(input),
    189. 

  189.     { size, frequency } = r;
    190. 

  190.   const lattice = (x: number, y: number) => {
    191. 

  191.     x = ((x % frequency) + frequency) % frequency;
    192. 

  192.     y = ((y % frequency) + frequency) % frequency;
    193. 

  193.     let h =
    194. 

  194.       (r.seed ^ Math.imul(x + 1, 374761393) ^ Math.imul(y + 1, 668265263)) >>>
    195. 

  195.       0;
    196. 

  196.     h = Math.imul(h ^ (h >>> 13), 1274126177) >>> 0;
    197. 

  197.     return ((h ^ (h >>> 16)) >>> 0) / 4294967295;
    198. 

  198.   };
    199. 

  199.   const smooth = (n: number) => n * n * (3 - 2 * n);
    200. 

  200.   const theta = (r.directionDegrees * Math.PI) / 180;
    201. 

  201.   // Integer winding counts keep the periodic ridges seamless at any authored direction.
    202. 

  202.   const wx = Math.round(Math.cos(theta) * frequency),
    203. 

  203.     wy = Math.round(Math.sin(theta) * frequency);
    204. 

  204.   const heights = new Float64Array(size * size);
    205. 

  205.   for (let y = 0; y < size; y++)
    206. 

  206.     for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) {
    207. 

  207.       const px = (x / size) * frequency,
    208. 

  208.         py = (y / size) * frequency;
    209. 

  209.       const ix = Math.floor(px),
    210. 

  210.         iy = Math.floor(py),
    211. 

  211.         tx = smooth(px - ix),
    212. 

  212.         ty = smooth(py - iy);
    213. 

  213.       const top = lattice(ix, iy) * (1 - tx) + lattice(ix + 1, iy) * tx;
    214. 

  214.       const bottom =
    215. 

  215.         lattice(ix, iy + 1) * (1 - tx) + lattice(ix + 1, iy + 1) * tx;
    216. 

  216.       const noise = top * (1 - ty) + bottom * ty;
    217. 

  217.       heights[y * size + x] =
    218. 

  218.         r.pattern === 'ridges'
    219. 

  219.           ? 0.5 +
    220. 

  220.             0.4 *
    221. 

  221.               Math.sin(
    222. 

  222.                 2 * Math.PI * ((wx * x) / size + (wy * y) / size) +
    223. 

  223.                   lattice(0, 0) * 2 * Math.PI,
    224. 

  224.               ) +
    225. 

  225.             0.1 * (noise - 0.5)
    226. 

  226.           : r.pattern === 'cells'
    227. 

  227.             ? 1 - Math.abs(2 * noise - 1)
    228. 

  228.             : noise;
    229. 

  229.     }
    230. 

  230.   const normal = new Uint8Array(size * size * 4),
    231. 

  231.     roughness = new Uint8Array(normal.length);
    232. 

  232.   const height = (x: number, y: number) =>
    233. 

  233.     heights[((y + size) % size) * size + ((x + size) % size)]!;
    234. 

  234.   for (let y = 0; y < size; y++)
    235. 

  235.     for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) {
    236. 

  236.       const index = (y * size + x) * 4;
    237. 

  237.       const dx = ((height(x + 1, y) - height(x - 1, y)) * size) / 2;
    238. 

  238.       const dy = ((height(x, y + 1) - height(x, y - 1)) * size) / 2;
    239. 

  239.       const n = normalized([-dx * r.normalStrength, -dy * r.normalStrength, 1]);
    240. 

  240.       normal.set(
    241. 

  241.         [...n.map((c) => Math.round((c * 0.5 + 0.5) * 255)), 255],
    242. 

  242.         index,
    243. 

  243.       );
    244. 

  244.       const value = Math.round(
    245. 

  245.         clamp(r.roughness + (height(x, y) - 0.5) * 2 * r.roughnessVariation) *
    246. 

  246.           255,
    247. 

  247.       );
    248. 

  248.       roughness.set([value, value, value, 255], index);
    249. 

  249.     }
    250. 

  250.   return {
    251. 

  251.     width: size,
    252. 

  252.     height: size,
    253. 

  253.     normal,
    254. 

  254.     roughness,
    255. 

  255.     parameters: r,
    256. 

  256.     encoding: 'rgba8-linear-data' as const,
    257. 

  257.     roughnessMapSemantics:
    258. 

  258.       'Absolute roughness in green; set material.roughness=1 when using this map.',
    259. 

  259.     orientation:
    260. 

  260.       'Rows advance +v; normal XY encode -dheight/du and -dheight/dv. Adapter must preserve this UV orientation.',
    261. 

  261.     algorithm: 'periodic-seeded-height-central-difference-v1',
    262. 

  262.   };
    263. 

  263. }

    78. 

  78.     lastKey = '';
    79. 

  79.   const win = element.ownerDocument.defaultView!;
    80. 

  80.   let pending = 0,
    81. 

  81.     pendingIdle = false,
    82. 

  82.     initializationStart = 0;
    83. 

  83.   let phase: 'context' | 'link' | 'texture' = 'context';
    84. 

  84.   let parallel: KHR_parallel_shader_compile | null = null;
    85. 

  85.   const uniforms = new Map<string, WebGLUniformLocation | null>();
    86. 

  86.   function fallback(reason: string) {
    87. 

  87.     stats.status = 'fallback';
    88. 

  88.     stats.reason = reason;
    89. 

  89.     canvas.hidden = true;
    90. 

  90.     element.setAttribute('data-dom-renderer', 'css');
    91. 

  91.   }
    92. 

  92.   function release() {
    93. 

  93.     if (gl && program) gl.deleteProgram(program);
    94. 

  94.     if (gl && texture) gl.deleteTexture(texture);
    95. 

  95.     program = texture = null;
    96. 

  96.     uniforms.clear();
    97. 

  97.   }
    98. 

  98.   function compile(type: number, source: string) {
    99. 

  99.     const shader = gl!.createShader(type)!;
    100. 

  100.     gl!.shaderSource(shader, source);
    101. 

  101.     gl!.compileShader(shader);
    102. 

  102.     return shader;
    103. 

  103.   }
    104. 

  104.   function upload() {
    105. 

  105.     if (!gl || !texture || suspended || gl.isContextLost()) return;
    106. 

  106.     if (!tile) {
    107. 

  107.       const start = performance.now();
    108. 

  108.       tile = createDomSurfaceTexture(
    109. 

  109.         recipe.input.material,
    110. 

  110.         recipe.input.textureSeed,
    111. 

  111.       );
    112. 

  112.       stats.textureBuilds++;
    113. 

  113.       stats.textureBuildMs += performance.now() - start;
    114. 

  114.     }
    115. 

  115.     gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, texture);
    116. 

  116.     gl.texImage2D(
    117. 

  117.       gl.TEXTURE_2D,
    118. 

  118.       0,
    119. 

  119.       gl.RGBA,
    120. 

  120.       tile.width,
    121. 

  121.       tile.height,
    122. 

  122.       0,
    123. 

  123.       gl.RGBA,
    124. 

  124.       gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE,
    125. 

  125.       tile.pixels,
    126. 

  126.     );
    127. 

  127.     gl.texParameteri(
    128. 

  128.       gl.TEXTURE_2D,
    129. 

  129.       gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER,
    130. 

  130.       gl.LINEAR_MIPMAP_LINEAR,
    131. 

  131.     );
    132. 

  132.     gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, gl.LINEAR);
    133. 

  133.     gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_S, gl.REPEAT);
    134. 

  134.     gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_T, gl.REPEAT);
    135. 

  135.     gl.generateMipmap(gl.TEXTURE_2D);
    136. 

  136.     stats.textureUploads++;
    137. 

  137.   }
    138. 

  138.   function initialize() {
    139. 

  139.     if (disposed || suspended || stats.status !== 'initializing') return;
    140. 

  140.     const start = performance.now();
    141. 

  141.     if (!initializationStart) initializationStart = start;
    142. 

  142.     try {
    143. 

  143.       if (phase === 'context') {
    144. 

  144.         gl = canvas.getContext('webgl2', {
    145. 

  145.           alpha: true,
    146. 

  146.           premultipliedAlpha: false,
    147. 

  147.           antialias: false,
    148. 

  148.           depth: false,
    149. 

  149.           stencil: false,
    150. 

  150.           powerPreference: 'low-power',
    151. 

  151.           preserveDrawingBuffer: false,
    152. 

  152.         });
    153. 

  153.         if (!gl) {
    154. 

  154.           fallback('WebGL2 unavailable');
    155. 

  155.           return;
    156. 

  156.         }
    157. 

  157.         parallel = gl.getExtension('KHR_parallel_shader_compile');
    158. 

  158.         stats.parallelCompile = parallel !== null;
    159. 

  159.         let vertex: WebGLShader | null = null,
    160. 

  160.           fragment: WebGLShader | null = null;
    161. 

  161.         try {
    162. 

  162.           vertex = compile(gl.VERTEX_SHADER, DOM_SURFACE_VERTEX);
    163. 

  163.           fragment = compile(gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER, DOM_SURFACE_FRAGMENT);
    164. 

  164.           program = gl.createProgram()!;
    165. 

  165.           gl.attachShader(program, vertex);
    166. 

  166.           gl.attachShader(program, fragment);
    167. 

  167.           gl.linkProgram(program);
    168. 

  168.         } finally {
    169. 

  169.           if (vertex) gl.deleteShader(vertex);
    170. 

  170.           if (fragment) gl.deleteShader(fragment);
    171. 

  171.         }
    172. 

  172.         phase = 'link';
    173. 

  173.         schedule(16);
    174. 

  174.         return;
    175. 

  175.       }
    176. 

  176.       if (phase === 'link') {
    177. 

  177.         if (
    178. 

  178.           parallel &&
    179. 

  179.           !gl!.getProgramParameter(program!, parallel.COMPLETION_STATUS_KHR)
    180. 

  180.         ) {
    181. 

  181.           schedule(16);
    182. 

  182.           return;
    183. 

  183.         }
    184. 

  184.         if (!gl!.getProgramParameter(program!, gl!.LINK_STATUS))
    185. 

  185.           throw new Error(
    186. 

  186.             gl!.getProgramInfoLog(program!) ?? 'Shader link failed',
    187. 

  187.           );
    188. 

  188.         stats.programBuilds++;
    189. 

  189.         for (const name of [
    190. 

  190.           'Texture',
    191. 

  191.           'Base',
    192. 

  192.           'LightColor',
    193. 

  193.           'Light',
    194. 

  194.           'PlaneNormal',
    195. 

  195.           'Aspect',
    196. 

  196.           'Roughness',
    197. 

  197.           'Metalness',
    198. 

  198.           'Variation',
    199. 

  199.           'TextureAmount',
    200. 

  200.           'Scale',
    201. 

  201.           'Intensity',
    202. 

  202.           'Ambient',
    203. 

  203.           'Exposure',
    204. 

  204.           'Point',
    205. 

  205.           'Alpha',
    206. 

  206.           'Output',
    207. 

  207.         ])
    208. 

  208.           uniforms.set(name, gl!.getUniformLocation(program!, `u${name}`));
    209. 

  209.         texture = gl!.createTexture();
    210. 

  210.         phase = 'texture';
    211. 

  211.         schedule();
    212. 

  212.         return;
    213. 

  213.       }
    214. 

  214.       upload();
    215. 

  215.       stats.status = 'ready';
    216. 

  216.       stats.reason = '';
    217. 

  217.       canvas.hidden = false;
    218. 

  218.       element.setAttribute('data-dom-renderer', 'webgl');
    219. 

  219.       if (latest) draw(latest);
    220. 

  220.     } catch (error) {
    221. 

  221.       release();
    222. 

  222.       fallback(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
    223. 

  223.     } finally {
    224. 

  224.       stats.initializationMs = performance.now() - initializationStart;
    225. 

  225.       stats.initializationTaskMaxMs = Math.max(
    226. 

  226.         stats.initializationTaskMaxMs,
    227. 

  227.         performance.now() - start,
    228. 

  228.       );
    229. 

  229.       stats.initializationTasks++;
    230. 

  230.     }
    231. 

  231.   }
    232. 

  232.   function update(next: DomSurfaceRecipe) {
    233. 

  233.     recipe = next;
    234. 

  234.     dirty = true;
    235. 

  235.     const nextKey = `${recipe.input.material}:${recipe.input.textureSeed}`;
    236. 

  236.     if (key !== nextKey) {
    237. 

  237.       key = nextKey;
    238. 

  238.       tile = null;
    239. 

  239.       upload();
    240. 

  240.     }
    241. 

  241.   }
    242. 

  242.   function resize(cssWidth: number, cssHeight: number) {
    243. 

  243.     width = Math.max(1, cssWidth);
    244. 

  244.     height = Math.max(1, cssHeight);
    245. 

  245.     const scale = Math.min(ratio, Math.sqrt(maxPixels / (width * height)));
    246. 

  246.     const w = Math.max(1, Math.floor(width * scale)),
    247. 

  247.       h = Math.max(1, Math.floor(height * scale));
    248. 

  248.     if (canvas.width !== w || canvas.height !== h) {
    249. 

  249.       canvas.width = w;
    250. 

  250.       canvas.height = h;
    251. 

  251.       dirty = true;
    252. 

  252.     }
    253. 

  253.     stats.pixels = w * h;
    254. 

  254.     if (latest) draw(latest);
    255. 

  255.   }
    256. 

  256.   function draw(sample: DomSurfaceLightSample) {
    257. 

  257.     latest = sample;
    258. 

  258.     if (disposed || suspended || !gl || !program || stats.status !== 'ready')
    259. 

  259.       return;
    260. 

  260.     const renderKey = [...sample.normal, ...sample.lightDirection]
    261. 

  261.       .map((v) => Math.round(v * 100000))
    262. 

  262.       .join(':');
    263. 

  263.     if (!dirty && renderKey === lastKey) return;
    264. 

  264.     dirty = false;
    265. 

  265.     lastKey = renderKey;
    266. 

  266.     const r = recipe.input;
    267. 

  267.     // CSS percentage coordinates use down-positive y; shader world uses up-positive y.
    268. 

  268.     // Light direction already contains the host's pointer/scroll/tilt target, including maxTilt=0.
    269. 

  269.     const light = sample.lightDirection.map(
    270. 

  270.       (v) => (v * r.light.z) / sample.lightDirection[2]!,
    271. 

  271.     ) as [number, number, number];
    272. 

  272.     gl.viewport(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    273. 

  273.     gl.useProgram(program);
    274. 

  274.     gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE0);
    275. 

  275.     gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, texture);
    276. 

  276.     gl.uniform1i(uniforms.get('Texture')!, 0);
    277. 

  277.     gl.uniform3fv(uniforms.get('Base')!, domSurfaceLinearColor(r.baseColor));
    278. 

  278.     gl.uniform3fv(
    279. 

  279.       uniforms.get('LightColor')!,
    280. 

  280.       domSurfaceLinearColor(r.light.color),
    281. 

  281.     );
    282. 

  282.     gl.uniform3f(uniforms.get('Light')!, light[0], -light[1], light[2]);
    283. 

  283.     gl.uniform3f(
    284. 

  284.       uniforms.get('PlaneNormal')!,
    285. 

  285.       sample.normal[0]!,
    286. 

  286.       -sample.normal[1]!,
    287. 

  287.       sample.normal[2]!,
    288. 

  288.     );
    289. 

  289.     const scalars = {
    290. 

  290.       Aspect: width / height,
    291. 

  291.       Roughness: r.roughness,
    292. 

  292.       Metalness: r.metalness,
    293. 

  293.       Variation: r.roughnessVariation,
    294. 

  294.       TextureAmount: r.textureAmount,
    295. 

  295.       Scale: r.textureScale,
    296. 

  296.       Intensity: r.light.intensity,
    297. 

  297.       Ambient: r.light.ambient,
    298. 

  298.       Exposure: r.exposure,
    299. 

  299.       Point: r.light.kind === 'point' ? 1 : 0,
    300. 

  300.       Alpha: 1 - r.translucency,
    301. 

  301.     };
    302. 

  302.     for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(scalars))
    303. 

  303.       gl.uniform1f(uniforms.get(name)!, value);
    304. 

  304.     gl.uniform1i(uniforms.get('Output')!, outputs.indexOf(output));
    305. 

  305.     gl.drawArrays(gl.TRIANGLES, 0, 3);
    306. 

  306.     stats.draws++;
    307. 

  307.   }
    308. 

  308.   function onLost(event: Event) {
    309. 

  309.     event.preventDefault();
    310. 

  310.     fallback('WebGL context lost');
    311. 

  311.   }
    312. 

  312.   function onRestored() {
    313. 

  313.     release();
    314. 

  314.     dirty = true;
    315. 

  315.     phase = 'context';
    316. 

  316.     initializationStart = 0;
    317. 

  317.     stats.status = 'initializing';
    318. 

  318.     schedule();
    319. 

  319.   }
    320. 

  320.   canvas.addEventListener('webglcontextlost', onLost);
    321. 

  321.   canvas.addEventListener('webglcontextrestored', onRestored);
    322. 

  322.   update(recipe);
    323. 

  323.   element.prepend(canvas);
    324. 

  324.   // Keep the readable CSS frame available while the small optional program initializes.
    325. 

  325.   function schedule(delay = 0) {
    326. 

  326.     if (pending || disposed || suspended || stats.status !== 'initializing')
    327. 

  327.       return;
    328. 

  328.     pendingIdle = delay === 0 && typeof win.requestIdleCallback === 'function';
    329. 

  329.     const run = () => {
    330. 

  330.       pending = 0;
    331. 

  331.       initialize();
    332. 

  332.     };
    333. 

  333.     pending = pendingIdle
    334. 

  334.       ? win.requestIdleCallback(run, { timeout: 500 })
    335. 

  335.       : win.setTimeout(run, delay);
    336. 

  336.   }
    337. 

  337.   schedule();
    338. 

  338.   return {
    339. 

  339.     canvas,
    340. 

  340.     stats,
    341. 

  341.     setSuspended(value: boolean) {
    342. 

  342.       suspended = value;
    343. 

  343.       if (!suspended) {
    344. 

  344.         schedule();
    345. 

  345.         if (stats.status === 'ready' && !tile) upload();
    346. 

  346.         if (latest) draw(latest);
    347. 

  347.       }
    348. 

  348.     },
    349. 

  349.     draw,
    350. 

  350.     resize,
    351. 

  351.     update,
    352. 

  352.     setOutput(next: DomSurfaceOutput) {
    353. 

  353.       if (!outputs.includes(next)) throw new Error('Unknown material output');
    354. 

  354.       output = next;
    355. 

  355.       dirty = true;
    356. 

  356.       if (latest) draw(latest);
    357. 

  357.     },
    358. 

  358.     dispose() {
    359. 

  359.       if (disposed) return;
    360. 

  360.       disposed = true;
    361. 

  361.       if (pending) {
    362. 

  362.         if (pendingIdle) win.cancelIdleCallback(pending);
    363. 

  363.         else win.clearTimeout(pending);
    364. 

  364.       }
    365. 

  365.       release();
    366. 

  366.       canvas.removeEventListener('webglcontextlost', onLost);
    367. 

  367.       canvas.removeEventListener('webglcontextrestored', onRestored);
    368. 

  368.       canvas.remove();
    369. 

  369.       stats.status = 'disposed';
    370. 

  370.       if (originalRenderer === null)
    371. 

  371.         element.removeAttribute('data-dom-renderer');
    372. 

  372.       else element.setAttribute('data-dom-renderer', originalRenderer);
    373. 

  373.     },
    374. 

  374.   };
    375. 

  375. }

  86.   function write(vars: Record<string, string>) {

  121.   function sample(dtSeconds: number) {
    122. 

  122.     if (!Number.isFinite(dtSeconds) || dtSeconds < 0 || dtSeconds > 1)
    123. 

  123.       throw new Error('dtSeconds must be in [0,1]; reset hidden-time gaps');
    124.

    133. 

  133.       targetX = externalTarget.x;
    134. 

  134.       targetY = externalTarget.y;
    135. 

  135.     }
    136. 

  136.     const fraction = -Math.expm1(-rate * dtSeconds);
    137. 

  137.     currentX += (targetX - currentX) * fraction;
    138. 

  138.     currentY += (targetY - currentY) * fraction;
    139. 

  139.     latest = render(currentX, currentY);
    140. 

  140.     stats.frames++;
    141. 

  141.     return latest;
    142. 

  142.   }
    143. 

  143.   function tick(time: number) {
    144. 

  144.     frame = 0;
    145. 

  145.     if (!enabled()) return;
    146. 

  146.     const dt =
    147. 

  147.       lastTime === null
    148. 

  148.         ? 0
    149. 

  149.         : Math.min(0.05, Math.max(0, (time - lastTime) / 1000));
    150. 

  150.     lastTime = time;
    151. 

  151.     sample(dt);
    152. 

  152.     if (
    153. 

  153.       dt === 0 ||
    154. 

  154.       // A slow response can move very little while still far from its target.
    155. 

  155.       Math.abs(targetX - currentX) + Math.abs(targetY - currentY) > 0.00001 ||
    156. 

  156.       sensor?.status === 'active'
    157. 

  157.     )
    158. 

  158.       request();
    159. 

  159.     else lastTime = null;
    160. 

  160.   }
    161. 

  161.   function onScroll() {
    162. 

  162.     scrollY =
    163. 

  163.       options.scroll === false
    164. 

  164.         ? 0
    165. 

  165.         : clamp(
    166. 

  166.             1 -
    167. 

  167.               2 *
    168. 

  168.                 ((bounds.top - win.scrollY + bounds.height / 2) /
    169. 

  169.                   Math.max(1, win.innerHeight)),
    170. 

  170.           );
    171. 

  171.     request();
    172. 

  172.   }
    173. 

  173.   function refreshBounds() {
    174. 

  174.     if (disposed) return;
    175. 

  175.     const r = element.getBoundingClientRect();
    176. 

  176.     stats.geometryReads++;
    177. 

  177.     bounds = {
    178. 

  178.       left: r.left + win.scrollX,
    179. 

  179.       top: r.top + win.scrollY,
    180. 

  180.       width: Math.max(1, r.width),
    181. 

  181.       height: Math.max(1, r.height),
    182. 

  182.     };
    183. 

  183.     gpu?.resize(bounds.width, bounds.height);
    184. 

  184.     onScroll();
    185. 

  185.   }
    186. 

  186.   function onPointer(event: PointerEvent) {
    187. 

  187.     pointerX = clamp(
    188. 

  188.       ((event.clientX + win.scrollX - bounds.left) / bounds.width) * 2 - 1,
    189. 

  189.     );
    190. 

  190.     pointerY = clamp(
    191. 

  191.       ((event.clientY + win.scrollY - bounds.top) / bounds.height) * 2 - 1,
    192. 

  192.     );
    193. 

  193.     request();
    194. 

  194.   }
    195. 

  195.   function onLeave() {
    196. 

  196.     pointerX = pointerY = 0;
    197. 

  197.     request();
    198. 

  198.   }
    199. 

  199.   function synchronize() {
    200. 

  200.     if (disposed) return;
    201. 

  201.     gpu?.setSuspended(doc.hidden || offscreen);
    202. 

  202.     if (!enabled()) {
    203. 

  203.       stop();
    204. 

  204.       currentX = currentY = targetX = targetY = pointerX = pointerY = 0;
    205. 

  205.       latest = render(0, 0);
    206. 

  206.     } else request();
    207. 

  207.     stats.active = enabled();
    208. 

  208.     stats.reduced = reduced.matches;
    209. 

  209.     stats.offscreen = offscreen;
    210. 

  210.   }
    211. 

  211.   if (options.pointer !== false) {
    212. 

  212.     element.addEventListener('pointermove', onPointer, { passive: true });
    213. 

  213.     element.addEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave);
    214. 

  214.   }
    215. 

  215.   win.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
    216. 

  216.   win.addEventListener('resize', refreshBounds, { passive: true });
    217. 

  217.   doc.addEventListener('visibilitychange', synchronize);
    218. 

  218.   reduced.addEventListener('change', synchronize);
    219. 

  219.   const resize =
    220. 

  220.     typeof ResizeObserver === 'undefined'
    221. 

  221.       ? null
    222. 

  222.       : new ResizeObserver(refreshBounds);
    223. 

  223.   resize?.observe(element);
    224. 

  224.   const intersection =
    225. 

  225.     typeof IntersectionObserver === 'undefined'
    226. 

  226.       ? null
    227. 

  227.       : new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
    228. 

  228.           offscreen = !entries[0]!.isIntersecting;
    229. 

  229.           synchronize();
    230. 

  230.         });
    231. 

  231.   intersection?.observe(element);
    232. 

  232.   refreshBounds();
    233. 

  233.   synchronize();
    234. 

  234.   return {
    235. 

  235.     sample,
    236. 

  236.     setLightTarget(target: { x: number; y: number } | null) {
    237. 

  237.       if (
    238. 

  238.         target &&
    239. 

  239.         (!Number.isFinite(target.x) ||
    240. 

  240.           !Number.isFinite(target.y) ||
    241. 

  241.           Math.abs(target.x) > 1 ||
    242. 

  242.           Math.abs(target.y) > 1)
    243. 

  243.       )
    244. 

  244.         throw new Error('Light target x/y must be in [-1,1]');
    245. 

  245.       if (disposed) return;
    246. 

  246.       externalTarget = target;
    247. 

  247.       request();
    248. 

  248.     },
    249. 

  249.     refreshBounds,
    250. 

  250.     updateRecipe(next: DomSurfaceRecipe) {
    251. 

  251.       if (disposed) return;
    252. 

  252.       recipe = next;
    253. 

  253.       gpu?.update(next);
    254. 

  254.       element.setAttribute('data-dom-surface', recipe.input.material);
    255. 

  255.       write(recipe.cssVariables);
    256. 

  256.       latest = render(currentX, currentY);
    257. 

  257.     },
    258. 

  258.     get status() {
    259. 

  259.       return disposed ? 'disposed' : !enabled() ? 'paused' : 'active';
    260. 

  260.     },
    261. 

  261.     stats,
    262. 

  262.     get renderer() {
    263. 

  263.       return gpu?.stats ?? { status: 'css', reason: 'CSS renderer requested' };
    264. 

  264.     },
    265. 

  265.     setOutput(output: DomSurfaceOutput) {
    266. 

  266.       gpu?.setOutput(output);
    267. 

  267.     },
    268. 

  268.     async requestTiltFromUserGesture() {
    269. 

  269.       if (disposed || !sensor) return 'unsupported' as const;
    270. 

  270.       const status = await sensor.requestFromUserGesture();
    271. 

  271.       request();
    272. 

  272.       return status;
    273. 

  273.     },
    274. 

  274.     dispose() {
    275. 

  275.       if (disposed) return;
    276. 

  276.       disposed = true;
    277. 

  277.       stop();
    278. 

  278.       sensor?.dispose();
    279. 

  279.       gpu?.dispose();
    280. 

  280.       resize?.disconnect();
    281. 

  281.       intersection?.disconnect();
    282. 

  282.       element.removeEventListener('pointermove', onPointer);
    283. 

  283.       element.removeEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave);
    284. 

  284.       win.removeEventListener('scroll', onScroll);
    285. 

  285.       win.removeEventListener('resize', refreshBounds);
    286. 

  286.       doc.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', synchronize);
    287. 

  287.       reduced.removeEventListener('change', synchronize);
    288. 

  288.       for (const [key, original] of originals)
    289. 

  289.         original.value
    290. 

  290.           ? element.style.setProperty(key, original.value, original.priority)
    291. 

  291.           : element.style.removeProperty(key);
    292. 

  292.       if (!hadClass) element.classList.remove('dt-dom-surface');
    293. 

  293.       if (oldMaterial === null) element.removeAttribute('data-dom-surface');
    294. 

  294.       else element.setAttribute('data-dom-surface', oldMaterial);
    295. 

  295.       stats.active = false;
    296. 

  296.     },
    297. 

  297.   };
    298. 

  298. }
    299.
